`README.md`:

```md
# H7ModEngine

A Lua 5.4 modding engine for **Might & Magic Heroes VII** (v2.2.1, 64-bit).

Mods are plain Lua files. They can read and change any game object, call any UnrealScript function, and hook game
functions to watch, change or block what they do. Mods reload automatically while the game runs, and players turn
them on and off from a **Mods** entry in the main menu.

The engine loads as a `dinput8.dll` next to the game executable. It does not patch game files or game code: it finds
the engine's internals in memory at startup and works through them.

```lua
-- mods\DailyGold\main.lua: the human player gets 500 gold every new day
h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7Calendar.NextDay", function(calendar)
    local controller = h7.first("H7AdventureController")
    local player = controller and controller:GetLocalPlayer(true)
    if not player then return end
    player:GetResourceSet():ModifyCurrency(500, true)
    h7.log("+500 gold for " .. player:GetName())
end)
```

## Features

- **Full access to the game's objects**: properties (including structs and arrays) read and written in place,
  UnrealScript functions called with normal Lua arguments, every class, enum and function listed by name.
- **Hooks** on any UnrealScript function, including calls made from script to script: run code before or after a
  function, read and change its arguments, block it or replace its return value.
- **Events and timers**: every frame, map changes, mods loaded or toggled; timers on game time.
- **Mod management**: per-mod manifests, load order, isolated globals, saved data, hot reload, and a Mods menu
  in the game's main menu (built on the game's own map selection screen) to enable and disable mods live.
- **Development tools**: an `exec` folder that runs Lua in the running game, a log of everything mods do, and an
  index of every engine object written at startup.
- **Safe failures**: Lua errors are logged with a stack trace and never take the game down.

## Requirements

- Might & Magic Heroes VII **v2.2.1**, 64-bit, Windows (the Ubisoft Connect version). Other versions may not work.
- Single player. Multiplayer is untested and will likely desync when a mod changes gameplay.

## Installation

1. Download the `H7ModEngine` artifact from the latest successful
   [Build run](https://github.com/Whaileee/H7ModEngine/actions/workflows/build.yml) on `main` and unzip it.
2. Copy its contents into the game folder (the folder that contains `Binaries`), so that you get:
   - `<Game>\Binaries\Win64\dinput8.dll`
   - `<Game>\H7ModEngine\` (configuration, example mods and `API.md`)
3. Start the game through Ubisoft Connect as usual.

To check that it works, open `<Game>\H7ModEngine\logs\engine.log`: it should end with lines like
`Loaded mod HelloWorld` and `Map: councilhub`. The main menu gets a **Mods** entry.

To uninstall, delete `<Game>\Binaries\Win64\dinput8.dll` (and the `H7ModEngine` folder if you don't want to keep
your mods and their saved data).

## Using mods

Each mod is a folder in `<Game>\H7ModEngine\mods\`. To install one, copy its folder there; the engine picks it up
on the next start (or at once after `h7.reload()`).

Open **Mods** in the main menu to see every installed mod with its version, category, author and description.
Select a mod and press **Enable** or **Disable**: the change applies immediately and is remembered in
`H7ModEngine.ini`.

The engine ships with these example mods:

| Mod | What it does | Shipped |
|---|---|---|
| HelloWorld | Logs a greeting, counts launches and session time | on |
| DailyGold | The human player gets bonus gold every new day | off |
| CombatXP | Heroes earn more experience from combat | off |
| DamageScale | Scales combat damage | off |
| HeroMovement | The human player's heroes get more movement points per day | off |
| ModManager | The Mods entry in the main menu (hidden from its own list) | on |

## Writing mods

Start with **[docs/API.md](docs/API.md)**, the modder's reference: your first mod in five lines, the manifest,
how to find the game function or property you want to change, hooks, events, saved data and the full `h7` API.
The same guide and a complete **[API reference](https://whaileee.github.io/H7ModEngine/reference/)** are published
for each release at <https://whaileee.github.io/H7ModEngine/>.

A mod is at least a `main.lua`:

```
<Game>\H7ModEngine\mods\MyMod\
    mod.lua    -- optional manifest: name, version, author, description, category, load_after, enabled
    main.lua   -- runs when the mod loads
```

Save `main.lua` while the game runs and the mod reloads within a second.

## Building from source

You need Windows, Visual Studio 2022 (or the Build Tools) with the C++ x64 tools, and Git.

```bash
git clone --recursive https://github.com/Whaileee/H7ModEngine.git
```

```bash
build.bat
```

`build.bat` writes `bin\dinput8.dll`. `build.bat deploy` also copies it, the ini and ModManager into the game
folder; `build.bat deploy examples` adds the example mods. Set `H7_GAME_DIR` to point it at your installation. See **[docs/DEVELOPMENT.md](docs/DEVELOPMENT.md)**
for the architecture, the development loop, in-game testing and the reverse-engineering notes.

## How it works

1. Windows loads `dinput8.dll` from the game folder. The engine's copy forwards every DirectInput call to the
   system DLL and starts an init thread.
2. The game executable is packed, so the init thread waits until it has unpacked and built its name and object
   tables, then finds them in memory and works out the engine's data layout by inspecting known objects.
3. It hooks the game viewport's per-frame `PostRender` call and starts Lua there, on the game thread.
4. Mods call into the engine through reflection: properties by offset, functions through `ProcessEvent`, and
   hooks by rewriting a function's bytecode into a small trampoline that calls back into the engine.

The details are in [docs/DEVELOPMENT.md](docs/DEVELOPMENT.md#architecture).

## Repository layout

| Path | Contents |
|---|---|
| `src/` | Engine source (C++20): DLL proxy, UE3 discovery and reflection, hooks, Lua runtime and bindings |
| `dist/` | What gets installed into `<Game>\H7ModEngine\`: default configuration and the example mods |
| `docs/` | [API.md](docs/API.md) (modders), [DEVELOPMENT.md](docs/DEVELOPMENT.md) (engine developers) |
| `tests/` | In-game test and tracing scripts, and the tools that run them |
| `re/` | Reverse-engineering helpers (PE and Flash movie inspection) |
| `third_party/lua` | Lua 5.4.7 (git submodule) |
| `RESUME.md` | Running engineering notes: findings, verified layouts, history of in-game runs |

## Status

Version 0.1.0. The engine, hooks and all example mods are verified in game on v2.2.1. The API may still change.

## Disclaimer

H7ModEngine is an unofficial fan project. It is not affiliated with or endorsed by Ubisoft. Might & Magic and
Heroes are trademarks of Ubisoft Entertainment. Use mods at your own risk and keep backups of your saves.

```

`RESUME.md`:

```md
# H7ModEngine — resume notes (saved 2026-11-04 ~13:30)

Engineering notebook: findings, verified layouts and the history of in-game runs. For an overview start with
[README.md](README.md), [docs/DEVELOPMENT.md](docs/DEVELOPMENT.md) (engine developers) and [docs/API.md](docs/API.md)
(modders). The Mods menu (PR #2) is merged into `main`; see [Mods menu](#mods-menu-feature-branch-in-progress).

Goal: a Lua 5.4 modding engine for Might & Magic Heroes VII (UE3, build v2.2.1) that exposes an easy API
to the community. Loaded as a `dinput8.dll` proxy; no game code is patched.

Game workspace reference: `N:\Ubisoft\games\Might and Magic Heroes VII\H7_REFERENCE.md`.

**Decompiled UnrealScript for the whole game** (H7 SDK): `N:\UDK\Custom\Development\Src\<Package>\Classes\*.uc`
(MMH7Game: 1432 classes). Grep it to choose hook targets and find callers before tracing at runtime.

## Where things are

| Path | What |
|---|---|
| `N:\Projects\H7ModEngine\src\` | Engine source (C++20, MSVC) |
| `build.bat` / `build.bat deploy` | Build `bin\dinput8.dll`; `deploy` copies it to `<Game>\Binaries\Win64\` plus `dist\` content to `<Game>\H7ModEngine\` |
| `dist\H7ModEngine.ini`, `dist\mods\` | Default config; example mods HelloWorld (enabled) + DailyGold, CombatXP, DamageScale, HeroMovement (shipped `enabled = false`; `deploy` overwrites the game copies, so re-enable them there for testing) |
| `docs\API.md` | Modder reference (written 2026-11-03 from the source + verified behaviour) |
| `tests\*.lua` | In-game scripts: copy into `<Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\` while the game runs. `selftest` (API), `hooktest` (block/override/args/script→script), `sigdump` (signatures), `daytrace` (call-count tracer for ~117 combat/calendar functions), `modwindow_autotest` (Mods window, run with `tests/run_ingame.sh`, which also takes screenshots) |
| `third_party\lua` | Lua 5.4.7: git submodule of github.com/lua/lua pinned at tag v5.4.7 (`git submodule update --init` after cloning); compiled as C++ so errors unwind as exceptions |
| `re\dump\` | Unpacked game image dump + early names/objects dumps (git-ignored, like `bin\`, `build\` and `re\ghidra_proj\`) |
| `re\ghidra_proj\` | Ghidra project (headless analysis of the dump was started in background; may be complete) |
| `<Game>\H7ModEngine\` | Runtime root: `logs\engine.log`, `mods\`, `exec\`, `data\`, `lib\`, `dumps\` |

## Source layout

- `proxy/` — dinput8 export forwarding (`dinput8.def`, `proxy.cpp`)
- `core/` — logging, SEH-safe memory reads, region scan, `.pdata` function-start lookup
- `ue3/core.*` — runtime discovery of GNames/GObjects, names, objects, FindObject/FindClass
- `ue3/reflection.*` — layout calibration + UStruct/UProperty/UFunction/UEnum helpers
- `ue3/hooks.*` — function hooks: bytecode trampoline for script functions, `UFunction::Func` swap for natives;
  ProcessEvent discovery/calls
- `ue3/dumper.*` — names/objects/probe/image dumps (dev)
- `lua/bindings.cpp` — `h7.*` API: object/struct/array proxies, marshaling, calls, hooks
- `lua/runtime.cpp` — Lua state, mod loading/order/hot-reload, events, timers, persistent data, exec folder
- `engine/dllmain.cpp` — init thread: wait for unpack → discover → calibrate → find viewport → tick hook

## Key facts established

- Exe is **packed** (stub entry in `.text1`, `.xtext` decrypted in place at runtime). Static analysis of the
  on-disk exe is useless; use the runtime dump (`Dev/DumpImage=1`).
- Ubisoft Connect: starting the exe directly makes it exit and relaunch via Connect; `uplay://launch/1176/0`
  did **not** start it unattended — the user has to click Play. Two processes briefly overlap (log opened shared).
- Discovery works: GNames (FNameEntry: index field `(i<<1)|unicode`, ANSI name inline), GObjects
  (`ObjectInternalInteger` at 0x38 == index).
- Engine allocator is tbbmalloc (`scalable_realloc`) → used for FString/TArray writes from Lua.
- Native classes are registered first (~4.5k objects); script packages load later — calibrate only after
  `Function Engine.Actor.Touch` exists, function pointers only after GameViewportClient exists.

### Confirmed layout (x64)

| Type | Field | Offset |
|---|---|---|
| UObject | VfTable / ObjectFlags / InternalIndex / Outer / Name / Class / Archetype | 0x00 / 0x10 / 0x38 / 0x40 / 0x48 / 0x50 / 0x58 (size 0x60) |
| UField | Next | 0x60 |
| UStruct | SuperField / Children / PropertySize | 0x78 / 0x80 / 0x88 (size 0xD0) |
| UFunction | FunctionFlags / iNative / FriendlyName / NumParms / ParmsSize / ReturnValueOffset / Func | 0xD0 / 0xD4 / 0xD8 / 0xE1 / 0xE2 / 0xE4 / 0xF0 (size 0xF8) |
| UProperty | ArrayDim / ElementSize / PropertyFlags / Offset | 0x68 / 0x6C / 0x70 / 0x8C (size 0xA8; subclass data at 0xA8) |
| UBoolProperty | BitMask | 0xA8 |
| UEnum | Names (TArray<FName>) | 0x68 |
| UClass | size | 0x270 (ClassDefaultObject: calibrated at runtime) |

## Status

Done:
1. Probe build ran in game: discovery + dumps verified (names/objects/probe/unpacked image).
2. Full engine (Lua runtime, API, mod manager, hooks) written and **builds**.
3. Second in-game run exposed two bugs, both fixed: log handle conflict between the relaunching processes
   (`_SH_DENYNO` + per-PID fallback); `UFunction::Func` calibrated too early (fixed offset 0xF0 +
   `CalibrateFunctionPointers()` after the viewport exists; `hooks::Maintain()` re-applies re-bound hooks).
4. Run 3 (2026-11-03 16:25): init fine but Lua never started — `GameViewportClient.Tick` is an empty **probe
   event**, so ProcessEvent filters it (IsProbing) and never calls Func. Heartbeat moved to
   `GameViewportClient.PostRender(Canvas)` (dt from QPC). Hooking a bodiless event now logs a warning.
5. Run 4 (16:51): **engine works end to end** — Lua ready, HelloWorld loaded, `ProcessEvent = vtable[70]
   (game+266b5a0)`, selftest 20/20 OK (reads/writes, structs, arrays, is_a, script call, hook 182× in 2s).
   Three bugs found and fixed in the current build (built + deployed, **not yet re-tested in game**):
   - unhooking from inside a dispatch (timer → `h7.unhook`) still ran the copied listener with a freed Lua
     ref ("attempt to call a number value") → listeners get a `removed` flag;
   - map name came from the first WorldInfo found (a stale `NeutralCreatures` package) → now
     `viewport.GamePlayers[0].Actor.WorldInfo` outermost package;
   - `UClass::ClassDefaultObject` not found → engine structs are **packed to 4 bytes** (UStruct::Script TArray
     sits at 0x8C), so the CDO search now steps by 4. Selftest now asserts `get_default()` is non-nil.

6. Run 5 (17:15): CDO fix confirmed (`Class{CDO=1d4}`, `get_default()` → `Default__Actor`); unhook-in-dispatch
   fix confirmed (no error). `tests\hooktest.lua`: **pre-hook blocking works, post-hook return override works**,
   unhook restores. Two more bugs, fixed for run 6:
   - map name still empty: `GamePlayers` is on `Engine` (the viewport client's outer `GameEngine_0`), not on
     GameViewportClient → now `ObjOuter(viewport).GamePlayers[0].Actor.WorldInfo`;
   - hook `args` nil (FFrame layout never found): FFrame scan stepped by 8 but with pack(4) → scan steps by 4.
7. Run 6 (17:20): **everything verified, no errors.** `Map: h7logoentry` → `Map: councilhub`;
   `FFrame layout: Node=+14 Object=+1c Code=+24 Locals=+2c`; hooktest all OK incl. `PostRender.Canvas` =
   `Canvas Transient.CanvasObject`; selftest 20/20 OK + hook fired 176× in 2s.

Notes: Main menu map is `CouncilHub` (`H7CouncilPlayerController`); boot map `H7LogoEntry`.
In run 5's first instance the game stopped ticking ~1 min in while the window was in the background
(main thread in a game-side `SleepEx` loop, not in our code — checked with `cdb -pv`); it resumed after the user
focused it. Treat as background throttling; ticks (and the exec folder) pause while the game isn't focused.

8. Run 7 (18:47): **script→script calls were never hooked** — the VM calls ProcessInternal directly, so a Func
   swap only sees ProcessEvent (C++→script) calls. Replaced by a **bytecode trampoline** (see "Script hooks"
   below): `DrawTransition` (called from PostRender's script) 88 calls/s with args. Example mods: HeroMovement
   (22 → 33 points) and CombatXP (634 → 951) worked; DailyGold and DamageScale silent.
9. Run 8 (19:21): tracer crashed the game — `Unknown code token 04` in `H7GameProcessor.ResolveCombat` (has
   `optional bool isMainResult = false`). Also showed `h7.first("H7AdventureController")` = the archetype
   `H7Config.H7AdventureSetup`.
10. Run 9 (19:32): fixes below verified; **all four example mods work**: `+500 gold for Sephinroth` on NextDay,
    `Damage 22 -> 28` etc. on real hits (forecasts scaled silently), `Combat XP 500 -> 750`, movement ×1.5;
    clean exit. Fixes: default-parm blocks kept in the trampoline; out params read via `FFrame::OutParms`;
    `h7.first`/`find_all` skip CDOs + archetypes (`ObjectFlags & 0x600`). Run 9 still fell back to Func for
    optional params *without* `= default` (e.g. `H7CombatResult.GetMultiplier`).
11. Run 10 (19:37): treating those as "no entry" crashed at the first attack (`Unknown code token 04` in
    `H7CombatResult.IsRedirect(optional int Idx)`) — each such param has a 1-byte `EX_Nothing` placeholder.
12. Run 11 (19:40): parser accepts a `49` block or `0B` per optional param (else falls back to Func, never
    guesses). Tracer hooked all 117 functions through the trampoline (0 fallbacks); a full manual battle ran with
    every hit scaled and CombatXP at the end. **Hook system fully verified.**

### Script hooks (bytecode trampoline) — verified
- Script = `[original optional-param entries] 04 1C <fake native UFunction*> 16 04 0B 53`. ProcessInternal
  (game+266de40) steps statements until `EX_Return` (0x04), then evaluates ONE expression into the result — so the
  leading `04` makes our `EX_FinalFunction` call the return expression. The fake native is a clone of
  `Core.Object.Sin` (Final|Native, iNative 0, Func = `ScriptHookNative`).
- `ScriptHookNative` runs pre listeners, then ProcessInternal on a 0x80-byte copy of the frame with Node = a shadow
  copy of the UFunction (keeps the original Script; jumps index `Node->Script`) and Code = original Script + body
  offset, then post listeners. Natives, latent and iterator functions still use the Func swap.
- Optional params: one entry per optional param at the start of Script — `EX_DefaultParmValue` =
  `49 <u16 len> <expr> 15` (handler game+264a770, tests `GRuntimeUCFlags & 2` = param skipped), or a lone
  `EX_Nothing` (0B) when the param has no default. The engine steps one entry per optional param while binding
  the call, before ProcessInternal, so the entries must stay at the front of the trampoline.
- Out params: on script→script calls they are NOT copied into the callee's locals; `FFrame::OutParms` (+0x3C,
  list of `{UProperty*, PropAddr, Next}` at +0/+8/+0x10) points at the caller's variable (see
  `EX_LocalOutVariable`, GNatives[0x48]). Hook `args` resolve out params through it (`hooks::OutParmAddr`).
- GNatives at game+2352220. Opcodes checked: 0x04 Return, 0x15 EndParmValue, 0x16 EndFunctionParms,
  0x1C FinalFunction, 0x41 (optional, after the return expression), 0x48 LocalOutVariable, 0x49 DefaultParmValue,
  0x53 EndOfScript. Disassemble with `cdb -z <Game>\H7ModEngine\dumps\MMH7Game_unpacked.exe -c "u <addr>; q"`.

### Gameplay hook targets (from the SDK sources, verified in game)
- New day: `H7AdventureController.SetNextPlayer` → `mCalendar.NextDay()` (calendar is `Transient.H7Calendar_0`).
  Gold: `player:GetResourceSet():ModifyCurrency(n, true)`; local player: `controller:GetLocalPlayer(true)` (native).
- Damage: `H7GameProcessor.ResolveCombat` → `ResolveCombatSingle` → `DamageEffectAttack_X2C` →
  `CalculateDamage(out H7CombatResult Result, int resultIdx, bool forecast)` (ends with `Result.SetDamage`;
  heals pass through it too) → `ApplyDamage`. `_X2C` functions are ordinary script (no natives by that name).
- XP: `H7CombatArmy.GetKilledCreaturesXP()`; movement: `H7EditorHero.GetMovementPoints()` (caravans are
  H7EditorHero too). `H7EditorHero.AddXp` is native.

Next steps:
1. More example mods; update H7_REFERENCE.md. (`dist\H7ModEngine.ini` now ships DumpProbe/DumpImage=0, DumpSDK=1;
   deploy never overwrites an existing game ini; the dev copy has DumpSDK=1, DumpProbe=1, DumpImage=0.)

Open risks: natives with iNative called through ProcessEvent (SDK trick: clear iNative); hooks on native
functions get no params and can't block; protector integrity checks (none hit so far — we only touch heap data).

## Mods menu (feature branch, in progress)

User request: when the engine is on, a main menu entry **Mods** lists every mod with its description and lets
players enable/disable it. The user wants it to look like the game's **map selection screen** (table on the left,
details on the right, Back/Continue buttons), not a small popup.

Committed on `feature/mods-menu` (dff0a32, local, not pushed); the 2026-11-04 batch below is not committed yet.
- Engine (`runtime.cpp`): mod state — manifest `enabled`/`hidden`/`category`; ini `[Mods] Enabled=`/`Disabled=`
  override the manifest; `h7.set_mod_enabled(name, on)` writes the ini, the mod loads/unloads on the next frame and
  `mod_toggled(name, enabled)` fires; `h7.mods()` returns name/version/author/description/category/enabled/loaded/
  hidden. Disabled mods are still discovered (listed) but not loaded.
- Engine (`bindings.cpp`): delegate params accept `{object=, func=}` (FScriptDelegate {UObject*, FName}).
- Example mods have a `category` (Combat/Economy/Adventure/Example). All of it is in `docs/API.md`.
- `dist/mods/ModManager/` (`hidden = true`): uses the map selection window. The previous popup version
  (custom-difficulty popup, paged) worked; it was never committed.
- `tests/swfdump.lua` (dumps SwfMovie.RawData to `dumps\swf\`), `tests/menulab.lua` (main menu button),
  `tests/modlistlab.lua` (fills the map selection window with mods via exec — verified with a screenshot).
- `re/swfinfo.py <file.swf> [--strings]`: lists a SWF/GFX movie's tags, symbols and AS3 classes with members
  (instance and static).

Batch 2026-11-04 (all **verified in game**, run 17 + hot reloads; game kept running):
- Engine: hooks run inside `ModScope(mod)` (`ModScope` in runtime.h, `SetCurrentModId`): logs, timers, handlers and
  hooks created in hook callbacks belong to the mod. Verified: `[ModManager] Mods button installed`.
- Engine: `WriteElem` handles `ArrayProperty` (Lua sequence or engine array replaces the contents; old buffer and
  strings freed with `EngineRealloc(p, 0)`); `CallUFunction` frees strings/arrays left in its parameter buffer
  (`FreeOwned`). Selftest 22/22 incl. "write array" and "array argument" (`Invoke("push", {ASValue...})`).
- ModManager (final design, verified by `tests/modwindow_autotest.lua`):
  - columns Mod | (blank, "-") | Version | Category | Status; details panel = "Creator" (author) + Description;
    picture frame shows the game's "no picture" label; Generate map and the 2nd column's filter hidden.
  - Continue = Enable/Disable; on `mod_toggled` the window is filled again (restore() + open()) so Status is
    right — the selection goes back to the first row (rows can't be changed or selected from Lua).
  - `change()` remembers each part's original value once per game session (`ui.originals`), `restore()` sets
    each one in its own pcall — a failed restore once lost the originals and left "Mods" texts in the game's
    map selection (repaired by hand with an exec script).
- Test tooling: `tests/run_ingame.sh <test.lua> <shotdir> [timeout]` copies a test into exec, prints its log
  lines and saves a screenshot (`tests/shot.ps1`, PrintWindow of the game window, any display) for every
  "SHOT <name>" line, ending at "DONE". **The game only draws while it is visible**: covered, PrintWindow returns
  the last frame — trust the logged state, not the picture.

How ModManager drives the window:
- Main menu button: `AttachMovie("H7TextButton", "mBtnMods", -1, GFxClikWidget)` on `H7MainMenuCntl.mMainMenu`,
  `AddEventListener("CLIK_click", {object=cntl, func="Options"})`; its clicks arrive at
  `H7MainMenuCntl.Options` with **empty EventData**, so a pre-hook checks `mouseX/mouseY` against the button and
  blocks. Layout: Extras/Options move up one step (80px), Mods takes Options' slot (y=860), Quit stays (940).
- Window: `sel = h7.first("H7MapSelectCntl")`, `list = sel.mMapSelection` (H7GfxMapList). Open =
  `mMainMenu:SetVisibleSave(false); list:Reset(); list:Update(sel.mMapThumbnail)`. Rows: `sel:CreateArray()`, per
  row `sel:CreateObject("Object", GFxObject)` with Index, Name, Creator, Description, PlayerCount, Size, Type
  (optionally VictoryConditions/LoseConditions arrays); then `list:SetObject("mPolledMaps", arr)`,
  `list:Invoke("AddMaps")`, `list:Invoke("ListingMapDone")`.
- Parts on `list`: mLblTitle, header labels mLblMapName/mLblVictoryCondition/mLblPlayers/mLblMapSize/mLblMapType,
  mMapThumbnail (1345,110 493x493), mLblNoThumbnail ("Brak obrazka"), mMapDetails (1322,634 550x311),
  mBtnContinue, mBtnBack, mGenerateMap; `mMapList` has the filters mDDCondition/mDDPlayerAmount/mDDMapSize/
  mDDMapType (filled from the row data), mBtnClearFilters and the sort buttons.
- Clicks come back as UnrealScript calls on `H7MapSelectCntl`: `MapClicked(clickedMapIndex)` (also once after
  AddMaps for the first row), `BtnContinueClicked(selectedListIndex, isCampaign)`, `BtnGenerateMapClicked()`,
  `BtnBackClicked()` (→ ClosePopup: hides the window, shows the main menu). ModManager pre-hooks them while
  `ui.showing`; Back is not blocked, it restores the window first.

Other gotchas:
- **GFxObjects get garbage collected**: the UObject wrappers from GetObject/AttachMovie are freed when no
  UnrealScript object references them (the Flash clip stays). Never cache them across frames; keep the instance
  name and `GetObject` again. Objects held by script vars (`cntl.mMainMenu`, `sel.mMapSelection`) are safe.
- Hook `args` are by name (`args.clickedMapIndex`), not index.
- `mBG`, `mBtnOK`, `mBlockLayer` of H7CustomDifficultyPopUp are not reachable via GetObject (nil).
- An exec script that errors or is empty still gets renamed to `.done` and overwrites an older `.done` of the
  same name — keep lab scripts in `tests\` and copy them in.
- Ubisoft Connect restarts the game process once at launch: a watcher must not treat the first exit as the end.

What does not work from Lua (Scaleform/UE3 limits found this session):
- `GetObject` returns nil for members holding AS-created Arrays/Objects/Dictionaries (`mMapList.mMapItems`,
  `mVisibleMaps`, `mSelectedMapListItem`, `mData`, `mMapDetails.mChildsAdded`, `Loca.mLocaDictionary`); it works
  for display objects (timeline instances) and for objects Unreal created (`mPolledMaps`). `GetVariableObject
  ("global.Loca")` returns the class, but not its statics. Invoke on mMapList's `clearList`/`sortByNameDown`
  did nothing visible; `AddMaps` adds rows on top of existing ones (sorted by name) and selects the first row.
- **The movie caches translations** (`Loca.mLocaDictionary`, static): `FlashLocalize(locaKey)` is asked once per
  key and movie. Never override a shared key (VICTORY_CONDITIONS, LOSE_CONDITIONS, DESCRIPTION, CREATOR...):
  the game's own map list keeps the override until restart. Row texts also go through it: all-caps values are
  taken for keys and shown as `[VALUE]` when unknown.
- Details panel: shows a Creator line ("Edytor kampanii", key CREATOR) only if `Creator` is set, victory/lose
  lines only if those arrays are set; the list's 2nd column is `VictoryConditions[0]` ("-" if missing).
- Status colors: not possible (rows unreachable); H7Label `htmlText` resets the font size.

Next steps:
1. Commit this batch on `feature/mods-menu` (ask the user), then push / open a PR (ask; `gh` not installed).
2. Optional polish: keep the selection after Enable/Disable (would need a way to select a row by index);
   localized captions for "Mods", "Enable", "Disable" (H7Loca has the game's language).
3. More example mods / API work.

## Object factory + H2Enchantress faction mod (2026-11-04)

Engine part: branch `feature/object-factory` (stacked on `docs/readme-dev-guide`). The mod lives in its own
repository, `N:\Projects\H2Enchantress` (deploy: copy its `H2Enchantress` folder to `<Game>\H7ModEngine\mods\`).

User request: a "serious" mod porting the Heroes II Enchantress (Sorceress) town as a **new** faction (user chose
a real extra faction over reskinning Sylvan).

Engine (`src/ue3/factory.*`, bindings): `h7.clone(src, name [, outer [, extra_flags]])`, `h7.new_object`,
`h7.package(name)`, `h7.name(str)`, `h7.can_create()`, `h7.flags` (Public/Standalone/Transient/Archetype/RootSet).
Calls the game directly at fixed RVAs (checked against their first bytes, else disabled):
StaticConstructObject game+26e13b0 (found in execNew, GNatives[0x11]; execNew refuses Actor classes, so no script
`new`), FName(TCHAR*, FNAME_Add) game+26cb5c0, GetTransientPackage game+26bea20. With a template the
SubobjectRoot is INVALID_OBJECT (like execNew). RootSet is OR'ed into ObjectFlags after construction.
Clones of archetypes keep RF_ArchetypeObject and `H7GameUtility.IsArchetype` is true for them.
Also fixed: `h7.to_table` expanded objects inside arrays (C stack overflow); `Core.DynLoadObjectProperty` (H7's
lazily loaded object refs, e.g. H7Faction.mStartTown, H7Creature/H7EditorHero.mVisuals, town screen, icons) is
now read/written as an object property.

Key facts learned:
- DynLoad fields load on demand from the object's linker ("Cannot find DynloadProperty 'mVisuals' serialized in
  Object X and its ArchetypeParents" -> fatal error in combat). Copies have no linker: call
  `src:DynLoadObjectProperty(name)` / `DynLoadObjectArrayProperty("mTownAssets")` on the original before cloning,
  and block the game's Del* release functions for copies and their sources.
- Build the faction at the main menu (councilhub), not on h7logoentry: GetStartTown returns none there.
- This install is heavily modded: 14 factions before ours (Frost, Hades, Sanctuary, Xel, Citadel, Mercenary,
  Conflux), Sylvan town has extra mod dwellings; Sylvan's town names point at H7FactionSylvan_extra.
- Creatures have a second stat set for skirmish (`mSkirmish*`); cost is `mUnitCost`/`mSkirmishUnitCost`.
- Duel: armies offered per faction by their creatures' faction (H7GameData.GetDuelArmies), heroes from
  mDuelHeroes; without them the army is empty.
- Names are localised by object name; copies show the fallback text, so copy GetName()/GetDesc() texts.

Mod (data.lua lineup/names/heroes, stats.lua, faction.lua builder, hooks.lua):
clones Sylvan faction/town/fort/map dwellings/all buildings/hero classes/heroes/random hero/town names/duel
armies+heroes into package `H2Enchantress`, remaps references, blocks dwellings without an H2 counterpart,
registers in H7GameData. Verified in game: faction in GetFactions (all policies), duel with Enchantress army runs.
Lab script `tools/factionlab.lua` in the mod repo dumps Sylvan's structure to `dumps\factionlab.txt`.

Next: skirmish/adventure map (town screen, recruiting, AI), save/load, H2 abilities (dwarf magic resistance,
unicorn blind, phoenix fire immunity), stat balance (Phoenix initiative low, Dwarf scaling capped at x3),
H2 hero portraits/bios.

## Mod settings + Mods button fix (2026-11-04, branch `feature/mod-settings`, verified in game)

- Manifest `settings = { {key, label, description, type, default, min, max, step, options, reload}, ... }`;
  values in the ini `[Mod.<name>]`; `mod.settings` proxy (read/assign/pairs), `h7.mod_settings`,
  `h7.set_mod_setting`, event `setting_changed`; `reload = true` restarts the mod. `h7.mods()[i].settings` = count.
  Example mods use settings (DailyGold gold, CombatXP/DamageScale/HeroMovement multipliers, DamageScale log,
  HeroMovement who). `tests/settingstest.lua`: 13/13 OK.
- ModManager: the map window's Generate Map button = Settings (mods with settings) / Default (settings view);
  settings view rows: label, value, range, "Changed"; Continue = Change; Back = back to the mod list. Details panel
  lists current values. Selection resets to row 1 after each change (rows unreachable: `mMapList.masked` and its
  children are AS-created; `GetObject` returns nil).
- Mods button in sub-menus: the movie hides its own buttons (`visible = false`) in sub-menus; ModManager copies
  Options' visible/alpha/x to Mods every 0.1 s. Verified with real clicks.
- `GFxObject:Invoke("GotoSPMenu")` etc. on the main menu does NOT open sub-menus; drive the UI with real input:
  `tests/click.ps1 <x> <y>` (window pixels, as in shot.ps1 screenshots; brings the game to the front). Clicks
  right after a list refill can be lost; wait ~2 s. Main menu buttons (2048x1080 window): Jeden gracz 1730,330;
  Mods 1730,890; sub-menu Wstecz 1730,970; map window Back 240,1015, Continue 1810,1015, Generate 1025,978,
  rows from y=224 every 49 px.

### Settings panel (2026-11-04, follow-up on `feature/mod-settings`, verified in game)
User wanted sliders/check boxes, the description in the picture frame and the settings below it. ModManager:
- `panel.lua`: description = H7Label (multiline) in the frame (1366,128 452x456); hidden: mMapThumbnail,
  mLblNoThumbnail, mMapDetails, mGenerateMap. Settings area 1342,640 (470 wide), 5 rows of 48 px.
- Controls are clips from H7MapSelect's own library (no checkbox/slider *component* symbol exists there):
  slider = `H7Slider_BG_Gold` (a 14x423 vertical bar, rotated -90) + `H7Slider_Gold_Thumb`; check box =
  `H7GUICommandButton` (36 px, DisplaySelectState + alpha 0.35 when off); choices = `H7LeftArrow`/`H7RightArrow`
  + H7Label. `H7DropDown` also attaches (AddItem API untested).
- Input: Flash listeners on attached clips call back with EMPTY EventData (no type/target) and only for
  clicks, so the panel ignores Flash events and hooks `Engine.Console.InputKey` (Key "LeftMouseButton", Event 0
  pressed / 1 released / 2 repeat) and hit-tests `window:GetFloat("mouseX"/"mouseY")` (window coords = screen
  pixels - 64 in x here). The press is handled on the next tick: at InputKey time Flash's mouse position is
  still the old one. Slider drag follows the mouse in `tick`, saves on release.
- Synthetic input: the game reads mouse movement as raw input; `SetCursorPos` alone doesn't move the game's
  cursor. `tests/click.ps1` nudges with relative moves and drags with absolute VIRTUALDESK moves (the game
  window is on a monitor with negative coordinates).
- Number box (`control = "input"`, default for numbers without a range): H7TooltipBG frame + its own H7Label
  (created after the frame so it draws on top; clips stack in creation order). Keys come from
  Console.InputKey; key names are lower-case as the engine first registered them ("seven", "enter"), so the
  panel compares lower-cased. A post-hook returning true marks keys as handled while editing (Escape then
  doesn't close the window). `tests/keys.ps1 <keys>` types into the game (keybd_event works, unlike mouse).
  H7DropDown as a box left a grey slot where its arrow was; don't reuse clip names across clip types
  (ensure() returns the existing clip of that name).
- Hot-reloading ModManager while the window is open loses the panel state; re-clicking the selected row
  doesn't fire MapClicked (select another row first).

## Example mods removed from the game install (2026-11-04)
The game's `H7ModEngine\mods` now holds only ModManager and H2Enchantress; the five example mods (and their ini
settings, HelloWorld's saved data) were deleted there but stay in `dist\mods` for the docs and tests.
`build.bat deploy` installs only ModManager; `build.bat deploy examples` installs them all again (needed before
running `tests\settingstest.lua` / `modwindow_autotest.lua`).

## Lua API (implemented)

- Logging: `h7.log/warn/error`, `print` → `logs\engine.log` with mod prefix
- Lookup: `h7.find("Class Pkg.Name" | "Pkg.Obj")`, `h7.find_class`, `h7.find_all(class [, include_templates])`,
  `h7.first(class)` (nil if none; both skip class defaults and archetypes), `h7.enum(name)`, `h7.properties(x)`,
  `h7.functions(x)` (entries: name/type/owner/flags), `h7.signature(func)` → UnrealScript-style signature,
  `h7.to_table(v)`
- Objects: `obj.Prop` read/write, `obj:Func(args)` → return + out params, helpers `get_name/get_full_name/
  get_path_name/get_class/get_outer/get_default/get_super/get_address/is_valid/is_a/call`
- Structs/arrays: live proxies; `#arr`, `arr[i]`, `arr:add(v)`, `arr:remove(i)`
- Hooks: `h7.hook(path, fn(self, args) -> true blocks)`, `h7.hook_post(path, fn(self, args, ret) -> new ret)`,
  `h7.unhook(id)`. Script functions see every call (script and C++ callers); `args` exposes params incl. out
  params (read/write). Natives: no params, can't block.
- Events: `h7.on("tick"|"map_loaded"|"mods_loaded", fn)`, `h7.off(id)`; timers `h7.after/every/cancel`
- Mods: `mods\<Name>\mod.lua` (manifest: name, version, author, description, load_after, enabled) + `main.lua`;
  per-mod env with `mod.name/dir/require/save/load`, optional `on_unload()`; hot reload on file change; `h7.reload()`,
  `h7.mods()`; `[Mods] Disabled=` in ini; `h7.exec("console cmd")`

```

`build.bat`:

```bat
@echo off
rem Builds bin\dinput8.dll with MSVC (x64). Usage: build.bat [deploy]
setlocal
set ROOT=%~dp0
rem Game folder for "deploy"; set H7_GAME_DIR to use another one.
set GAME=N:\Ubisoft\games\Might and Magic Heroes VII
if defined H7_GAME_DIR set GAME=%H7_GAME_DIR%
rem Find any Visual Studio edition with the C++ x64 tools (Community, BuildTools, Enterprise on CI runners).
rem (%%VSWHERE%% expands inside the child command: the ")" of "Program Files (x86)" would end the for clause.)
set VSWHERE=%ProgramFiles(x86)%\Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe
set VSDIR=
for /f "usebackq delims=" %%i in (`call "%%VSWHERE%%" -latest -products * -requires Microsoft.VisualStudio.Component.VC.Tools.x86.x64 -property installationPath`) do set VSDIR=%%i
echo Using Visual Studio at %VSDIR%
if not defined VSDIR (
  echo Visual Studio with the C++ x64 build tools was not found.
  exit /b 1
)
call "%VSDIR%\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat" >nul || exit /b 1

if not exist "%ROOT%build\obj" mkdir "%ROOT%build\obj"
if not exist "%ROOT%bin" mkdir "%ROOT%bin"
cd /d "%ROOT%build\obj"
del /q *.obj 2>nul

rem Lua is compiled as C++ so Lua errors unwind as exceptions through C++ frames.
set LUA=%ROOT%third_party\lua
set LUASRC=lapi lauxlib lbaselib lcode lcorolib lctype ldblib ldebug ldo ldump lfunc lgc linit liolib llex lmathlib lmem loadlib lobject lopcodes loslib lparser lstate lstring lstrlib ltable ltablib ltm lundump lutf8lib lvm lzio
set CSRC=
for %%f in (%LUASRC%) do call set CSRC=%%CSRC%% "%LUA%\%%f.c"
cl /nologo /c /O2 /MT /W0 /TP /EHsc %CSRC% || exit /b 1

set CPPSRC=
for /r "%ROOT%src" %%f in (*.cpp) do call set CPPSRC=%%CPPSRC%% "%%f"
cl /nologo /c /O2 /MT /EHsc /std:c++20 /W3 /Zi /D_CRT_SECURE_NO_WARNINGS /DNOMINMAX /DWIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN /I"%LUA%" %CPPSRC% || exit /b 1

link /nologo /DLL /DEBUG /OPT:REF /OUT:"%ROOT%bin\dinput8.dll" /DEF:"%ROOT%src\proxy\dinput8.def" *.obj user32.lib shell32.lib || exit /b 1
echo Built %ROOT%bin\dinput8.dll

if /i "%1"=="deploy" (
  rem Replace whatever dinput8.dll is in the game folder (the old one was a dangling symlink).
  del /f /q "%GAME%\Binaries\Win64\dinput8.dll" 2>nul
  copy /y "%ROOT%bin\dinput8.dll" "%GAME%\Binaries\Win64\dinput8.dll" >nul || exit /b 1
  if not exist "%GAME%\H7ModEngine" mkdir "%GAME%\H7ModEngine"
  if not exist "%GAME%\H7ModEngine\H7ModEngine.ini" copy /y "%ROOT%dist\H7ModEngine.ini" "%GAME%\H7ModEngine\H7ModEngine.ini" >nul
  rem Only ModManager is installed; "build.bat deploy examples" also installs the example mods.
  if /i "%2"=="examples" (
    xcopy /e /i /y /q "%ROOT%dist\mods" "%GAME%\H7ModEngine\mods" >nul
  ) else (
    xcopy /e /i /y /q "%ROOT%dist\mods\ModManager" "%GAME%\H7ModEngine\mods\ModManager" >nul
  )
  xcopy /e /i /y /q "%ROOT%dist\lib" "%GAME%\H7ModEngine\lib" >nul
  echo Deployed to %GAME%
)
endlocal

```

`dist/mods/CombatXP/main.lua`:

```lua
-- CombatXP: heroes earn more experience from combat (all heroes, AI included).
-- Shows: a post-hook that replaces a function's return value.

-- int H7CombatArmy.GetKilledCreaturesXP() computes the experience a fight is worth.
-- A post-hook receives (self, args, returnValue); returning a value replaces the result.
h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7CombatArmy.GetKilledCreaturesXP", function(army, args, xp)
    if xp <= 0 then return end
    local boosted = math.floor(xp * mod.settings.multiplier + 0.5)
    h7.log(("Combat XP %d -> %d"):format(xp, boosted))
    return boosted
end)

```

`dist/mods/DailyGold/main.lua`:

```lua
-- DailyGold: the local (human) player receives bonus gold at the start of every new day.
-- Shows: a post-hook on a game function, finding a game object, calling game functions, a mod setting.

-- The amount is a setting (see mod.lua): players change it in the Mods menu, mod.settings has the current value.

local function local_player()
    local adventure = h7.first("H7AdventureController")
    return adventure and adventure:GetLocalPlayer(true)
end

-- H7Calendar.NextDay() runs once each time the calendar advances to a new day.
h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7Calendar.NextDay", function(calendar)
    local player = local_player()
    if not player then return end
    -- H7ResourceSet.ModifyCurrency(int modAmount, bool updateGui)
    local gold = mod.settings.gold
    if gold <= 0 then return end
    player:GetResourceSet():ModifyCurrency(gold, true)
    h7.log(("+%d gold for %s"):format(gold, player:GetName()))
end)

```

    enabled = false, -- example mod: turn it on in the Mods menu
    -- Shown and editable in the Mods menu; read with mod.settings.gold
    settings = {

          description = "Gold the human player receives at the start of every day." },
    },
}

`dist/mods/DamageScale/main.lua`:

```lua
-- DamageScale: scales all combat damage dealt by creatures, heroes and spells.
-- Shows: reading a hooked function's arguments and calling methods on them.

-- H7GameProcessor.CalculateDamage(out H7CombatResult Result, int resultIdx, bool forecast) fills in the damage of
-- one attack target (heals go through it too). Forecasts (tooltips, AI planning) are scaled as well, so they
-- keep matching real hits.
h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7GameProcessor.CalculateDamage", function(processor, args)
    local result, idx = args.Result, args.resultIdx
    if not result or result:IsHeal(idx) then return end
    local damage = result:GetDamage(idx)
    result:SetDamage(math.floor(damage * mod.settings.multiplier + 0.5), idx)
    if not args.forecast and mod.settings.log then
        h7.log(("Damage %d -> %d"):format(damage, result:GetDamage(idx)))
    end
end)

```

          description = "All combat damage (creatures, heroes, spells) is multiplied by this." },

`dist/mods/HelloWorld/main.lua`:

```lua
-- HelloWorld: the smallest useful mod.
-- Edit this file while the game runs; H7ModEngine reloads it automatically.

local data = mod.load()            -- persistent table (H7ModEngine\data\HelloWorld.lua)
data.launches = (data.launches or 0) + 1
mod.save(data)

h7.log("Hello from " .. mod.name .. "! Loaded " .. data.launches .. " time(s).")

h7.on("map_loaded", function(map)
    h7.log("Entered map: " .. map)
end)

-- Report how long the session has been running, once a minute.
local minutes = 0
h7.every(60, function()
    minutes = minutes + 1
    h7.log(("Session time: %d min"):format(minutes))
end)

function on_unload()
    h7.log("Goodbye from " .. mod.name)
end

```

`dist/mods/HeroMovement/main.lua`:

```lua
-- HeroMovement: the human player's heroes get more movement points per day.
-- Shows: filtering a hook by owner, and caching per object to keep a hot hook cheap.

-- Remember which heroes belong to the local player (GetMovementPoints runs often, e.g. for path previews).
local is_local = {}
h7.on("map_loaded", function() is_local = {} end)

local function owned_by_local_player(hero)
    local key = hero:get_address()
    if is_local[key] == nil then
        local player = hero:GetPlayer()
        is_local[key] = player ~= nil and player:IsControlledByLocalPlayer()
    end
    return is_local[key]
end

-- int H7EditorHero.GetMovementPoints(): the hero's movement points per day.
h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7EditorHero.GetMovementPoints", function(hero, args, points)
    if mod.settings.who == "Everyone" or owned_by_local_player(hero) then
        return math.floor(points * mod.settings.multiplier + 0.5)
    end
end)

```

        { key = "multiplier", label = "Movement multiplier", default = 1.5, min = 1, max = 3, step = 0.1,
          description = "Movement points per day of the human player's heroes are multiplied by this." },
        { key = "who", label = "Applies to", options = { "Human player", "Everyone" }, default = "Human player",
          description = "Whose heroes move further." },

`dist/mods/ModManager/main.lua`:

```lua
-- ModManager: a "Mods" entry in the main menu that lists every mod and lets players enable or disable them and
-- change their settings. The list is the game's own map selection window (H7MapSelect movie), filled with mods
-- instead of maps while it is opened from here; everything changed in it is put back when it closes.
--
-- Left: one row per mod (name, version, category, status). Continue = Enable/Disable.
-- Right (panel.lua): the selected mod's description in the picture frame, its settings as sliders, check boxes
-- and arrows below it, numbers that are typed in a number box. Changes are saved at once.

local Obj = h7.find_class("GFxUI.GFxObject")
local Clik = h7.find_class("GFxUI.GFxClikWidget")
local SELECT = "MMH7Game.H7MapSelectCntl."
local panel = mod.require("panel")

-- Column headers of the map list. Rows fill the columns from their Name, VictoryConditions[0], PlayerCount, Size
-- and Type fields. VictoryConditions is left out (the second column stays empty): renaming its caption would be
-- cached by the movie for the game's own map list too.
local HEADERS = { mLblMapName = "Mod", mLblVictoryCondition = "", mLblPlayers = "Version", mLblMapSize = "Category",
    mLblMapType = "Status" }
-- Parts of the window that mods don't use: the picture, its "no picture" text, the map details (the panel takes
-- their place), Generate Map and the second column's filter.
local UNUSED = { "mMapThumbnail", "mLblNoThumbnail", "mMapDetails", "mGenerateMap", "mMapList.mDDCondition" }

-- State that has to survive reloads of this mod lives as long as the main menu.
_G.__modmanager = _G.__modmanager or {}
local ui = _G.__modmanager

-- GFxObjects returned by GetObject/AttachMovie are garbage collected by the engine when nothing in
-- UnrealScript holds them (the Flash clips stay), so they are looked up by name whenever they are used.
-- `path` may name nested parts: "mMapList.mDDCondition".
local function child(parent, path)
    for key in path:gmatch("[^.]+") do
        if not parent then return nil end
        parent = parent:GetObject(key, Obj)
    end
    return parent
end
local function select_cntl() return h7.first("H7MapSelectCntl") end
local function window() return select_cntl().mMapSelection end

-- "Failed": enabled but not loaded (the log says why).
local function status_of(m)
    if not m.enabled then return "Disabled" end
    return m.loaded and "Enabled" or "Failed"
end

local function mod_by_name(name)
    for _, m in ipairs(h7.mods()) do
        if m.name == name then return m end
    end
end

-- Changes a window part, remembering its original value the first time (once per game session), so restore()
-- puts the window back even after a reload of this mod or a failed restore.
local function change(path, field, value, kind)
    local o = child(window(), path)
    if not o then return end
    ui.originals = ui.originals or {}
    local key = path .. "|" .. field
    if ui.originals[key] == nil then
        ui.originals[key] = { path = path, field = field, kind = kind, value = o["Get" .. kind](o, field) }
    end
    o["Set" .. kind](o, field, value)
end

local function restore()
    ui.showing = false
    pcall(panel.hide_all)
    for _, s in pairs(ui.originals or {}) do
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local o = child(window(), s.path)
            if o then o["Set" .. s.kind](o, s.field, s.value) end
        end)
        if not ok then h7.warn(("could not restore %s.%s: %s"):format(s.path, s.field, tostring(err))) end
    end
end

-- Rows can't be changed once added, so enabling or disabling a mod fills the list again (the selection goes back
-- to the first row; AddMaps sorts the rows by name). Row texts go through the movie's localization: all-caps
-- words would be taken for keys.
local function fill()
    local sel = select_cntl()
    local list = sel.mMapSelection
    list:Reset()
    list:Update(sel.mMapThumbnail)
    ui.showing = true
    change("mLblTitle", "text", "Mods", "String")
    for key, text in pairs(HEADERS) do change(key, "text", text, "String") end
    for _, key in ipairs(UNUSED) do change(key, "visible", false, "Bool") end
    change("mBtnContinue", "text", child(list, "mBtnContinue"):GetString("text"), "String") -- set per row
    local rows = sel:CreateArray()
    ui.rows = {} -- row index (from 0, as the window reports it) -> mod name
    local n = 0
    for _, m in ipairs(h7.mods()) do
        if not m.hidden then
            local o = sel:CreateObject("Object", Obj)
            o:SetInt("Index", n)
            o:SetString("Name", m.name)
            o:SetString("PlayerCount", m.version ~= "" and m.version or "-")
            o:SetString("Size", m.category ~= "" and m.category or "-")
            o:SetString("Type", status_of(m))
            rows:SetElementObject(n, o)
            ui.rows[n] = m.name
            n = n + 1
        end
    end
    list:SetObject("mPolledMaps", rows)
    list:Invoke("AddMaps") -- selects the first row (MapClicked)
    list:Invoke("ListingMapDone")
    h7.log(("Mods window: %d mods"):format(n))
end

local function open()
    ui.cntl.mMainMenu:SetVisibleSave(false)
    fill()
end

-- A row was selected: its description and settings on the right, Enable/Disable on Continue.
local function on_row(index)
    ui.selected = index
    local m = ui.rows and mod_by_name(ui.rows[index])
    local b = child(window(), "mBtnContinue")
    if b then b:SetString("text", (m and m.enabled) and "Disable" or "Enable") end
    panel.show(m)
end

-- The window reports clicks to its controller; while it shows mods they are handled here instead.
h7.hook(SELECT .. "MapClicked", function(self, args)
    if not ui.showing then return end
    on_row(args.clickedMapIndex)
    return true -- no map thumbnail to load
end)

h7.hook(SELECT .. "BtnContinueClicked", function(self, args)
    if not ui.showing then return end
    local m = mod_by_name(ui.rows[args.selectedListIndex])
    if m then h7.set_mod_enabled(m.name, not m.enabled) end -- the list is filled again on mod_toggled
    return true -- don't start a map
end)

h7.on("mod_toggled", function()
    if ui.showing then
        restore()
        fill()
    end
end)

h7.hook(SELECT .. "BtnGenerateMapClicked", function()
    if ui.showing then return true end
end)

-- Back closes the window the usual way (back to the main menu); first put the window back as it was.
h7.hook(SELECT .. "BtnBackClicked", function()
    if ui.showing then restore() end
end)
h7.hook(SELECT .. "Update", function()
    if ui.showing then restore() end -- the game opens the window for maps
end)

-- Mouse and keyboard for the settings controls (Flash doesn't report which of our clips was clicked). While a
-- number box is being edited it takes the keys; the post-hook then reports them as handled, so the game and the
-- menu don't act on them (Escape would close the window).
h7.hook("Engine.Console.InputKey", function(console, args)
    ui.key_used = false
    if not ui.showing then return end
    if args.Key == "LeftMouseButton" then
        if args.Event == panel.PRESSED then
            pcall(panel.press)
        elseif args.Event == panel.RELEASED then
            pcall(panel.release)
        end
        return
    end
    local ok, used = pcall(panel.key, args.Key, args.Event)
    ui.key_used = ok and used
end)
h7.hook_post("Engine.Console.InputKey", function(console, args, handled)
    if ui.key_used then
        ui.key_used = false
        return true
    end
end)
h7.on("tick", function()
    if ui.showing then pcall(panel.tick) end
end)

-- The Mods button in the main menu. Its clicks arrive at H7MainMenuCntl.Options without event data, so the
-- hook checks whether the mouse is over it.
local function under_mouse(o)
    if not (o and o:GetBool("visible")) then return false end
    local mx, my = o:GetFloat("mouseX"), o:GetFloat("mouseY")
    local w, h = o:GetFloat("width") / o:GetFloat("scaleX"), o:GetFloat("height") / o:GetFloat("scaleY")
    return mx >= 0 and my >= 0 and mx < w and my < h
end

h7.hook("MMH7Game.H7MainMenuCntl.Options", function(cntl, args)
    if ui.cntl and under_mouse(child(ui.cntl.mMainMenu, "mBtnMods")) then
        open()
        return true -- don't open the options menu
    end
end)

-- The main menu's sub-menus (single player, multiplayer, extras...) hide the movie's own buttons, which the movie
-- tracks in a list our button isn't in. So the Mods button copies the Options button's visibility,
-- transparency and horizontal position.
local function follow_options()
    local cntl = ui.cntl
    if not (cntl and cntl:is_valid()) then return end
    local menu = cntl.mMainMenu
    local options, mods = child(menu, "mBtnOptions"), child(menu, "mBtnMods")
    if not (options and mods) then return end
    local visible, alpha, x = options:GetBool("visible"), options:GetFloat("alpha"), options:GetFloat("x")
    if mods:GetBool("visible") ~= visible then mods:SetBool("visible", visible) end
    if mods:GetFloat("alpha") ~= alpha then mods:SetFloat("alpha", alpha) end
    if mods:GetFloat("x") ~= x then mods:SetFloat("x", x) end
end

-- Adds the Mods button to the main menu: Extras and Options move up into the free slot above them, Mods takes
-- Options' place and Quit stays at the bottom of the frame.
local function install()
    local cntl = h7.first("H7MainMenuCntl")
    local menu = cntl and cntl.mMainMenu
    if not menu then return end
    if ui.cntl ~= cntl then -- a new main menu
        for k in pairs(ui) do ui[k] = nil end
        ui.cntl = cntl
    end
    ui.open = open -- for tests\modwindow_autotest.lua
    ui.panel = panel
    if not child(menu, "mBtnMods") then
        local extras, options = child(menu, "mBtnExtras"), child(menu, "mBtnOptions")
        local step = options:GetFloat("y") - extras:GetFloat("y")
        local button = menu:AttachMovie("H7TextButton", "mBtnMods", -1, Clik)
        button:AddEventListener("CLIK_click", { object = cntl, func = "Options" })
        button:SetString("htmlText", "Mods")
        for _, f in ipairs({ "x", "y", "width", "height" }) do button:SetFloat(f, options:GetFloat(f)) end
        extras:SetFloat("y", extras:GetFloat("y") - step)
        options:SetFloat("y", options:GetFloat("y") - step)
    end
    h7.log("Mods button installed")
end

h7.on("map_loaded", function(map)
    if map == "councilhub" then h7.after(1, install) else ui.cntl = nil end
end)
h7.every(0.1, function()
    if ui.cntl then pcall(follow_options) end
end)
install() -- already at the main menu (hot reload)

```

`dist/mods/ModManager/mod.lua`:

```lua
return {
    name = "ModManager",
    version = "1.0",
    author = "H7ModEngine",
    description = "Adds a Mods entry to the main menu to see, enable and disable mods.",
    hidden = true, -- not listed in its own menu
}

```

`dist/mods/ModManager/panel.lua`:

```lua
-- The right side of the Mods window: the selected mod's description in the picture frame, and its settings
-- below it as controls (sliders for numbers, check boxes for on/off, arrows for choices).
--
-- The controls are clips from the map window's own library (H7Label, H7Slider_BG_Gold, H7Slider_Gold_Thumb,
-- H7GUICommandButton, H7LeftArrow/H7RightArrow, H7TooltipBG as the number box) attached to the window. Flash doesn't
-- tell Unreal which clip was clicked, so mouse and keyboard input come from Engine.Console.InputKey: clicks are
-- hit-tested against the controls' rectangles in window coordinates, and a number box being edited takes the keys.

local P = {}

local Obj = h7.find_class("GFxUI.GFxObject")
local AS = h7.enum("ASType")
local PRESSED, RELEASED = 0, 1

-- Layout in window coordinates (the window is 1920x1080 inside the movie).
local FRAME = { x = 1366, y = 128, w = 452, h = 456 }           -- inside the picture frame
local AREA = { x = 1342, y = 640, w = 470, h = 300 }            -- the details panel below it
local ROW_H, ROWS = 48, 5
local LABEL_W = 210
local CTRL_X = AREA.x + LABEL_W + 20
local VALUE_W = 70
local TRACK_W = AREA.x + AREA.w - VALUE_W - 10 - CTRL_X

local state = { mod = nil, rows = {}, drag = nil }
P.state = state

local function window()
    local sel = h7.first("H7MapSelectCntl")
    return sel and sel.mMapSelection
end

-- Clips are looked up by name every time: the engine frees GFxObject wrappers nobody in UnrealScript holds.
local function get(name)
    local w = window()
    return w and w:GetObject(name, Obj)
end

local function ensure(name, linkage)
    local o = get(name)
    if not o then o = window():AttachMovie(linkage, name, -1, Obj) end
    return o
end

local function hide(name)
    local o = get(name)
    if o then o:SetBool("visible", false) end
end

local function place(o, x, y, w, h)
    o:SetFloat("x", x)
    o:SetFloat("y", y)
    if w then o:SetFloat("width", w) end
    if h then o:SetFloat("height", h) end
    o:SetBool("visible", true)
end

local function label(name, text, x, y, w, size, multiline)
    local o = ensure(name, "H7Label")
    if multiline then o:SetBool("multiline", true) end
    if size then o:SetFloat("size", size) end
    o:SetFloat("width", w)
    o:SetString("text", text)
    place(o, x, y)
    return o
end

local function num(v)
    if v == math.floor(v) and math.abs(v) < 1e15 then return ("%d"):format(v) end
    return (("%.3f"):format(v):gsub("0+$", ""):gsub("%.$", ""))
end

local function value_text(s, v)
    if v == nil then v = s.value end
    if s.type == "bool" then return v and "On" or "Off" end
    if s.type == "number" then return num(v) end
    return tostring(v)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Rows
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

local ROW_PARTS = { "lbl", "track", "thumb", "val", "check", "left", "right", "frame", "boxtext", "hint" }
local BOX_W = 150

local function hide_row(i)
    for _, part in ipairs(ROW_PARTS) do hide(("mModSet%d_%s"):format(i, part)) end
end

-- Position of the slider thumb for value v.
local function slider_fraction(s, v)
    if not (s.min and s.max) or s.max <= s.min then return 0 end
    return math.max(0, math.min(1, (v - s.min) / (s.max - s.min)))
end

local function draw_slider(i, s, v, cy)
    local track = ensure(("mModSet%d_track"):format(i), "H7Slider_BG_Gold")
    -- The gold bar is vertical: rotated -90 degrees it runs to the right of its origin, its thickness upwards.
    track:SetFloat("rotation", 0)
    track:SetFloat("height", TRACK_W)
    track:SetFloat("rotation", -90)
    place(track, CTRL_X, cy + 7)
    local thumb = ensure(("mModSet%d_thumb"):format(i), "H7Slider_Gold_Thumb")
    local tw, th = thumb:GetFloat("width"), thumb:GetFloat("height")
    place(thumb, CTRL_X + slider_fraction(s, v) * TRACK_W - tw / 2, cy - th / 2)
    label(("mModSet%d_val"):format(i), value_text(s, v), CTRL_X + TRACK_W + 10, cy - 12, VALUE_W, 20)
end

-- How a setting is edited: the manifest's `control` when it makes sense for the type, else a slider for numbers
-- with a range, a number box for other numbers.
local function kind_of(s)
    if s.type == "bool" then return "check" end
    if s.type == "choice" then return "arrows" end
    if s.type == "number" then
        if s.control == "input" or s.control == "arrows" then return s.control end
        return (s.min and s.max) and "slider" or "input"
    end
    return "text"
end

-- The number box: the game's tooltip background as the frame, a label with the value (or the text being typed
-- followed by a cursor) on top of it.
local function draw_box(i, s, text, editing, cy)
    local box = ensure(("mModSet%d_frame"):format(i), "H7TooltipBG")
    place(box, CTRL_X, cy - 17, BOX_W, 34)
    box:SetFloat("alpha", editing and 1 or 0.6)
    label(("mModSet%d_boxtext"):format(i), editing and (text .. "_") or text, CTRL_X + 10, cy - 12, BOX_W - 20, 20)
    local range = (s.min and s.max) and (num(s.min) .. " - " .. num(s.max)) or (s.min and (">= " .. num(s.min)))
        or (s.max and ("<= " .. num(s.max))) or ""
    label(("mModSet%d_hint"):format(i), editing and "Enter: save   Esc: cancel" or range, CTRL_X + BOX_W + 10,
        cy - 10, AREA.x + AREA.w - CTRL_X - BOX_W - 10, 16)
end

local function draw_row(i, s)
    local y = AREA.y + 44 + (i - 1) * ROW_H
    local cy = y + ROW_H / 2
    hide_row(i)
    label(("mModSet%d_lbl"):format(i), s.label, AREA.x, cy - 12, LABEL_W, 20)
    local r = { setting = s, cy = cy }
    local kind = kind_of(s)
    if kind == "input" then
        local editing = state.edit and state.edit.row == i
        draw_box(i, s, editing and state.edit.text or value_text(s), editing, cy)
        r.kind = "input"
        r.box = { x = CTRL_X, y = cy - 18, w = BOX_W, h = 36 }
    elseif kind == "slider" then
        draw_slider(i, s, s.value, cy)
        r.kind = "slider"
        r.box = { x = CTRL_X - 10, y = cy - 18, w = TRACK_W + 20, h = 36 }
    elseif kind == "check" then
        local c = ensure(("mModSet%d_check"):format(i), "H7GUICommandButton")
        place(c, CTRL_X, cy - 18, 36, 36)
        c:Invoke("DisplaySelectState", { { Type = AS.AS_Boolean, b = s.value } })
        c:SetFloat("alpha", s.value and 1 or 0.35) -- the button's own selected look is subtle
        label(("mModSet%d_val"):format(i), value_text(s), CTRL_X + 46, cy - 12, VALUE_W, 20)
        r.kind = "check"
        r.box = { x = CTRL_X, y = cy - 18, w = 46 + VALUE_W, h = 36 }
    elseif kind == "arrows" then
        local left = ensure(("mModSet%d_left"):format(i), "H7LeftArrow")
        local right = ensure(("mModSet%d_right"):format(i), "H7RightArrow")
        place(left, CTRL_X, cy - 14, 28, 28)
        place(right, AREA.x + AREA.w - 28, cy - 14, 28, 28)
        label(("mModSet%d_val"):format(i), value_text(s), CTRL_X + 36, cy - 12, AREA.x + AREA.w - 36 - CTRL_X - 36, 20)
        r.kind = "arrows"
        r.left = { x = CTRL_X, y = cy - 16, w = 32, h = 32 }
        r.right = { x = AREA.x + AREA.w - 32, y = cy - 16, w = 32, h = 32 }
    else
        label(("mModSet%d_val"):format(i), value_text(s) .. "  (edit in H7ModEngine.ini)", CTRL_X, cy - 12,
            AREA.x + AREA.w - CTRL_X, 18)
        r.kind = "text"
    end
    state.rows[i] = r
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Showing a mod
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function P.show(m)
    if not window() then return end
    state.mod, state.rows, state.drag, state.pending, state.edit = m and m.name, {}, nil, false, nil
    for i = 1, ROWS do hide_row(i) end
    if not m then
        for _, n in ipairs({ "mModDesc", "mModSetTitle", "mModSetMore" }) do hide(n) end
        return
    end
    local about = m.description ~= "" and m.description or "No description."
    local by = (m.author ~= "" and ("by " .. m.author) or "") .. (m.version ~= "" and ("   version " .. m.version) or "")
    label("mModDesc", m.name .. "\n" .. by .. "\n\n" .. about, FRAME.x, FRAME.y, FRAME.w, 22, true)

    local settings = h7.mod_settings(m.name)
    label("mModSetTitle", #settings > 0 and "Settings" or "This mod has no settings.", AREA.x, AREA.y, AREA.w, 24)
    for i, s in ipairs(settings) do
        if i > ROWS then break end
        draw_row(i, s)
    end
    if #settings > ROWS then
        label("mModSetMore", ("+%d more in H7ModEngine.ini, section [Mod.%s]"):format(#settings - ROWS, m.name),
            AREA.x, AREA.y + 44 + ROWS * ROW_H, AREA.w, 16)
    else
        hide("mModSetMore")
    end
end

function P.hide_all()
    for i = 1, ROWS do hide_row(i) end
    for _, n in ipairs({ "mModDesc", "mModSetTitle", "mModSetMore" }) do hide(n) end
    state.mod, state.rows, state.drag, state.pending, state.edit = nil, {}, nil, false, nil
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Input
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function inside(b, x, y) return b and x >= b.x and y >= b.y and x < b.x + b.w and y < b.y + b.h end

local function mouse()
    local w = window()
    if not w then return nil end
    return w:GetFloat("mouseX"), w:GetFloat("mouseY")
end

local function slider_value(s, mx)
    local t = math.max(0, math.min(1, (mx - CTRL_X) / TRACK_W))
    local v = s.min + t * (s.max - s.min)
    if s.step then v = s.min + math.floor((v - s.min) / s.step + 0.5) * s.step end
    return math.max(s.min, math.min(s.max, v))
end

local function set(i, s, v)
    local stored = h7.set_mod_setting(state.mod, s.key, v)
    s.value = stored
    draw_row(i, s)
end

-- Number box editing. The text is checked when it is saved: h7.set_mod_setting clamps it to the range and rounds
-- it to the step; text that isn't a number is dropped.
local function stop_edit(save)
    local e = state.edit
    if not e then return end
    state.edit = nil
    local r = state.rows[e.row]
    if not r then return end
    local v = tonumber(e.text)
    if save and v then
        set(e.row, r.setting, v)
    else
        draw_row(e.row, r.setting)
    end
end

local function start_edit(i)
    stop_edit(true)
    local r = state.rows[i]
    state.edit = { row = i, text = value_text(r.setting), fresh = true }
    draw_row(i, r.setting)
end

-- Key names as the engine reports them, lower-cased (names compare case-insensitively in Unreal, and the engine
-- keeps the spelling a name was first created with: "seven", "Enter").
local KEY_CHARS = {
    zero = "0", one = "1", two = "2", three = "3", four = "4", five = "5", six = "6", seven = "7", eight = "8",
    nine = "9", numpadzero = "0", numpadone = "1", numpadtwo = "2", numpadthree = "3", numpadfour = "4",
    numpadfive = "5", numpadsix = "6", numpadseven = "7", numpadeight = "8", numpadnine = "9",
    period = ".", decimal = ".", comma = ".", subtract = "-", underscore = "-", minus = "-",
}

-- A key while a number box is being edited; returns true when the key was used (the game shouldn't see it).
function P.key(key, event)
    local e = state.edit
    if not e then return false end
    if event ~= PRESSED and event ~= 2 then return true end -- releases and repeats of other keys: swallowed
    key = tostring(key):lower()
    if key == "enter" then stop_edit(true) return true end
    if key == "escape" then stop_edit(false) return true end
    if key == "backspace" or key == "delete" then
        e.text = e.fresh and "" or e.text:sub(1, -2)
        e.fresh = false
    else
        local c = KEY_CHARS[key]
        if not c or event ~= PRESSED then return c ~= nil end
        if e.fresh then e.text = "" e.fresh = false end
        if c == "-" and e.text ~= "" then return true end
        if c == "." and e.text:find(".", 1, true) then return true end
        if #e.text < 12 then e.text = e.text .. c end
    end
    local r = state.rows[e.row]
    if r then draw_row(e.row, r.setting) end
    return true
end

function P.editing() return state.edit ~= nil end

local function cycle(s, dir)
    if s.type == "choice" then
        for k, o in ipairs(s.options) do
            if o == s.value then return s.options[(k - 1 + dir) % #s.options + 1] end
        end
        return s.options[1]
    end
    return s.value + dir * (s.step or 1) -- numbers without a range
end

-- The button event arrives before Flash has seen the mouse move that came with it, so a press is handled on the
-- next frame (P.tick), when the window's mouse position is current.
function P.press()
    if state.mod then state.pending = true end
end

local function handle_press()
    if not state.mod then return false end
    local mx, my = mouse()
    if not mx then return false end
    for i, r in pairs(state.rows) do
        if r.kind == "input" and inside(r.box, mx, my) then
            if not (state.edit and state.edit.row == i) then start_edit(i) end
            return true
        end
    end
    stop_edit(true) -- a click anywhere else saves the number being typed
    for i, r in pairs(state.rows) do
        local s = r.setting
        if r.kind == "slider" and inside(r.box, mx, my) then
            state.drag = { row = i, value = slider_value(s, mx) }
            draw_slider(i, s, state.drag.value, r.cy)
            return true
        elseif r.kind == "check" and inside(r.box, mx, my) then
            set(i, s, not s.value)
            return true
        elseif r.kind == "arrows" and (inside(r.left, mx, my) or inside(r.right, mx, my)) then
            set(i, s, cycle(s, inside(r.left, mx, my) and -1 or 1))
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function P.release()
    if state.pending then
        state.pending = false
        handle_press()
    end
    local d = state.drag
    if not d then return end
    state.drag = nil
    local r = state.rows[d.row]
    if r then set(d.row, r.setting, d.value) end
end

-- While a slider is held, its thumb and value follow the mouse; the setting is saved on release.
function P.tick()
    if state.pending then
        state.pending = false
        handle_press()
    end
    local d = state.drag
    if not d then return end
    local r = state.rows[d.row]
    local mx = mouse()
    if not (r and mx) then state.drag = nil return end
    local v = slider_value(r.setting, mx)
    if v ~= d.value then
        d.value = v
        draw_slider(d.row, r.setting, v, r.cy)
    end
end

P.PRESSED, P.RELEASED = PRESSED, RELEASED
return P

```

`docs/API.md`:

```md
# H7ModEngine modding API

H7ModEngine lets you write mods for **Might & Magic Heroes VII** (v2.2.1) in **Lua 5.4**. A mod can read and change
any game object, call any UnrealScript function, and hook game functions to watch, change or block what they do.
Mods reload automatically while the game runs, so you can edit and see the result without restarting.

- [Installation](#installation)
- [Your first mod](#your-first-mod)
- [Mod folder and manifest](#mod-folder-and-manifest)
- [Finding what to change](#finding-what-to-change)
- [Engine objects](#engine-objects)
- [Values and types](#values-and-types)
- [Looking things up](#looking-things-up)
- [Hooks](#hooks)
- [Events and timers](#events-and-timers)
- [Saving data](#saving-data)
- [Logging and debugging](#logging-and-debugging)
- [Configuration](#configuration)
- [Reference](#reference)
- [Limitations](#limitations)

## Installation

1. Copy `dinput8.dll` into `<Game>\Binaries\Win64\`.
2. Copy the `H7ModEngine` folder into the game folder (`<Game>\H7ModEngine\`).
3. Start the game as usual (through Ubisoft Connect).

`<Game>\H7ModEngine\` then holds everything the engine uses:

| Folder | Contents |
|---|---|
| `mods\` | One folder per mod |
| `logs\engine.log` | Everything the engine and your mods log (the previous run is in `engine.prev.log`) |
| `data\` | Saved mod data (`mod.save`) |
| `lib\` | Shared Lua libraries any mod can `require` |
| `exec\` | Drop a `.lua` file here to run it once in the running game (for testing) |
| `dumps\` | `objects.txt` / `names.txt`: an index of every engine object, written at startup |
| `H7ModEngine.ini` | Configuration |

## Your first mod

Create `<Game>\H7ModEngine\mods\MyFirstMod\main.lua`:

```lua
h7.log("Hello from " .. mod.name)

h7.on("map_loaded", function(map)
    h7.log("Entered map: " .. map)
end)
```

Start the game and open `logs\engine.log`:

```
[19:32:14.962] [MyFirstMod] Hello from MyFirstMod
[19:32:14.962] Loaded mod MyFirstMod
[19:32:14.963] [MyFirstMod] Entered map: h7logoentry
[19:32:19.583] [MyFirstMod] Entered map: councilhub
```

Now edit `main.lua` while the game runs and save it: within a second the engine unloads the mod and runs it
again. Errors don't crash the game; they are logged with a stack trace:

```
[19:40:01.120] ERROR in MyFirstMod/main.lua: ...\mods\MyFirstMod\main.lua:3: attempt to call a nil value ...
```

A real gameplay mod is just as short. This one gives the human player 500 gold every new day:

```lua
h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7Calendar.NextDay", function(calendar)
    local controller = h7.first("H7AdventureController")
    local player = controller and controller:GetLocalPlayer(true)
    if not player then return end
    player:GetResourceSet():ModifyCurrency(500, true)
    h7.log("+500 gold for " .. player:GetName())
end)
```

## Mod folder and manifest

```
mods\
  MyMod\
    mod.lua      -- manifest (optional)
    main.lua     -- runs when the mod loads (required)
    util.lua     -- extra files, loaded with mod.require("util")
```

`mod.lua` returns a table. Every field is optional; without a manifest the folder name is the mod name.

```lua
return {
    name = "MyMod",              -- default: folder name
    version = "1.0",
    author = "You",
    description = "What the mod does.",
    load_after = { "OtherMod" }, -- load after these mods (if they are installed)
    enabled = true,              -- false: off until a player turns it on
    category = "Combat",         -- shown (and filterable) in the Mods menu
    hidden = false,              -- true: not listed in the in-game Mods menu
    settings = { ... },          -- values players can change in the Mods menu, see Settings below
}
```

**Load order.** Mods load alphabetically by name, except that a mod is moved after every mod it lists in
`load_after`. After all mods have loaded, the `mods_loaded` event fires.

**Turning mods on and off.** Players use the **Mods** entry in the main menu (the bundled ModManager mod),
which lists every mod with its description and an Enabled/Disabled toggle. A change applies at once: the mod
loads or unloads on the next frame. The choice is stored in `H7ModEngine.ini` (see
[Configuration](#configuration)) and overrides the manifest's `enabled`. Mods can do the same with
`h7.set_mod_enabled(name, on)`; the `mod_toggled` event reports each change.

**Settings.** A mod can offer values that players change in the Mods menu: selecting a mod shows its description
and, below it, its settings as controls.
Declare them in the manifest:

```lua
settings = {
    { key = "multiplier", label = "Damage multiplier", default = 1.25, min = 0.25, max = 4, step = 0.25,
      description = "All combat damage is multiplied by this." },
    { key = "gold", label = "Gold per day", default = 500, min = 0, max = 100000, control = "input" },
    { key = "log", label = "Log hits", default = true },
    { key = "who", label = "Applies to", options = { "Human player", "Everyone" }, default = "Human player" },
    { key = "greeting", label = "Greeting", type = "text", default = "Hello" },
}
```

| Field | |
|---|---|
| `key` | Name used in code (required) |
| `label`, `description` | Shown in the Mods menu (`label` defaults to `key`) |
| `type` | `"bool"`, `"number"`, `"choice"` or `"text"`; inferred from `default` (and `options`) when left out |
| `default` | Value until the player changes it |
| `min`, `max`, `step` | Numbers: values are clamped to `min`..`max` and rounded to a multiple of `step` (counted from `min`) |
| `options` | Choices: the allowed values, in the order the menu cycles through them |
| `reload` | `true`: the mod restarts when the value changes (for values only read at load time) |
| `control` | Numbers: how the Mods menu shows it. `"slider"` (default with `min` and `max`), `"input"` (a box the player types the number into; default without a range) or `"arrows"` (steps of `step`) |

Read the current values from `mod.settings` (`mod.settings.multiplier`); it always has the latest value, so read
it when you need it rather than copying it once. Assigning (`mod.settings.log = false`) changes the setting like
the menu does. `pairs(mod.settings)` lists them. Values are stored in `H7ModEngine.ini`, section
`[Mod.<name>]`. When a value changes the `setting_changed` event fires (`mod`, `key`, `value`). Other mods
and exec scripts use `h7.mod_settings(name)` (a list of the declarations with their current `value`) and
`h7.set_mod_setting(name, key, value)`, which returns the stored (clamped) value and raises an error for an
unknown key or an invalid value. In the menu, numbers get a slider (drag or click it) or a number box (click it,
type, Enter saves, Escape cancels, clicking elsewhere saves; the value is clamped to the range), on/off settings
a check box, choices arrows that step through the values; text settings are shown but can only be changed in
code or the ini. Every change is saved at once. The panel has room
for five settings; further ones are only listed as being in the ini.

**Each mod has its own globals.** Global variables you create stay inside your mod; reading a global you didn't
create falls through to the shared environment (`h7`, `string`, `math`, `table`, ...). Two mods can both define
`function on_unload()` without clashing.

**The `mod` table** is available in every mod:

| Field | |
|---|---|
| `mod.name`, `mod.version`, `mod.author` | From the manifest |
| `mod.dir` | Full path of the mod folder |
| `mod.require(name)` | Runs `<mod folder>\<name>.lua` in the mod's environment once and returns its result (or `true`); later calls return the cached result |
| `mod.save(table)` / `mod.load()` | Persistent data, see [Saving data](#saving-data) |
| `mod.settings` | The mod's settings: read the current values, assign to change them (see Settings above) |

**Unloading and hot reload.** About once a second the engine checks each loaded mod's folder (including
subfolders) for changed `.lua` files. When one changed, the mod is unloaded and its `main.lua` runs again.
Unloading calls the mod's global `on_unload()` if it defines one, then removes **everything the mod
registered**: hooks, event handlers and timers. You don't have to clean those up yourself.

Hot reload only covers mods that are already loaded. To pick up a new mod folder or a mod you just enabled,
call `h7.reload()` (for example from the `exec` folder) or restart the game.

**Shared libraries.** The standard `require("name")` loads `<Game>\H7ModEngine\lib\name.lua`. Use it for code
several mods share.

## Finding what to change

Heroes VII's gameplay is written in UnrealScript, and every class, property and function in it is reachable from
Lua. To find the right one:

- **Read the UnrealScript sources.** Decompiled sources for the game (the `MMH7Game` package has about 1400
  classes) are the best map: they show what each function does and who calls it. Search them for the
  behaviour you want to change, then hook the function that computes it.
- **Search `dumps\objects.txt`.** One line per engine object, written at startup:
  `12345 00007ff4fd9f4f40 Function MMH7Game.H7Calendar.NextDay`.
- **Ask the engine.** From a mod or the `exec` folder:

  ```lua
  h7.log(h7.signature("MMH7Game.H7GameProcessor.CalculateDamage"))
  -- function void MMH7Game.H7GameProcessor.CalculateDamage(out H7CombatResult Result, int resultIdx, bool forecast)

  for _, f in ipairs(h7.functions("MMH7Game.H7Calendar")) do h7.log(f.owner, f.name) end
  for _, p in ipairs(h7.properties("MMH7Game.H7Calendar")) do h7.log(p.type, p.name) end
  ```

Hook targets used by the example mods (all verified in game):

| To change | Hook | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Start of a new day | `MMH7Game.H7Calendar.NextDay` | Called from `H7AdventureController.SetNextPlayer` when the last player ends the turn |
| Damage of an attack | `MMH7Game.H7GameProcessor.CalculateDamage` | Also runs for damage previews (`forecast = true`) and heals |
| Experience after combat | `MMH7Game.H7CombatArmy.GetKilledCreaturesXP` | `H7EditorHero.AddXp` is native and can't be hooked usefully |
| Hero movement points | `MMH7Game.H7EditorHero.GetMovementPoints` | Caravans are `H7EditorHero`s too |
