### Events

| Event | Arguments |
|---|---|
| [`tick`](events.md#tick) | `dt` |
| [`map_loaded`](events.md#map_loaded) | `map` |
| [`mods_loaded`](events.md#mods_loaded) | `count` |
| [`mod_toggled`](events.md#mod_toggled) | `name`, `enabled` |
| [`setting_changed`](events.md#setting_changed) | `name`, `key`, `value` |

## Conventions in this reference

- Signatures are written `h7.find_all(class [, include_templates]) → table`: square brackets mark optional
  parameters, `→` what the function returns, `|` alternatives.
- **class** parameters take a class name (`"H7AdventureHero"` or `"MMH7Game.H7AdventureHero"`) or a class object.
- **func** parameters take a function path (`"MMH7Game.H7Calendar.NextDay"`, optionally prefixed with
  `"Function "`) or a function object.
- **Errors** are raised with Lua's `error`, so a mistake stops the current chunk, hook, handler or timer and is
  logged with a stack trace (see [Errors](../API.md#logging-and-debugging)); use `pcall` to handle one yourself.
- Every hook, event handler and timer belongs to the mod whose code created it and is removed when that mod
  unloads. Ones created from an `exec` script belong to no mod: nothing removes them automatically.

```

`docs/reference/mod.md`:

```md
# Mods, manifest and settings

## Mod folder

```
<Game>\H7ModEngine\mods\
  MyMod\
    mod.lua      -- manifest (optional)
    main.lua     -- runs when the mod loads (required)
    util.lua     -- extra files, loaded with mod.require("util")
```

A folder without `main.lua` is listed but never loads (`Mod X has no main.lua; skipped` in the log). A folder
whose `mod.lua` raises an error is skipped entirely.

## Manifest

`mod.lua` returns a table. Every field is optional.

```lua
return {
    name = "MyMod",
    version = "1.0",
    author = "You",
    description = "What the mod does.",
    category = "Combat",
    load_after = { "OtherMod" },
    enabled = true,
    hidden = false,
    settings = { ... },
}
```

| Field | Type | Default | |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | folder name | The mod's name: in the log, the Mods menu, `H7ModEngine.ini`, `data\<name>.lua` and every `h7.*` function that takes a mod name. Keep it stable |
| `version` | string | `""` | Shown in the Mods menu and the log |
| `author` | string | `""` | Shown in the Mods menu and the log |
| `description` | string | `""` | Shown in the Mods menu |
| `category` | string | `""` | Shown, and filterable, in the Mods menu |
| `load_after` | `{ string, ... }` | `{}` | Mods (by `name`) this one must load after, when they are installed |
| `enabled` | boolean | `true` | `false`: off until the player turns it on. The player's choice (stored in the ini) overrides this |
| `hidden` | boolean | `false` | `true`: not listed in the Mods menu (libraries, the ModManager itself) |
| `settings` | `{ setting, ... }` | `{}` | Values players change in the Mods menu, see [Settings](#settings) |

The manifest runs once when mods are discovered (at startup and on [`h7.reload()`](h7.md#h7reload)), in the shared
environment, so keep it to a plain `return { ... }`.

**Load order.** Mods load alphabetically by name (case-insensitive), except that each mod is moved after every
installed mod it lists in `load_after`. A cycle is logged as `load_after cycle among remaining mods` and the mods
in it load alphabetically. After all enabled mods have loaded, [`mods_loaded`](events.md#mods_loaded) fires.

## Settings

Each entry of the manifest's `settings` list declares one value players can change in the Mods menu:

```lua
settings = {
    { key = "multiplier", label = "Damage multiplier", default = 1.25, min = 0.25, max = 4, step = 0.25,
      description = "All combat damage is multiplied by this." },
    { key = "gold", label = "Gold per day", default = 500, min = 0, max = 100000, control = "input" },
    { key = "log", label = "Log hits", default = true },
    { key = "who", label = "Applies to", options = { "Human player", "Everyone" }, default = "Human player" },
    { key = "greeting", label = "Greeting", type = "text", default = "Hello" },
}
```

| Field | | |
|---|---|---|
| `key` | string, **required** | Name used in code (`mod.settings.key`) and in the ini. Entries without a key are ignored |
| `label` | string | Shown in the Mods menu. Default: `key` |
| `description` | string | Shown in the Mods menu |
| `type` | `"bool"` \| `"number"` \| `"choice"` \| `"text"` | Default: inferred, `bool` for a boolean `default`, `number` for a number, `choice` when `options` is given, otherwise `text` |
| `default` | value | Value until the player changes it. Missing or invalid: `false`, `min` (or `0`), the first option, or `""` |
| `min`, `max` | number | Numbers: allowed range (values are clamped) |
| `step` | number | Numbers: values are rounded to a multiple of `step`, counted from `min` (or `0`) |
| `options` | `{ value, ... }` | Choices: the allowed values, in the order the menu cycles through them. Required for `choice` |
| `control` | `"slider"` \| `"input"` \| `"arrows"` | Numbers: how the menu shows it. Default: `slider` with both `min` and `max`, otherwise `input` |
| `reload` | boolean | `true`: the mod restarts when the value changes (for values only read at load time) |

Invalid declarations (unknown `type`, a `choice` without `options`) are logged and skipped.

**Values.** Booleans come back as `true`/`false`, numbers as integers when whole, choices and text as strings.
Choices compare case-insensitively and are stored with the spelling of `options`. Booleans also accept
`"true"/"false"`, `"yes"/"no"`, `"on"/"off"`, `1`/`0`. Values are stored in `H7ModEngine.ini`, section
`[Mod.<name>]`; an invalid value there is logged and replaced by the default.

**In the Mods menu** numbers get a slider, a number box (Enter saves, Escape cancels) or arrows, booleans a check
box, and choices arrows. Text settings are shown but can only be changed in code or in the ini. The panel shows up
to five settings; further ones are listed as being in the ini.

## The mod table

Every mod has a global `mod` table describing itself:

| Field | |
|---|---|
| `mod.name` | The mod's name (manifest `name` or folder name) |
| `mod.version` | Manifest `version` (or `""`) |
| `mod.author` | Manifest `author` (or `""`) |
| `mod.dir` | Full path of the mod folder, e.g. `C:\...\H7ModEngine\mods\MyMod` |
| `mod.require(name)` | See [below](#modrequire) |
| `mod.save(table)`, `mod.load()` | See [below](#modsave) |
| `mod.settings` | See [below](#modsettings) |

### mod.require

```lua
mod.require(name) → value
```

Runs `<mod folder>\<name>.lua` in the mod's environment (it sees and sets the same globals as `main.lua`) and
returns what the file returns, or `true` if it returns nothing. The file runs once per load of the mod; later calls
return the cached result. `name` is a path relative to the mod folder without `.lua`: `"util"`,
`"lib/strings"`.

**Errors:** the file is missing or has a syntax error; any error it raises.

```lua
-- util.lua
local M = {}
function M.round(x) return math.floor(x + 0.5) end
return M

-- main.lua
local util = mod.require("util")
```

### mod.save

```lua
mod.save(table)
```

Writes `table` to `<Game>\H7ModEngine\data\<mod name>.lua`, replacing what was saved before. Numbers, strings,
booleans and nested tables are kept; other values (functions, engine objects) become `nil`. Keys must be strings,
numbers or booleans. The data belongs to the mod, not to a save game.

**Errors:** `table too deep to save` (more than 32 levels, or a table that contains itself); `could not write save
data`.

### mod.load

```lua
mod.load() → table
```

The table last saved with [`mod.save`](#modsave), or an empty table if there is none. The data file is read
without access to any functions.

```lua
local data = mod.load()
data.launches = (data.launches or 0) + 1
mod.save(data)
```

### mod.settings

```lua
mod.settings.key → value
mod.settings.key = value
pairs(mod.settings)
```

The mod's [settings](#settings). Reading gives the current value (`nil` for an unknown key); read it when you need
it rather than copying it at load time, since players can change it at any time. Assigning works like
[`h7.set_mod_setting`](h7.md#h7set_mod_setting): the value is validated and saved and
[`setting_changed`](events.md#setting_changed) fires. `pairs(mod.settings)` iterates `key, value`.

**Errors (assigning):** `mod <name> has no setting '<key>'`; `invalid value for <name>.<key> (<type>)`.

```lua
h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7CombatArmy.GetKilledCreaturesXP", function(army, args, xp)
    return math.floor(xp * mod.settings.multiplier + 0.5)
end)
```

## Globals

Each mod has its own global environment. Globals a mod assigns stay inside it; reading a global it didn't assign
falls through to the shared environment (`h7`, `string`, `math`, ...). Two mods can both define the same global
names without clashing. `_G` still refers to the shared environment.

### on_unload

```lua
function on_unload() ... end
```

If a mod defines a global function `on_unload`, it is called before the mod unloads (when it's disabled, hot
reloaded, or on [`h7.reload()`](h7.md#h7reload)). Use it to undo changes the game won't undo by itself, such as
property values you changed. Hooks, event handlers and timers the mod registered are removed after it returns;
you don't need to remove them yourself.

## Loading, hot reload and errors

- Loading runs `main.lua` in the mod's environment. An error in it is logged
  (`ERROR in MyMod/main.lua: ...`) with a stack trace; whatever the mod registered before the error stays active.
- About once a second, the engine checks each loaded mod's folder (including subfolders) for changed `.lua`
  files. When one changed, the mod is unloaded and loaded again. Fixing an error and saving the file therefore
  retries the mod.
- New mod folders and mods turned on in the ini need [`h7.reload()`](h7.md#h7reload) or a restart; mods turned on
  in the Mods menu or with [`h7.set_mod_enabled`](h7.md#h7set_mod_enabled) load on the next frame.
- Errors in hooks, event handlers and timers are logged with the mod's name and a stack trace; the hook, handler
  or timer stays registered.

## Shared libraries

The standard `require(name)` loads `<Game>\H7ModEngine\lib\<name>.lua`; as usual in Lua, dots in `name` are
folders (`require("ui.list")` loads `lib\ui\list.lua`). The library runs once, in the shared environment, and its result is shared by every mod. Use it for code
several mods share; use [`mod.require`](#modrequire) for a mod's own files.

```

`docs/reference/objects.md`:

```md
# Engine objects, structs and arrays

Values from the engine reach Lua as three kinds of reference. They are **live**: reading one always gives the
engine's current value, and writing one changes the game immediately. How individual values are converted is in
[Values and types](types.md).

## Object references

An object reference points at an engine object: an actor, hero, player, army, class, function, archetype, ... You
get them from [`h7.first`](h7.md#h7first), [`h7.find`](h7.md#h7find) and friends, from properties and function
results, and as `self` in hooks.

```lua
local hero = h7.first("H7AdventureHero")
h7.log(hero)                       -- H7AdventureHero sce_sephinroth.TheWorld.PersistentLevel.H7AdventureHero_0
h7.log(hero.mName)                 -- read a property (declared in H7Unit)
hero.mName = "Sir Lua"             -- write a property
local player = hero:GetPlayer()    -- call a function
```

Looking up `obj.key` checks, in order:

1. the [helper methods](#helper-methods) below (`get_name`, `is_a`, ...): lowercase, so they never collide with
   UnrealScript members, which are spelled as declared;
2. the object's class and its parents: a **function** gives a callable, a **property** gives its value;
3. otherwise `nil`.

| Operation | Result |
|---|---|
| `tostring(obj)` | Class and path, as [`get_full_name`](#objget_full_name); `"<invalid object>"` once destroyed |
| `a == b` | `true` when both refer to the same engine object |
| `obj.Missing` | `nil` |
| `obj.Missing = v` | Error: `<Class> has no property 'Missing'` |

UnrealScript's `private`, `protected` and `const` don't apply: every property can be read and written.

### Properties

```lua
value = obj.Name
obj.Name = value
```

Reads give the value converted as described in [Values and types](types.md): numbers, strings and booleans are
copies, structs and arrays are live [struct](#struct-references) and [array](#array-references) references into
the object. Writes convert the Lua value to the property's type and raise an error if they can't, e.g.
`object H7Player ... is not a H7AdventureHero` or `number has no integer representation`.

Writing a property only stores the value. If the game caches something derived from it (a GUI text, a combat
stat), call the game function that refreshes it, or change the value through the game's own function instead.

### Calling functions

```lua
r, out1, out2 = obj:FunctionName(arg1, arg2, ...)
```

Calls the UnrealScript function on `obj`, exactly as the game would: the call goes through the engine's
`ProcessEvent`, runs the function's code (or the native code behind it) and any hooks on it.

- **Arguments** are taken in declaration order and converted to the parameter types. Missing or `nil` arguments
  are passed as zero (`0`, `false`, `""`, `None`, empty struct or array). UnrealScript **default values for
  `optional` parameters are not applied**: pass them explicitly when the default isn't zero. Extra arguments are
  ignored.
- **Results**: the return value (if the function has one) comes first, then the final values of the `out`
  parameters in declaration order. Structs and arrays come back as plain Lua tables, since the call's copy is gone
  once it returns.
- Use `:` (`obj:F(...)`). `obj.F` alone is a function that expects the object as its first argument.

```lua
-- function void H7EditorHero.GetDamageRangeBase(out int minDamage, out int maxDamage)
local min_damage, max_damage = hero:GetDamageRangeBase()   -- no return value: only the out parameters
```

**Errors:** `engine object ... is no longer valid`; `function calls are not available yet (ProcessEvent not
discovered)` (only very early during startup); argument conversion errors.

Static functions are called on any instance of the class or on its default object
(`h7.find_class("X"):get_default():StaticFunc()`).

### Helper methods

#### obj:get_name

```lua
obj:get_name() → string
```

The object's own name, e.g. `H7AdventureHero_0` or `NextDay`.

#### obj:get_full_name

```lua
obj:get_full_name() → string
```

Class and path: `H7AdventureHero sce_sephinroth.TheWorld.PersistentLevel.H7AdventureHero_0`. This is what
`tostring` gives, and the form [`h7.find`](h7.md#h7find) accepts.

#### obj:get_path_name

```lua
obj:get_path_name() → string
```

The path without the class: `sce_sephinroth.TheWorld.PersistentLevel.H7AdventureHero_0`.

#### obj:get_class

```lua
obj:get_class() → class
```

The object's class, as an object reference (`Class MMH7Game.H7AdventureHero`).

#### obj:get_outer

```lua
obj:get_outer() → object | nil
```

The object that contains this one (its level, package or owning class), or `nil` for a top-level package.

#### obj:get_default

```lua
class:get_default() → object
```

On a class: its default object (`Default__ClassName`), which holds the class's default property values. Changing
a property there changes the default for objects created afterwards.

#### obj:get_super

```lua
obj:get_super() → object | nil
```

On a class, struct or function: its parent (the class it extends, the function it overrides), or `nil`.

#### obj:get_address

```lua
obj:get_address() → integer
```

The object's memory address. Unique while the object lives, so it makes a good table key for per-object caches.
Addresses are reused after an object is destroyed: clear such caches on [`map_loaded`](events.md#map_loaded).

#### obj:is_valid

```lua
obj:is_valid() → boolean
```

`false` once the game has destroyed the object (for example when its map unloads). This and `tostring` are the only things
you can use on a destroyed object; everything else raises `engine object 0x... is no longer valid`.

#### obj:is_a

```lua
obj:is_a(class) → boolean
```

Whether the object's class is `class` or a subclass of it. `class` is a class name or class object.

**Errors:** `unknown class '<name>'`.

```lua
if unit:is_a("H7CreatureStack") then ... end
```

#### obj:call

```lua
obj:call(name, ...) → results
```

Calls the function `name` like `obj:Name(...)`. Use it when a property and a function share a name (the lookup
finds only one of them) or when the name is in a variable.

**Errors:** `<Class> has no function '<name>'`.

## Struct references

A struct reference is a view of a struct stored inside an object, inside another struct, or in hook arguments.
Reading a field reads the engine's current value; writing one changes it in place.

```lua
local loc = actor.Location          -- struct reference (Vector)
h7.log(loc.X, loc.Y, loc.Z)
loc.Z = loc.Z + 100                 -- moves the stored value; same as actor.Location.Z = ...
```

| Operation | Result |
|---|---|
| `s.Field` | The field's value, or `nil` for an unknown field |
| `s.Field = v` | Writes the field. Error for an unknown field: `<Struct> has no field 'X'` |
| `tostring(s)` | `"<StructName>: 0x..."` |
| `h7.to_table(s)` | Plain table copy of all fields |
| `h7.properties(s)` | The struct's fields |

Assigning to a struct property replaces the whole struct, from a struct reference of the same type or from a
table of fields (fields not in the table keep their values):

```lua
actor.Location = { X = 0, Y = 0, Z = 100 }
other.Location = actor.Location     -- copies the struct
```

A struct reference into an object is only as alive as the object: don't keep one after the object may have been
destroyed.

### Hook arguments

The `args` passed to a [hook](h7.md#h7hook) is a struct reference over the function's parameters: read them by
name, assign them to change what the function receives (in a pre-hook) or what the caller gets back through an
`out` parameter (in a post-hook).

`args`, and every struct or array reference read from it, is **only valid while the hook runs**. Using one later
raises `this value referred to hook parameters and is no longer valid`. Copy what you need with
[`h7.to_table`](h7.md#h7to_table). Object references read from `args` stay valid as long as the object.

## Array references

An array reference is a view of an UnrealScript dynamic array (`array<T>`) stored in an object, a struct or hook
arguments. Indices are **1-based**, like Lua tables.

```lua
local obelisks = player.mVisitedObelisks   -- array<H7Obelisk>
h7.log(#obelisks)
for i = 1, #obelisks do h7.log(obelisks[i]) end
for i, obelisk in ipairs(obelisks) do h7.log(i, obelisk) end
```

| Operation | Result |
|---|---|
| `#arr` | Number of elements |
| `arr[i]` | Element `i`, or `nil` outside `1..#arr` |
| `arr[i] = v` | Replaces element `i`. Error outside `1..#arr`: `array index i out of range (1..n); use :add()` |
| `ipairs(arr)` | Iterates the elements |
| `h7.to_table(arr)` | Plain table copy |

`pairs(arr)` doesn't work; use `ipairs` or a numeric `for`.

Assigning a Lua sequence or another array reference to an array property (or array parameter) replaces its
contents: `obj.mNames = { "a", "b" }`. `nil` holes in the sequence become zero elements.

Arrays **returned by a function call** are plain Lua tables, not references.

### arr:add

```lua
arr:add([value]) → integer
```

Appends `value` (or a zero element when it's omitted) and returns the new length. Memory comes from the engine's
allocator, so the game can grow, shrink and free the array as usual.

### arr:remove

```lua
arr:remove(i)
```

Removes element `i`, shifting the following elements down.

**Errors:** `array index out of range`.

```

`docs/reference/types.md`:

```md
# Values and types

How UnrealScript values are converted when Lua reads them (properties, function results, hook arguments) and
writes them (property assignment, function arguments, hook argument changes, post-hook return values).

| UnrealScript | Read as | Written from |
|---|---|---|
| `int` | integer | integer, or a float with an integer value (`3.0`). `3.5` raises `number has no integer representation` |
| `byte`, enum | integer (enums: the value's index; see [`h7.enum`](h7.md#h7enum)) | integer (only the low 8 bits are kept) |
| `float` | number | number |
| `bool` | boolean | any value: `nil` and `false` are false, everything else true (including `0`) |
| `string` | string | string or number |
| `name` | string | string or number. A name the engine doesn't know yet is added to its name table |
| object | object reference or `nil` | object reference or `nil`. Must be an instance of the property's class: `object X is not a Y` |
| `class<T>`, interface, component | object reference or `nil` | object reference or `nil` (not type-checked) |
| struct | [struct reference](objects.md#struct-references) | struct reference of the same type (copied), or a table of fields |
| `array<T>` | [array reference](objects.md#array-references) | Lua sequence or array reference (replaces the contents) |
| fixed array (`int X[4]`) | Lua table copy (1-based) | Lua table; `nil` entries leave the element unchanged |
| delegate | `{ object = obj, func = "Name" }` | the same shape, or `nil` to clear |
| `qword` | integer | integer |

Other property types read as `nil` and can't be written (`assigning <type> properties is not supported`).

## Function results

Function calls and hooks convert in the same way, with one difference: **structs and arrays returned by a function
call** (return value and `out` parameters) are plain Lua tables, since the engine's copy only lives during the
call. In hooks, `args` and `result` are references valid while the hook runs (see
[Hook arguments](objects.md#hook-arguments)).

## Zero values

Missing or `nil` function arguments, zero elements added with `arr:add()`, and the result of a blocked call
use the type's zero value:

| Type | Zero |
|---|---|
| numbers, enums | `0` |
| `bool` | `false` |
| `string`, `name` | `""` / `None` |
| object, class | `None` (`nil` in Lua) |
| struct | every field zero |
| array | empty |

## Structs from tables

Assigning a table to a struct writes the fields it names, converting each by its type; fields it doesn't name
keep their values. Nested structs and arrays take nested tables:

```lua
actor.Location = { X = 0, Y = 0, Z = 100 }
widget:Invoke("setTextColor", { { Type = h7.enum("ASType").AS_Number, n = 0x9be37a } })  -- array<ASValue>
```

A key that isn't a field raises `struct <Name> has no field '<key>'`. Keys that aren't strings are ignored.

## Delegates

A delegate value is the object and the name of the function to call:

```lua
widget:AddEventListener("CLIK_click", { object = cntl, func = "Options" })
```

Writing one checks that the object's class has a function called `func`
(`delegate target has no function '<func>'`). Lua functions can't be used as delegates; hook the target function
instead.

## Strings

Strings are converted between Lua (UTF-8 bytes as given) and the engine's UTF-16 `FString`s. Text read from the
game (localised names, descriptions) comes back as UTF-8.

## Integers and enums

Lua 5.4 distinguishes integers and floats. `int`, `byte` and enum values read as integers; `float` values read as
floats even when whole (`2.0`). Use `math.floor`/`math.tointeger` before writing a computed number to an `int`.

```

`re/scripts/pe_scan.py`:

```py
"""Quick static scan of the game exe: sections, anchor strings (ASCII + UTF-16), and UE3
native-function lookup tables ({"execFoo", &Class::execFoo} pairs)."""
import struct, sys, re

path = sys.argv[1]
data = open(path, 'rb').read()

pe = struct.unpack_from('<I', data, 0x3C)[0]
nsec = struct.unpack_from('<H', data, pe + 6)[0]
opt_size = struct.unpack_from('<H', data, pe + 20)[0]
opt = pe + 24
image_base = struct.unpack_from('<Q', data, opt + 24)[0]
secs = []
for i in range(nsec):
    o = opt + opt_size + i * 40
    name = data[o:o + 8].rstrip(b'\0').decode()
    vsize, va, rsize, raw = struct.unpack_from('<IIII', data, o + 8)
    secs.append((name, va, vsize, raw, rsize))
print('ImageBase', hex(image_base))
for s in secs:
    print('  %-8s va=%08x vsize=%08x raw=%08x rsize=%08x' % s)

def off2va(off):
    for n, va, vs, raw, rs in secs:
        if raw <= off < raw + rs:
            return image_base + va + off - raw
def va2off(v):
    rva = v - image_base
    for n, va, vs, raw, rs in secs:
        if va <= rva < va + rs:
            return raw + rva - va

anchors = sys.argv[2:] or []
for a in anchors:
    for enc, pat in (('A', a.encode()), ('W', a.encode('utf-16-le'))):
        for m in list(re.finditer(re.escape(pat), data))[:5]:
            # show surrounding string
            s = m.start()
            if enc == 'A':
                b = s
                while b > 0 and data[b - 1] != 0: b -= 1
                e = data.index(b'\0', s)
                txt = data[b:e].decode('latin1')
            else:
                b = s
                while b > 1 and data[b - 2:b] != b'\0\0': b -= 2
                e = s
                while data[e:e + 2] != b'\0\0': e += 2
                txt = data[b:e].decode('utf-16-le', 'replace')
            print(f'[{enc}] {a!r} va={off2va(b):#x}  {txt[:120]!r}')

# Native lookup tables: pointer to ASCII "exec..." immediately followed by code pointer
text = [s for s in secs if s[0] == '.text'][0]
tlo, thi = image_base + text[1], image_base + text[1] + text[2]
exec_strs = {}
for m in re.finditer(rb'\0(exec[A-Za-z0-9_]{2,80})\0', data):
    exec_strs[off2va(m.start(1))] = m.group(1).decode()
print('exec* ASCII strings:', len(exec_strs))
found = 0
rdata = [s for s in secs if s[0] in ('.rdata', '.data')]
for n, va, vs, raw, rs in rdata:
    for o in range(raw, raw + rs - 16, 8):
        p = struct.unpack_from('<Q', data, o)[0]
        if p in exec_strs:
            f = struct.unpack_from('<Q', data, o + 8)[0]
            if tlo <= f < thi:
                found += 1
                if found <= 15 or exec_strs[p] in ('execDynamicLoadObject', 'execFindObject', 'execGetName'):
                    print(f'  table@{off2va(o):#x} {exec_strs[p]} -> {f:#x}')
print('exec table entries:', found)

```

`re/swfinfo.py`:

```py
"""swfinfo.py <file.swf> [--strings] : tags, symbol classes, imports/exports and AS3 classes of a SWF/GFX file."""
import struct
import sys
import zlib


class R:
    def __init__(self, b, p=0):
        self.b, self.p = b, p

    def u8(self):
        v = self.b[self.p]; self.p += 1; return v

    def u16(self):
        v = struct.unpack_from('<H', self.b, self.p)[0]; self.p += 2; return v

    def u32(self):
        v = struct.unpack_from('<I', self.b, self.p)[0]; self.p += 4; return v

    def d64(self):
        v = struct.unpack_from('<d', self.b, self.p)[0]; self.p += 8; return v

    def cstr(self):
        e = self.b.index(0, self.p); s = self.b[self.p:e].decode('utf-8', 'replace'); self.p = e + 1; return s

    def u30(self):
        v = 0
        for i in range(5):
            c = self.u8(); v |= (c & 0x7f) << (7 * i)
            if not c & 0x80:
                break
        return v

    def s24(self):
        v = self.b[self.p] | self.b[self.p + 1] << 8 | self.b[self.p + 2] << 16; self.p += 3
        return v - (1 << 24) if v & 0x800000 else v


def tags(b, p, end):
    while p < end:
        code_len = struct.unpack_from('<H', b, p)[0]; p += 2
        code, ln = code_len >> 6, code_len & 0x3f
        if ln == 0x3f:
            ln = struct.unpack_from('<I', b, p)[0]; p += 4
        yield code, b[p:p + ln]
        p += ln


def parse_abc(d):
    r = R(d)
    r.u16(); r.u16()
    ints = [0] + [r.u30() for _ in range(max(r.u30() - 1, 0))]
    n = r.u30(); [r.u30() for _ in range(max(n - 1, 0))]
    n = r.u30(); [r.d64() for _ in range(max(n - 1, 0))]
    strs = ['']
    for _ in range(max(r.u30() - 1, 0)):
        ln = r.u30(); strs.append(d[r.p:r.p + ln].decode('utf-8', 'replace')); r.p += ln
    nss = [('', '')]
    for _ in range(max(r.u30() - 1, 0)):
        k = r.u8(); nss.append((k, strs[r.u30()]))
    nssets = [[]]
    for _ in range(max(r.u30() - 1, 0)):
        nssets.append([r.u30() for _ in range(r.u30())])
    mns = [None]
    for _ in range(max(r.u30() - 1, 0)):
        k = r.u8()
        if k in (0x07, 0x0D):
            ns, nm = r.u30(), r.u30(); mns.append((nss[ns][1], strs[nm]))
        elif k in (0x0F, 0x10):
            mns.append(('', strs[r.u30()]))
        elif k in (0x11, 0x12):
            mns.append(('', '*'))
        elif k in (0x09, 0x0E):
            nm, _ = r.u30(), r.u30(); mns.append(('', strs[nm]))
        elif k in (0x1B, 0x1C):
            r.u30(); mns.append(('', '[late]'))
        elif k == 0x1D:
            base = r.u30(); params = [r.u30() for _ in range(r.u30())]
            mns.append(('', 'Vector.<...>'))
        else:
            raise ValueError('multiname kind %x' % k)

    def mn(i):
        m = mns[i] if i < len(mns) else None
        return '*' if not m else (m[0] + '::' + m[1] if m[0] else m[1])

    methods = []
    for _ in range(r.u30()):
        pc = r.u30(); ret = r.u30(); params = [r.u30() for _ in range(pc)]
        name = r.u30(); flags = r.u8()
        if flags & 0x08:
            for _ in range(r.u30()):
                r.u30(); r.u8()
        if flags & 0x80:
            [r.u30() for _ in range(pc)]
        methods.append((params, ret))
    for _ in range(r.u30()):
        r.u30(); [(r.u30(), r.u30()) for _ in range(r.u30())]

    def traits():
        out = []
        for _ in range(r.u30()):
            name = r.u30(); kind = r.u8(); k = kind & 0x0f
            if k in (0, 6):
                r.u30(); tn = r.u30(); vi = r.u30()
                if vi:
                    r.u8()
                out.append(('var' if k == 0 else 'const', mn(name), mn(tn)))
            elif k in (1, 2, 3):
                r.u30(); m = r.u30()
                params, ret = methods[m]
                out.append(({1: 'function', 2: 'get', 3: 'set'}[k], mn(name),
                             '(' + ', '.join(mn(p) for p in params) + '):' + mn(ret)))
            elif k == 4:
                r.u30(); r.u30(); out.append(('class', mn(name), ''))
            elif k == 5:
                r.u30(); r.u30(); out.append(('function', mn(name), ''))
            if kind & 0x40:
                [r.u30() for _ in range(r.u30())]
        return out

    insts = []
    for _ in range(r.u30()):
        name = r.u30(); sup = r.u30(); fl = r.u8()
        if fl & 0x08:
            r.u30()
        ifaces = [r.u30() for _ in range(r.u30())]
        r.u30()
        insts.append((mn(name), mn(sup), traits()))
    # Class (static) traits, in the same order as the instances.
    for i in range(len(insts)):
        r.u30()  # cinit
        name, sup, tr = insts[i]
        insts[i] = (name, sup, tr + [('static ' + k, n, t) for k, n, t in traits()])
    return strs, insts


def main():
    path = sys.argv[1]
    b = open(path, 'rb').read()
    sig = b[:3]
    if sig in (b'CFX', b'CWS'):
        b = b[:8] + zlib.decompress(b[8:])
    nbits = b[8] >> 3
    p = 8 + (5 + 4 * nbits + 7) // 8 + 4
    show_strings = '--strings' in sys.argv
    counts = {}
    for code, d in tags(b, p, len(b)):
        counts[code] = counts.get(code, 0) + 1
        if code == 76:  # SymbolClass
            r = R(d)
            for _ in range(r.u16()):
                cid = r.u16(); print('symbol %5d %s' % (cid, r.cstr()))
        elif code in (56,):
            r = R(d)
            for _ in range(r.u16()):
                cid = r.u16(); print('export %5d %s' % (cid, r.cstr()))
        elif code in (57, 71):
            r = R(d); url = r.cstr()
            if code == 71:
                r.u16()
            for _ in range(r.u16()):
                cid = r.u16(); print('import %5d %s from %s' % (cid, r.cstr(), url))
        elif code == 82:  # DoABC
            r = R(d); r.u32(); nm = r.cstr()
            strs, insts = parse_abc(d[r.p:])
            for name, sup, tr in insts:
                print('\nclass %s extends %s' % (name, sup))
                for t in tr:
                    print('    %-8s %s %s' % t)
            if show_strings:
                print('\nstrings:', ' | '.join(s for s in strs if s))
    print('\ntag counts:', dict(sorted(counts.items())))


main()

```

`src/core/log.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "log.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <cstdio>
#include <mutex>

namespace h7 {

static FILE* g_file = nullptr;
static std::mutex g_mutex;

void LogInit(const std::wstring& path) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_file) fclose(g_file);
    // Fully shared: Ubisoft Connect starts the exe, which exits and is relaunched, so two processes
    // briefly overlap. The log can also be tailed while the game runs.
    g_file = _wfsopen(path.c_str(), L"w", _SH_DENYNO);
    if (!g_file) {
        std::wstring alt = path.substr(0, path.size() - 4) + L"_" + std::to_wstring(GetCurrentProcessId()) + L".log";
        g_file = _wfsopen(alt.c_str(), L"w", _SH_DENYNO);
    }
}

void LogRaw(const std::string& line) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    SYSTEMTIME t;
    GetLocalTime(&t);
    char stamp[32];
    snprintf(stamp, sizeof(stamp), "[%02d:%02d:%02d.%03d] ", t.wHour, t.wMinute, t.wSecond, t.wMilliseconds);
    if (g_file) {
        fputs(stamp, g_file);
        fputs(line.c_str(), g_file);
        fputc('\n', g_file);
        fflush(g_file);
    }
    OutputDebugStringA(("[H7ModEngine] " + line + "\n").c_str());
}

void Log(const char* fmt, ...) {
    char buf[4096];
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    vsnprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
    LogRaw(buf);
}

std::string HexDump(const void* data, size_t len, uintptr_t displayBase) {
    const unsigned char* p = static_cast<const unsigned char*>(data);
    std::string out;
    char line[160];
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i += 16) {
        int n = snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "  +%04zx %016llx: ", i, (unsigned long long)(displayBase + i));
        for (size_t j = 0; j < 16; ++j)
            n += snprintf(line + n, sizeof(line) - n, i + j < len ? "%02x " : "   ", i + j < len ? p[i + j] : 0);
        n += snprintf(line + n, sizeof(line) - n, " ");
        for (size_t j = 0; j < 16 && i + j < len; ++j)
            line[n++] = (p[i + j] >= 32 && p[i + j] < 127) ? (char)p[i + j] : '.';
        line[n++] = '\n';
        line[n] = 0;
        out += line;
    }
    return out;
}

} // namespace h7

```

`src/core/log.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <cstdarg>

namespace h7 {

void LogInit(const std::wstring& path);
void Log(const char* fmt, ...);
void LogRaw(const std::string& line);
std::string HexDump(const void* data, size_t len, uintptr_t displayBase);

} // namespace h7

`src/core/memory.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "memory.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <cstring>

namespace h7::mem {

ModuleRange g_game;

void InitGameModule() {
    auto base = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(GetModuleHandleW(nullptr));
    auto dos = reinterpret_cast<IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(base);
    auto nt = reinterpret_cast<IMAGE_NT_HEADERS64*>(base + dos->e_lfanew);
    g_game.base = base;
    g_game.end = base + nt->OptionalHeader.SizeOfImage;
}

bool Read(const void* src, void* dst, size_t n) {
    __try {
        memcpy(dst, src, n);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

bool Write(void* dst, const void* src, size_t n) {
    __try {
        memcpy(dst, src, n);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

void ForEachRegion(uintptr_t lo, uintptr_t hi, bool writableOnly,
                   const std::function<void(uintptr_t, size_t)>& fn) {
    const DWORD readable = PAGE_READONLY | PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ |
                           PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY;
    const DWORD writable = PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY;
    uintptr_t p = lo;
    MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION mbi;
    while (p < hi && VirtualQuery(reinterpret_cast<void*>(p), &mbi, sizeof(mbi))) {
        uintptr_t rbase = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(mbi.BaseAddress);
        uintptr_t rend = rbase + mbi.RegionSize;
        if (mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT && !(mbi.Protect & (PAGE_GUARD | PAGE_NOACCESS)) &&
            (mbi.Protect & readable) && (!writableOnly || (mbi.Protect & writable))) {
            uintptr_t s = rbase < lo ? lo : rbase;
            uintptr_t e = rend > hi ? hi : rend;
            if (e > s) fn(s, e - s);
        }
        p = rend;
    }
}

uintptr_t FunctionStart(uintptr_t pc) {
    DWORD64 imageBase = 0;
    const RUNTIME_FUNCTION* rf = RtlLookupFunctionEntry(pc, &imageBase, nullptr);
    if (!rf) return 0;
    // Chained unwind info points back to the primary entry; follow it to the real start.
    auto info = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(imageBase + rf->UnwindData);
    while (info[0] >> 3 & UNW_FLAG_CHAININFO) {
        uint8_t codes = info[2];
        auto chained = reinterpret_cast<const RUNTIME_FUNCTION*>(info + 4 + ((codes + 1) & ~1) * 2);
        rf = chained;
        info = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(imageBase + rf->UnwindData);
    }
    return imageBase + rf->BeginAddress;
}

} // namespace h7::mem

```

`src/core/memory.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <functional>

namespace h7::mem {

struct ModuleRange {
    uintptr_t base = 0;
    uintptr_t end = 0;
    bool Contains(uintptr_t p) const { return p >= base && p < end; }
};

// The game executable's image range (resolved at startup).
extern ModuleRange g_game;

void InitGameModule();

// SEH-protected copy; returns false if any byte was unreadable.
bool Read(const void* src, void* dst, size_t n);
bool Write(void* dst, const void* src, size_t n);

template <class T>
inline bool Read(uintptr_t addr, T& out) { return Read(reinterpret_cast<const void*>(addr), &out, sizeof(T)); }

template <class T>
inline T ReadOr(uintptr_t addr, T fallback) {
    T v;
    return Read(addr, v) ? v : fallback;
}

// Calls fn(regionBase, regionSize) for committed, readable regions inside [lo, hi).
// If writableOnly is set, only regions with write access are reported.
void ForEachRegion(uintptr_t lo, uintptr_t hi, bool writableOnly,
                   const std::function<void(uintptr_t, size_t)>& fn);

// Start address of the function containing `pc`, using the image's .pdata unwind table.
uintptr_t FunctionStart(uintptr_t pc);

} // namespace h7::mem

`src/engine/dllmain.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <windows.h>
#include "paths.h"
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include "../lua/runtime.h"
#include "../ue3/core.h"
#include "../ue3/dumper.h"
#include "../ue3/hooks.h"
#include "../ue3/reflection.h"
#include "../ue3/factory.h"

using namespace h7;

static bool WaitFor(const char* what, int timeoutMs, bool (*probe)()) {
    for (int waited = 0; waited < timeoutMs; waited += 250) {
        if (probe()) return true;
        Sleep(250);
    }
    Log("Timed out waiting for %s", what);
    return false;
}

static ue3::UObject g_viewport = 0;

static bool FindViewportClient() {
    ue3::UObject cls = ue3::FindClass("Engine.GameViewportClient");
    if (!cls || !ue3::IsCalibrated()) return false;
    for (int32_t i = 0, n = ue3::ObjectCount(); i < n; ++i) {
        ue3::UObject o = ue3::ObjectAt(i);
        if (o && ue3::IsA(o, cls) && ue3::ObjName(o).rfind("Default__", 0) != 0) {
            g_viewport = o;
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

// The engine heartbeat: GameViewportClient.PostRender(Canvas) runs every drawn frame on the game thread.
// (GameViewportClient.Tick can't be used: it is an empty probe event, so ProcessEvent never calls it.)
static bool InstallTickHook() {
    ue3::UObject fn = ue3::FindField(ue3::ObjClass(g_viewport), "PostRender");
    if (!fn) return false;
    int id = hooks::Add(fn, nullptr, [](hooks::CallContext& c) {
        static LARGE_INTEGER freq{}, last{};
        LARGE_INTEGER now;
        QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
        if (!freq.QuadPart) QueryPerformanceFrequency(&freq);
        float dt = last.QuadPart ? float(double(now.QuadPart - last.QuadPart) / double(freq.QuadPart)) : 1.0f / 60.0f;
        last = now;
        if (dt < 0 || dt > 1.0f) dt = 1.0f / 60.0f; // after loading screens
        hooks::Maintain();
        lua::OnGameTick(dt, c.self);
    }, /*viaFunc*/ true); // installed from this thread; the engine calls PostRender from C++ anyway
    Log("Tick hook on %s: %s", ue3::ObjFullName(fn).c_str(), id ? "ok" : "FAILED");
    return id != 0;
}

static DWORD WINAPI InitThread(LPVOID) {
    for (auto dir : {L"", L"logs", L"dumps", L"mods", L"lib", L"exec", L"data"})
        CreateDirectoryW((*dir ? EnginePath(dir) : EngineRoot()).c_str(), nullptr);
    LogInit(EnginePath(L"logs\\engine.log"));
    mem::InitGameModule();
    Log("H7ModEngine starting; game image %p-%p", (void*)mem::g_game.base, (void*)mem::g_game.end);

    // The executable is packed: wait until the engine has unpacked and built its name/object tables.
    if (!WaitFor("GNames", 300000, [] { return ue3::FindGNames(); })) return 0;
    if (!WaitFor("GObjects", 300000, [] { return ue3::FindGObjects(); })) return 0;
    // Natively registered classes appear first; wait for script packages (functions/properties) to load.
    if (!WaitFor("script packages", 300000, [] {
            ue3::InvalidateLookupCache();
            return ue3::FindObject("Function Engine.Actor.Touch") != 0 && ue3::FindObject("Class MMH7Game.H7Creature") != 0;
        }))
        return 0;
    Log("Script loaded: %d names, %d objects", ue3::NameCount(), ue3::ObjectCount());
    if (!ue3::Calibrate()) {
        ue3::DumpProbe(EnginePath(L"dumps"));
        Log("Engine layout calibration failed; mods disabled. See dumps\\probe.txt");
        return 0;
    }
    if (ConfigInt(L"Dev", L"DumpProbe", 0)) ue3::DumpProbe(EnginePath(L"dumps"));
    if (ConfigInt(L"Dev", L"DumpImage", 0)) ue3::DumpImage(EnginePath(L"dumps\\MMH7Game_unpacked.exe"));

    if (!WaitFor("GameViewportClient", 300000, FindViewportClient)) return 0;
    Sleep(2000); // let the remaining startup packages finish linking
    if (!ue3::CalibrateFunctionPointers()) {
        Log("Could not identify UObject::ProcessInternal; mods disabled");
        return 0;
    }
    ue3::InitFactory(); // optional: h7.new_object / h7.clone stay unavailable if this fails
    if (!InstallTickHook()) return 0;
    if (ConfigInt(L"Dev", L"DumpSDK", 0)) ue3::DumpNamesAndObjects(EnginePath(L"dumps"));
    Log("Init complete; Lua starts on the next frame");
    return 0;
}

BOOL WINAPI DllMain(HINSTANCE inst, DWORD reason, LPVOID) {
    if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH) {
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(inst);
        if (HANDLE t = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, InitThread, nullptr, 0, nullptr)) CloseHandle(t);
    }
    return TRUE;
}

```

`src/engine/paths.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "paths.h"
#include <windows.h>

namespace h7 {

const std::wstring& EngineRoot() {
    static std::wstring root = [] {
        wchar_t exe[MAX_PATH];
        GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, exe, MAX_PATH);
        std::wstring p = exe;
        // ...\Binaries\Win64\MMH7Game-Win64-Shipping.exe -> game root is three levels up.
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) p = p.substr(0, p.find_last_of(L"\\/"));
        return p + L"\\H7ModEngine";
    }();
    return root;
}

std::wstring EnginePath(const std::wstring& rel) { return EngineRoot() + L"\\" + rel; }

int ConfigInt(const wchar_t* section, const wchar_t* key, int fallback) {
    return (int)GetPrivateProfileIntW(section, key, fallback, EnginePath(L"H7ModEngine.ini").c_str());
}

std::string Narrow(const std::wstring& w) {
    if (w.empty()) return {};
    int n = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, w.data(), (int)w.size(), nullptr, 0, nullptr, nullptr);
    std::string s(n, '\0');
    WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, w.data(), (int)w.size(), s.data(), n, nullptr, nullptr);
    return s;
}

std::wstring Widen(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.empty()) return {};
    int n = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.data(), (int)s.size(), nullptr, 0);
    std::wstring w(n, L'\0');
    MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.data(), (int)s.size(), w.data(), n);
    return w;
}

} // namespace h7

```

`src/lua/bindings.cpp`:

```cpp
// Lua <-> UnrealScript bindings: object/struct/array proxies, value marshaling, function calls, hooks.
#include "runtime.h"
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include "../ue3/core.h"
#include "../ue3/factory.h"
#include "../ue3/hooks.h"
#include "../ue3/reflection.h"
#include "../engine/paths.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <lua.h>
#include <lauxlib.h>
#include <lualib.h>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>

namespace h7::lua {

using ue3::PropInfo;
using ue3::PropKind;
using ue3::UObject;

static const char* kObj = "H7Object";
static const char* kStruct = "H7Struct";
static const char* kArray = "H7Array";

struct ObjRef { UObject obj; };
struct StructRef { uintptr_t addr; UObject type; uint64_t frame; uintptr_t outParms = 0; }; // outParms: hook args
struct ArrayRef { uintptr_t arr; UObject inner; uint64_t frame; };

// Proxies into hook parameter storage are only valid while the hook runs.
static std::unordered_set<uint64_t> g_liveFrames;
static uint64_t g_nextFrame = 1;
static bool FrameAlive(uint64_t f) { return f == 0 || g_liveFrames.count(f) != 0; }

using ue3::EngineRealloc;

static std::wstring ToWide(const char* s, size_t len) { return Widen(std::string(s, len)); }

// Writes a UTF-8 string into an FString (TArray<TCHAR>) using the engine allocator.
static bool AssignFString(uintptr_t at, const char* s, size_t len) {
    std::wstring w = ToWide(s, len);
    ue3::TArrayRaw a{};
    if (!mem::Read(at, a)) return false;
    int32_t num = (int32_t)w.size() + 1;
    void* data = EngineRealloc(reinterpret_cast<void*>(a.data), num * sizeof(wchar_t));
    if (!data) return false;
    memcpy(data, w.c_str(), num * sizeof(wchar_t));
    a = {reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(data), num, num};
    return mem::Write(reinterpret_cast<void*>(at), &a, sizeof(a));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Field lookup cache: (struct, name) -> field
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static std::unordered_map<UObject, std::unordered_map<std::string, UObject>> g_fieldCache;

static UObject LookupField(UObject s, const char* name) {
    auto& m = g_fieldCache[s];
    auto it = m.find(name);
    if (it != m.end()) return it->second;
    UObject f = ue3::FindField(s, name);
    m.emplace(name, f);
    return f;
}

static bool IsFunction(UObject field) { return ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(field)) == "Function"; }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Push / write values
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

void PushObject(lua_State* L, UObject o) {
    if (!o) {
        lua_pushnil(L);
        return;
    }
    auto* r = static_cast<ObjRef*>(lua_newuserdatauv(L, sizeof(ObjRef), 0));
    r->obj = o;
    luaL_setmetatable(L, kObj);
}

static void PushStruct(lua_State* L, uintptr_t addr, UObject type, uint64_t frame) {
    auto* r = static_cast<StructRef*>(lua_newuserdatauv(L, sizeof(StructRef), 0));
    *r = {addr, type, frame};
    luaL_setmetatable(L, kStruct);
}

static void PushArray(lua_State* L, uintptr_t arr, UObject inner, uint64_t frame) {
    auto* r = static_cast<ArrayRef*>(lua_newuserdatauv(L, sizeof(ArrayRef), 0));
    *r = {arr, inner, frame};
    luaL_setmetatable(L, kArray);
}

static UObject ToObject(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    if (lua_isnoneornil(L, idx)) return 0;
    auto* r = static_cast<ObjRef*>(luaL_testudata(L, idx, kObj));
    if (!r) luaL_error(L, "expected an engine object, got %s", luaL_typename(L, idx));
    return r->obj;
}

static UObject CheckValidObject(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    auto* r = static_cast<ObjRef*>(luaL_checkudata(L, idx, kObj));
    if (!ue3::IsValidObject(r->obj)) luaL_error(L, "engine object %p is no longer valid", (void*)r->obj);
    return r->obj;
}

static void PushElem(lua_State* L, uintptr_t addr, UObject prop, PropKind kind, uint64_t frame) {
    switch (kind) {
    case PropKind::Byte: lua_pushinteger(L, mem::ReadOr<uint8_t>(addr, 0)); break;
    case PropKind::Int: lua_pushinteger(L, mem::ReadOr<int32_t>(addr, 0)); break;
    case PropKind::QWord: lua_pushinteger(L, mem::ReadOr<int64_t>(addr, 0)); break;
    case PropKind::Float: lua_pushnumber(L, mem::ReadOr<float>(addr, 0)); break;
    case PropKind::Bool: lua_pushboolean(L, (mem::ReadOr<uint32_t>(addr, 0) & ue3::BoolMask(prop)) != 0); break;
    case PropKind::Object:
    case PropKind::Class:
    case PropKind::Component:
    case PropKind::Interface: PushObject(L, mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(addr, 0)); break;
    case PropKind::Name: lua_pushstring(L, ue3::NameToString(mem::ReadOr<ue3::FName>(addr, {0, 0})).c_str()); break;
    case PropKind::Str: lua_pushstring(L, ue3::ReadFString(addr).c_str()); break;
    case PropKind::Struct: PushStruct(L, addr, ue3::PropInner(prop), frame); break;
    case PropKind::Array: PushArray(L, addr, ue3::PropInner(prop), frame); break;
    case PropKind::Delegate:
        lua_createtable(L, 0, 2);
        PushObject(L, mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(addr, 0));
        lua_setfield(L, -2, "object");
        lua_pushstring(L, ue3::NameToString(mem::ReadOr<ue3::FName>(addr + 8, {0, 0})).c_str());
        lua_setfield(L, -2, "func");
        break;
    default: lua_pushnil(L); break;
    }
}

static void PushProp(lua_State* L, uintptr_t base, UObject prop, uint64_t frame) {
    PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(prop);
    if (p.arrayDim > 1) {
        lua_createtable(L, p.arrayDim, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < p.arrayDim; ++i) {
            PushElem(L, base + p.offset + i * p.elementSize, prop, p.kind, frame);
            lua_rawseti(L, -2, i + 1);
        }
        return;
    }
    PushElem(L, base + p.offset, prop, p.kind, frame);
}

static void WriteStructFromTable(lua_State* L, int idx, uintptr_t addr, UObject type);
static int H_to_table(lua_State* L);

// Frees an FString's or a dynamic array's buffer (and the strings in an array of strings) and empties it.
static void FreeOwned(uintptr_t addr, PropKind kind, UObject prop) {
    auto a = mem::ReadOr<ue3::TArrayRaw>(addr, {});
    if (!a.data) return;
    if (kind == PropKind::Array) {
        PropInfo ip = ue3::Describe(ue3::PropInner(prop));
        if (ip.kind == PropKind::Str)
            for (int32_t i = 0; i < a.num; ++i) FreeOwned(a.data + (uintptr_t)i * ip.elementSize, PropKind::Str, 0);
    } else if (kind != PropKind::Str) {
        return;
    }
    EngineRealloc(reinterpret_cast<void*>(a.data), 0);
    a = {};
    mem::Write(reinterpret_cast<void*>(addr), &a, sizeof(a));
}

static void WriteElem(lua_State* L, int idx, uintptr_t addr, UObject prop, PropKind kind) {
    idx = lua_absindex(L, idx);
    switch (kind) {
    case PropKind::Byte: { uint8_t v = (uint8_t)luaL_checkinteger(L, idx); mem::Write((void*)addr, &v, 1); break; }
    case PropKind::Int: { int32_t v = (int32_t)luaL_checkinteger(L, idx); mem::Write((void*)addr, &v, 4); break; }
    case PropKind::QWord: { int64_t v = luaL_checkinteger(L, idx); mem::Write((void*)addr, &v, 8); break; }
    case PropKind::Float: { float v = (float)luaL_checknumber(L, idx); mem::Write((void*)addr, &v, 4); break; }
    case PropKind::Bool: {
        uint32_t mask = ue3::BoolMask(prop), v = mem::ReadOr<uint32_t>(addr, 0);
        v = lua_toboolean(L, idx) ? (v | mask) : (v & ~mask);
        mem::Write((void*)addr, &v, 4);
        break;
    }
    case PropKind::Object:
    case PropKind::Class:
    case PropKind::Component:
    case PropKind::Interface: {
        UObject o = ToObject(L, idx);
        UObject want = ue3::PropInner(prop);
        if (o && want && kind == PropKind::Object && !ue3::IsA(o, want))
            luaL_error(L, "object %s is not a %s", ue3::ObjFullName(o).c_str(), ue3::ObjName(want).c_str());
        mem::Write((void*)addr, &o, 8);
        break;
    }
    case PropKind::Name: {
        const char* s = luaL_checkstring(L, idx);
        int32_t n = ue3::FindNameIndex(s);
        ue3::FName fn{n, 0};
        if (n < 0) fn = ue3::MakeName(s); // adds the name (needs the object factory)
        if (fn.index < 0) luaL_error(L, "name '%s' does not exist in the engine name table", s);
        mem::Write((void*)addr, &fn, 8);
        break;
    }
    case PropKind::Str: {
        size_t len;
        const char* s = luaL_checklstring(L, idx, &len);
        if (!AssignFString(addr, s, len)) luaL_error(L, "could not assign string");
        break;
    }
    case PropKind::Struct: {
        UObject type = ue3::PropInner(prop);
        if (auto* r = static_cast<StructRef*>(luaL_testudata(L, idx, kStruct))) {
            if (r->type != type) luaL_error(L, "struct type mismatch");
            std::vector<uint8_t> tmp(ue3::StructSize(type));
            mem::Read((void*)r->addr, tmp.data(), tmp.size());
            mem::Write((void*)addr, tmp.data(), tmp.size());
        } else {
            luaL_checktype(L, idx, LUA_TTABLE);
            WriteStructFromTable(L, idx, addr, type);
        }
        break;
    }
    case PropKind::Delegate: {
        // {object = obj, func = "FunctionName"} (or nil to clear): an FScriptDelegate {UObject*, FName}.
        struct { UObject object; ue3::FName func; } d{0, {0, 0}};
        if (!lua_isnil(L, idx)) {
            luaL_checktype(L, idx, LUA_TTABLE);
            lua_getfield(L, idx, "object");
            d.object = ToObject(L, -1);
            lua_getfield(L, idx, "func");
            const char* fn = luaL_checkstring(L, -1);
            if (!d.object || !LookupField(ue3::ObjClass(d.object), fn))
                luaL_error(L, "delegate target has no function '%s'", fn);
            d.func = {ue3::FindNameIndex(fn), 0};
            lua_pop(L, 2);
        }
        mem::Write((void*)addr, &d, sizeof(d));
        break;
    }
    case PropKind::Array: {
        // A Lua sequence (or another engine array) replaces the array's contents.
        UObject inner = ue3::PropInner(prop);
        PropInfo ip = ue3::Describe(inner);
        int src = idx;
        if (luaL_testudata(L, idx, kArray)) {
            lua_pushcfunction(L, H_to_table);
            lua_pushvalue(L, idx);
            lua_call(L, 1, 1);
            src = lua_gettop(L);
        } else {
            luaL_checktype(L, idx, LUA_TTABLE);
        }
        int32_t n = (int32_t)lua_rawlen(L, src);
        FreeOwned(addr, PropKind::Array, prop);
        ue3::TArrayRaw a{};
        if (n > 0) {
            void* data = EngineRealloc(nullptr, (size_t)n * ip.elementSize);
            if (!data) luaL_error(L, "allocation failed");
            memset(data, 0, (size_t)n * ip.elementSize);
            a = {reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(data), n, n};
        }
        mem::Write((void*)addr, &a, sizeof(a));
        for (int32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            lua_rawgeti(L, src, i + 1);
            if (!lua_isnil(L, -1)) WriteElem(L, -1, a.data + (uintptr_t)i * ip.elementSize, inner, ip.kind);
            lua_pop(L, 1);
        }
        if (src != idx) lua_pop(L, 1);
        break;
    }
    default: luaL_error(L, "assigning %s properties is not supported", ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(prop)).c_str());
    }
}

static void WriteProp(lua_State* L, int idx, uintptr_t base, UObject prop) {
    PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(prop);
    if (p.arrayDim > 1) {
        luaL_checktype(L, idx, LUA_TTABLE);
        for (int i = 0; i < p.arrayDim; ++i) {
            lua_rawgeti(L, idx, i + 1);
            if (!lua_isnil(L, -1)) WriteElem(L, -1, base + p.offset + i * p.elementSize, prop, p.kind);
            lua_pop(L, 1);
        }
        return;
    }
    WriteElem(L, idx, base + p.offset, prop, p.kind);
}

static void WriteStructFromTable(lua_State* L, int idx, uintptr_t addr, UObject type) {
    idx = lua_absindex(L, idx);
    lua_pushnil(L);
    while (lua_next(L, idx)) {
        if (lua_type(L, -2) == LUA_TSTRING) {
            const char* key = lua_tostring(L, -2);
            UObject f = LookupField(type, key);
            if (!f || IsFunction(f)) luaL_error(L, "struct %s has no field '%s'", ue3::ObjName(type).c_str(), key);
            WriteProp(L, -1, addr, f);
        }
        lua_pop(L, 1);
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Calling UFunctions
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static int CallUFunction(lua_State* L) {
    UObject fn = reinterpret_cast<UObject>(lua_touserdata(L, lua_upvalueindex(1)));
    UObject self = CheckValidObject(L, 1);
    if (!hooks::HasProcessEvent()) luaL_error(L, "function calls are not available yet (ProcessEvent not discovered)");
    std::vector<PropInfo> params = ue3::FuncParams(fn);
    std::vector<uint8_t> buf(ue3::FuncParmsSize(fn) + 64, 0);
    uintptr_t base = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(buf.data());
    int arg = 2;
    for (auto& p : params) {
        if (p.flags & ue3::CPF_ReturnParm) continue;
        if (!lua_isnoneornil(L, arg)) WriteProp(L, arg, base, p.prop);
        ++arg;
    }
    hooks::ProcessEvent(self, fn, buf.data());
    int nret = 0;
    for (auto& p : params)
        if (p.flags & ue3::CPF_ReturnParm) PushProp(L, base, p.prop, 0), ++nret;
    // Out parameters follow the return value. Struct/array results are copied to tables since the buffer dies here.
    for (auto& p : params) {
        if (!(p.flags & ue3::CPF_OutParm) || (p.flags & ue3::CPF_ReturnParm)) continue;
        PushProp(L, base, p.prop, 0);
        ++nret;
    }
    // Struct/array return values point into `buf`; convert them to plain tables.
    for (int i = lua_gettop(L) - nret + 1; i <= lua_gettop(L); ++i) {
        if (luaL_testudata(L, i, kStruct) || luaL_testudata(L, i, kArray)) {
            lua_getglobal(L, "h7");
            lua_getfield(L, -1, "to_table");
            lua_pushvalue(L, i);
            lua_call(L, 1, 1);
            lua_replace(L, i);
            lua_pop(L, 1);
        }
    }
    // The caller owns the strings and arrays in the parameter buffer (ProcessEvent copies parameters back).
    for (auto& p : params)
        for (int i = 0; i < p.arrayDim; ++i) FreeOwned(base + p.offset + i * p.elementSize, p.kind, p.prop);
    return nret;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// H7Object metatable
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static int Obj_index(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = static_cast<ObjRef*>(luaL_checkudata(L, 1, kObj));
    const char* key = luaL_checkstring(L, 2);
    // Helper methods (snake_case, so they never collide with UnrealScript members).
    lua_getfield(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, "H7ObjectMethods");
    lua_getfield(L, -1, key);
    if (!lua_isnil(L, -1)) return 1;
    lua_pop(L, 2);
    if (!ue3::IsValidObject(r->obj)) luaL_error(L, "engine object %p is no longer valid", (void*)r->obj);
    UObject f = LookupField(ue3::ObjClass(r->obj), key);
    if (!f) return 0;
    if (IsFunction(f)) {
        lua_pushlightuserdata(L, reinterpret_cast<void*>(f));
        lua_pushcclosure(L, CallUFunction, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    PushProp(L, r->obj, f, 0);
    return 1;
}

static int Obj_newindex(lua_State* L) {
    UObject o = CheckValidObject(L, 1);
    const char* key = luaL_checkstring(L, 2);
    UObject f = LookupField(ue3::ObjClass(o), key);
    if (!f || IsFunction(f)) luaL_error(L, "%s has no property '%s'", ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(o)).c_str(), key);
    WriteProp(L, 3, o, f);
    return 0;
}

static int Obj_eq(lua_State* L) {
    auto* a = static_cast<ObjRef*>(luaL_testudata(L, 1, kObj));
    auto* b = static_cast<ObjRef*>(luaL_testudata(L, 2, kObj));
    lua_pushboolean(L, a && b && a->obj == b->obj);
    return 1;
}

static int Obj_tostring(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = static_cast<ObjRef*>(luaL_checkudata(L, 1, kObj));
    std::string s = ue3::IsValidObject(r->obj) ? ue3::ObjFullName(r->obj) : "<invalid object>";
    lua_pushstring(L, s.c_str());
    return 1;
}

static int M_get_name(lua_State* L) { lua_pushstring(L, ue3::ObjName(CheckValidObject(L, 1)).c_str()); return 1; }
static int M_get_full_name(lua_State* L) { lua_pushstring(L, ue3::ObjFullName(CheckValidObject(L, 1)).c_str()); return 1; }
static int M_get_path_name(lua_State* L) { lua_pushstring(L, ue3::ObjPathName(CheckValidObject(L, 1)).c_str()); return 1; }
static int M_get_class(lua_State* L) { PushObject(L, ue3::ObjClass(CheckValidObject(L, 1))); return 1; }
static int M_get_outer(lua_State* L) { PushObject(L, ue3::ObjOuter(CheckValidObject(L, 1))); return 1; }
static int M_get_address(lua_State* L) { lua_pushinteger(L, (lua_Integer)CheckValidObject(L, 1)); return 1; }
static int M_get_default(lua_State* L) { PushObject(L, ue3::ClassDefaultObject(CheckValidObject(L, 1))); return 1; }
static int M_get_super(lua_State* L) { PushObject(L, ue3::StructSuper(CheckValidObject(L, 1))); return 1; }

static int M_is_valid(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = static_cast<ObjRef*>(luaL_checkudata(L, 1, kObj));
    lua_pushboolean(L, ue3::IsValidObject(r->obj));
    return 1;
}

static UObject ClassArg(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    if (lua_type(L, idx) == LUA_TSTRING) {
        UObject c = ue3::FindClass(lua_tostring(L, idx));
        if (!c) luaL_error(L, "unknown class '%s'", lua_tostring(L, idx));
        return c;
    }
    return CheckValidObject(L, idx);
}

static int M_is_a(lua_State* L) {
    UObject o = CheckValidObject(L, 1);
    lua_pushboolean(L, ue3::IsA(o, ClassArg(L, 2)));
    return 1;
}

// obj:call("FuncName", ...) — explicit form, useful when a property shadows a function name.
static int M_call(lua_State* L) {
    UObject o = CheckValidObject(L, 1);
    const char* name = luaL_checkstring(L, 2);
    UObject f = LookupField(ue3::ObjClass(o), name);
    if (!f || !IsFunction(f)) luaL_error(L, "%s has no function '%s'", ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(o)).c_str(), name);
    lua_remove(L, 2);
    lua_pushlightuserdata(L, reinterpret_cast<void*>(f));
    lua_pushcclosure(L, CallUFunction, 1);
    lua_insert(L, 1);
    lua_call(L, lua_gettop(L) - 1, LUA_MULTRET);
    return lua_gettop(L);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// H7Struct / H7Array metatables
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static StructRef* CheckStruct(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    auto* r = static_cast<StructRef*>(luaL_checkudata(L, idx, kStruct));
    if (!FrameAlive(r->frame)) luaL_error(L, "this value referred to hook parameters and is no longer valid");
    return r;
}

// Where field f of r is stored. For hook args, out parameters of script-to-script calls live at the caller.
static uintptr_t FieldBase(const StructRef* r, UObject f) {
    if (!r->outParms) return r->addr;
    PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(f);
    if (!(p.flags & ue3::CPF_OutParm)) return r->addr;
    hooks::CallContext c{};
    c.outParms = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(r->outParms);
    uint8_t* at = hooks::OutParmAddr(c, f);
    return at ? reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(at) - p.offset : r->addr;
}

static int Struct_index(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = CheckStruct(L, 1);
    const char* key = luaL_checkstring(L, 2);
    UObject f = LookupField(r->type, key);
    if (!f || IsFunction(f)) return 0;
    PushProp(L, FieldBase(r, f), f, r->frame);
    return 1;
}

static int Struct_newindex(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = CheckStruct(L, 1);
    const char* key = luaL_checkstring(L, 2);
    UObject f = LookupField(r->type, key);
    if (!f || IsFunction(f)) luaL_error(L, "%s has no field '%s'", ue3::ObjName(r->type).c_str(), key);
    WriteProp(L, 3, FieldBase(r, f), f);
    return 0;
}

static int Struct_tostring(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = static_cast<StructRef*>(luaL_checkudata(L, 1, kStruct));
    lua_pushfstring(L, "%s: %p", ue3::ObjName(r->type).c_str(), (void*)r->addr);
    return 1;
}

static ArrayRef* CheckArray(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    auto* r = static_cast<ArrayRef*>(luaL_checkudata(L, idx, kArray));
    if (!FrameAlive(r->frame)) luaL_error(L, "this value referred to hook parameters and is no longer valid");
    return r;
}

static int Array_len(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = CheckArray(L, 1);
    lua_pushinteger(L, mem::ReadOr<ue3::TArrayRaw>(r->arr, {}).num);
    return 1;
}

static int Array_add(lua_State* L);
static int Array_remove(lua_State* L);

static int Array_index(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = CheckArray(L, 1);
    if (lua_type(L, 2) == LUA_TSTRING) {
        const char* k = lua_tostring(L, 2);
        if (!strcmp(k, "add")) return lua_pushcfunction(L, Array_add), 1;
        if (!strcmp(k, "remove")) return lua_pushcfunction(L, Array_remove), 1;
        return 0;
    }
    lua_Integer i = luaL_checkinteger(L, 2);
    auto a = mem::ReadOr<ue3::TArrayRaw>(r->arr, {});
    if (i < 1 || i > a.num) return 0;
    PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(r->inner);
    PushElem(L, a.data + (i - 1) * p.elementSize, r->inner, p.kind, r->frame);
    return 1;
}

static int Array_newindex(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = CheckArray(L, 1);
    lua_Integer i = luaL_checkinteger(L, 2);
    auto a = mem::ReadOr<ue3::TArrayRaw>(r->arr, {});
    if (i < 1 || i > a.num) luaL_error(L, "array index %d out of range (1..%d); use :add()", (int)i, a.num);
    PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(r->inner);
    WriteElem(L, 3, a.data + (i - 1) * p.elementSize, r->inner, p.kind);
    return 0;
}

// arr:add(value) — append using the engine allocator.
static int Array_add(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = CheckArray(L, 1);
    PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(r->inner);
    auto a = mem::ReadOr<ue3::TArrayRaw>(r->arr, {});
    if (a.num + 1 > a.max) {
        int32_t newMax = a.max < 4 ? 4 : a.max * 2;
        void* data = EngineRealloc(reinterpret_cast<void*>(a.data), (size_t)newMax * p.elementSize);
        if (!data) luaL_error(L, "allocation failed");
        a.data = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(data);
        a.max = newMax;
    }
    uintptr_t elem = a.data + (uintptr_t)a.num * p.elementSize;
    memset(reinterpret_cast<void*>(elem), 0, p.elementSize);
    ++a.num;
    mem::Write(reinterpret_cast<void*>(r->arr), &a, sizeof(a));
    if (!lua_isnoneornil(L, 2)) WriteElem(L, 2, elem, r->inner, p.kind);
    lua_pushinteger(L, a.num);
    return 1;
}

// arr:remove(i) — removes element i (1-based), shifting the rest down. Strings in the element are freed.
static int Array_remove(lua_State* L) {
    auto* r = CheckArray(L, 1);
    lua_Integer i = luaL_checkinteger(L, 2);
    PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(r->inner);
    auto a = mem::ReadOr<ue3::TArrayRaw>(r->arr, {});
    if (i < 1 || i > a.num) luaL_error(L, "array index out of range");
    uintptr_t elem = a.data + (uintptr_t)(i - 1) * p.elementSize;
    if (p.kind == PropKind::Str) EngineRealloc(reinterpret_cast<void*>(mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(elem, 0)), 0);
    memmove(reinterpret_cast<void*>(elem), reinterpret_cast<void*>(elem + p.elementSize), (size_t)(a.num - i) * p.elementSize);
    --a.num;
    mem::Write(reinterpret_cast<void*>(r->arr), &a, sizeof(a));
    return 0;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// h7.* functions
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static std::string ArgsToString(lua_State* L, int from) {
    std::string out;
    int n = lua_gettop(L);
    for (int i = from; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (i > from) out += "\t";
        out += luaL_tolstring(L, i, nullptr);
        lua_pop(L, 1);
    }
    return out;
}

static int LogAt(lua_State* L, const char* level) {
    int id = CurrentModId();
    std::string prefix = id > 0 ? "[" + ModName(id) + "] " : "[lua] ";
    Log("%s%s%s", level, prefix.c_str(), ArgsToString(L, 1).c_str());
    return 0;
}
static int H_log(lua_State* L) { return LogAt(L, ""); }
static int H_warn(lua_State* L) { return LogAt(L, "WARNING "); }
static int H_error(lua_State* L) { return LogAt(L, "ERROR "); }

static int H_find(lua_State* L) {
    PushObject(L, ue3::FindObject(luaL_checkstring(L, 1)));
    return 1;
}

static int H_find_class(lua_State* L) {
    PushObject(L, ue3::FindClass(luaL_checkstring(L, 1)));
    return 1;
}

// Class defaults (Default__X) and archetypes (templates in content packages, e.g. H7Config.H7AdventureSetup)
// are instances of their class too, but never the object a mod is looking for.
constexpr uint64_t RF_ClassDefaultObject = 0x200, RF_ArchetypeObject = 0x400;
static bool IsDefaultObject(UObject o) {
    uint64_t flags = mem::ReadOr<uint64_t>(o + ue3::L.objFlags, 0);
    return (flags & (RF_ClassDefaultObject | RF_ArchetypeObject)) || ue3::ObjName(o).rfind("Default__", 0) == 0;
}

// h7.find_all(class [, include_defaults]) -> array of live instances of class (or subclasses); with
// include_defaults, class defaults and archetypes are listed too
static int H_find_all(lua_State* L) {
    UObject cls = ClassArg(L, 1);
    bool defaults = lua_toboolean(L, 2);
    lua_newtable(L);
    int n = 0;
    int32_t count = ue3::ObjectCount();
    for (int32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        UObject o = ue3::ObjectAt(i);
        if (!o || !ue3::IsA(o, cls) || (!defaults && IsDefaultObject(o))) continue;
        PushObject(L, o);
        lua_rawseti(L, -2, ++n);
    }
    return 1;
}

static int H_first(lua_State* L) {
    UObject cls = ClassArg(L, 1);
    int32_t count = ue3::ObjectCount();
    for (int32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        UObject o = ue3::ObjectAt(i);
        if (o && ue3::IsA(o, cls) && !IsDefaultObject(o)) return PushObject(L, o), 1;
    }
    lua_pushnil(L);
    return 1;
}


// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Creating objects
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static constexpr uint64_t kKeepFlags = ue3::RF_Public | ue3::RF_Standalone | ue3::RF_ArchetypeObject;

static uint64_t ObjFlags(UObject o) { return mem::ReadOr<uint64_t>(o + ue3::L.objFlags, 0); }

// The object called `name` directly inside `outer` (any class), or 0.
static UObject FindInOuter(UObject outer, const std::string& name) {
    int32_t idx = ue3::FindNameIndex(name);
    if (idx < 0) return 0;
    for (int32_t i = 0, n = ue3::ObjectCount(); i < n; ++i) {
        UObject o = ue3::ObjectAt(i);
        if (o && ue3::ObjOuter(o) == outer && ue3::ObjFName(o).index == idx && ue3::ObjFName(o).number == 0) return o;
    }
    return 0;
}

static UObject ConstructOrError(lua_State* L, UObject cls, UObject outer, const std::string& name, uint64_t flags,
                                UObject tmpl) {
    std::string err;
    UObject o = ue3::ConstructObject(cls, outer, name, flags, tmpl, err);
    if (!o) luaL_error(L, "could not create %s '%s': %s", ue3::ObjName(cls).c_str(), name.c_str(), err.c_str());
    return o;
}

// h7.new_object(class, {name=, outer=, template=, flags=, archetype=, replace=}) -> object
// Fails if `outer` already holds an object with that name, unless replace = true (the engine then reconstructs
// it in place: same address, properties reset).
static int H_new_object(lua_State* L) {
    UObject cls = ClassArg(L, 1);
    std::string name;
    UObject outer = 0, tmpl = 0;
    uint64_t flags = ue3::RF_Public | ue3::RF_Standalone;
    bool replace = false;
    if (!lua_isnoneornil(L, 2)) {
        luaL_checktype(L, 2, LUA_TTABLE);
        if (lua_getfield(L, 2, "name") != LUA_TNIL) name = luaL_checkstring(L, -1);
        if (lua_getfield(L, 2, "outer") != LUA_TNIL) outer = CheckValidObject(L, -1);
        if (lua_getfield(L, 2, "template") != LUA_TNIL) tmpl = CheckValidObject(L, -1);
        if (lua_getfield(L, 2, "flags") != LUA_TNIL) flags = (uint64_t)luaL_checkinteger(L, -1);
        if (lua_getfield(L, 2, "archetype") != LUA_TNIL && lua_toboolean(L, -1)) flags |= ue3::RF_ArchetypeObject;
        if (lua_getfield(L, 2, "replace") != LUA_TNIL) replace = lua_toboolean(L, -1);
        lua_pop(L, 6);
    }
    if (!name.empty() && !replace) {
        UObject where = outer ? outer : ue3::TransientPackage();
        if (UObject existing = FindInOuter(where, name))
            luaL_error(L, "%s already exists (pass replace = true to reconstruct it)", ue3::ObjFullName(existing).c_str());
    }
    PushObject(L, ConstructOrError(L, cls, outer, name, flags, tmpl));
    return 1;
}

// h7.clone(source, name [, outer [, extra_flags]]) -> object, created
// A copy of `source` (same class, properties copied from it) called `name` inside `outer` (default: the transient
// package). Archetypes stay archetypes, so the copy can be used wherever the game expects a template. If `outer`
// already holds an object called `name` of the same class it is returned instead (created = false), which makes
// mod code safe to run again after a hot reload.
static int H_clone(lua_State* L) {
    UObject src = CheckValidObject(L, 1);
    std::string name = luaL_checkstring(L, 2);
    UObject outer = lua_isnoneornil(L, 3) ? ue3::TransientPackage() : CheckValidObject(L, 3);
    UObject cls = ue3::ObjClass(src);
    if (UObject existing = FindInOuter(outer, name)) {
        if (ue3::ObjClass(existing) != cls)
            luaL_error(L, "%s already exists with another class", ue3::ObjFullName(existing).c_str());
        PushObject(L, existing);
        lua_pushboolean(L, 0);
        return 2;
    }
    uint64_t flags = (ObjFlags(src) & kKeepFlags) | ue3::RF_Public | ue3::RF_Standalone;
    if (!lua_isnoneornil(L, 4)) flags |= (uint64_t)luaL_checkinteger(L, 4);
    PushObject(L, ConstructOrError(L, cls, outer, name, flags, src));
    lua_pushboolean(L, 1);
    return 2;
}

// h7.package(name) -> the top-level package `name`, created if it doesn't exist. Objects created inside it get
// stable path names ("Name.Object"), which is what save games store.
static int H_package(lua_State* L) {
    std::string name = luaL_checkstring(L, 1);
    UObject cls = ue3::FindClass("Core.Package");
    if (!cls) luaL_error(L, "Core.Package not found");
    if (UObject p = ue3::FindObject("Package " + name)) {
        if (!ue3::ObjOuter(p)) return PushObject(L, p), 1;
    }
    std::string err;
    UObject p = ue3::ConstructTopLevel(cls, name, ue3::RF_Public | ue3::RF_Standalone, err);
    if (!p) luaL_error(L, "could not create package '%s': %s", name.c_str(), err.c_str());
    PushObject(L, p);
    return 1;
}

// h7.name(str) -> str; adds str to the engine name table (so it can be used as an FName)
static int H_name(lua_State* L) {
    const char* s = luaL_checkstring(L, 1);
    if (ue3::FindNameIndex(s) < 0 && ue3::MakeName(s).index < 0) luaL_error(L, "could not create name '%s'", s);
    lua_pushstring(L, s);
    return 1;
}

static int H_can_create(lua_State* L) {
    lua_pushboolean(L, ue3::FactoryAvailable());
    return 1;
}

static int H_enum(lua_State* L) {
    const char* name = luaL_checkstring(L, 1);
    UObject e = ue3::FindObject(std::string("Enum ") + name);
    if (!e) {
        // Allow bare enum names: search by object name.
        int32_t idx = ue3::FindNameIndex(name);
        for (int32_t i = 0, n = ue3::ObjectCount(); i < n && !e && idx >= 0; ++i) {
            UObject o = ue3::ObjectAt(i);
            if (o && ue3::ObjFName(o).index == idx && ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(o)) == "Enum") e = o;
        }
    }
    if (!e) luaL_error(L, "unknown enum '%s'", name);
    auto names = ue3::EnumNames(e);
    lua_createtable(L, 0, (int)names.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < names.size(); ++i) {
        lua_pushinteger(L, (lua_Integer)i);
        lua_setfield(L, -2, names[i].c_str());
    }
    return 1;
}

static void PushFieldList(lua_State* L, UObject s, bool functions) {
    lua_newtable(L);
    int n = 0;
    for (UObject f : ue3::Fields(s, true)) {
        bool isFn = IsFunction(f);
        if (isFn != functions) continue;
        if (!isFn && ue3::KindOf(f) == PropKind::Unknown) continue;
        lua_createtable(L, 0, 4);
        lua_pushstring(L, ue3::ObjName(f).c_str());
        lua_setfield(L, -2, "name");
        lua_pushstring(L, ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(f)).c_str());
        lua_setfield(L, -2, "type");
        lua_pushstring(L, ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjOuter(f)).c_str());
        lua_setfield(L, -2, "owner");
        if (!isFn) {
            PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(f);
            lua_pushinteger(L, p.offset);
            lua_setfield(L, -2, "offset");
        } else {
            lua_pushinteger(L, ue3::FuncFlags(f));
            lua_setfield(L, -2, "flags");
        }
        lua_rawseti(L, -2, ++n);
    }
}

// h7.properties(classOrObjOrStructName) / h7.functions(...)
static UObject StructArg(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    if (lua_type(L, idx) == LUA_TSTRING) {
        const char* s = lua_tostring(L, idx);
        UObject o = ue3::FindClass(s);
        if (!o) o = ue3::FindObject(std::string("ScriptStruct ") + s);
        if (!o) luaL_error(L, "unknown class/struct '%s'", s);
        return o;
    }
    if (auto* r = static_cast<StructRef*>(luaL_testudata(L, idx, kStruct))) return r->type;
    UObject o = CheckValidObject(L, idx);
    std::string cls = ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(o));
    return cls == "Class" || cls == "ScriptStruct" || cls == "Function" ? o : ue3::ObjClass(o);
}
static int H_properties(lua_State* L) { PushFieldList(L, StructArg(L, 1), false); return 1; }
static int H_functions(lua_State* L) { PushFieldList(L, StructArg(L, 1), true); return 1; }

// h7.to_table(struct/array/object) -> plain Lua copy (one level of objects, deep for structs/arrays)
static int H_to_table(lua_State* L) {
    if (auto* r = static_cast<ArrayRef*>(luaL_testudata(L, 1, kArray))) {
        CheckArray(L, 1);
        auto a = mem::ReadOr<ue3::TArrayRaw>(r->arr, {});
        PropInfo p = ue3::Describe(r->inner);
        lua_createtable(L, a.num, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < a.num; ++i) {
            lua_pushcfunction(L, H_to_table);
            PushElem(L, a.data + i * p.elementSize, r->inner, p.kind, r->frame);
            // objects stay as references (expanding them recursed through object graphs: C stack overflow)
            if (!luaL_testudata(L, -1, kObj)) lua_call(L, 1, 1);
            else lua_remove(L, -2);
            lua_rawseti(L, -2, i + 1);
        }
        return 1;
    }
    UObject type = 0;
    uintptr_t base = 0;
    uint64_t frame = 0;
    const StructRef* sref = nullptr;
    if (auto* r = static_cast<StructRef*>(luaL_testudata(L, 1, kStruct))) {
        CheckStruct(L, 1);
        type = r->type, base = r->addr, frame = r->frame, sref = r;
    } else if (luaL_testudata(L, 1, kObj)) {
        base = CheckValidObject(L, 1);
        type = ue3::ObjClass(base);
    } else {
        lua_settop(L, 1);
        return 1; // plain values pass through
    }
    lua_newtable(L);
    for (UObject f : ue3::Fields(type, true)) {
        if (IsFunction(f) || ue3::KindOf(f) == PropKind::Unknown) continue;
        lua_pushcfunction(L, H_to_table);
        PushProp(L, sref ? FieldBase(sref, f) : base, f, frame);
        // objects stay as references; structs/arrays are expanded
        if (!luaL_testudata(L, -1, kObj)) lua_call(L, 1, 1);
        else lua_remove(L, -2);
        lua_setfield(L, -2, ue3::ObjName(f).c_str());
    }
