```cpp
    return 1;
}

// h7.exec("console command") -> result string, via the local PlayerController
static int H_exec(lua_State* L) {
    const char* cmd = luaL_checkstring(L, 1);
    UObject pcClass = ue3::FindClass("Engine.PlayerController");
    UObject pc = 0;
    for (int32_t i = 0, n = ue3::ObjectCount(); i < n && !pc; ++i) {
        UObject o = ue3::ObjectAt(i);
        if (o && ue3::IsA(o, pcClass) && !IsDefaultObject(o)) pc = o;
    }
    if (!pc) luaL_error(L, "no PlayerController exists yet");
    PushObject(L, pc);
    lua_getfield(L, -1, "ConsoleCommand");
    lua_insert(L, -2);
    lua_pushstring(L, cmd);
    lua_call(L, 2, 1);
    return 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Hooks
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct LuaHook {
    int mod;
    int ref;
    int listener;
};
static std::unordered_map<int, LuaHook> g_luaHooks; // keyed by listener id

static UObject ResolveFunction(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    if (lua_type(L, idx) != LUA_TSTRING) {
        UObject f = CheckValidObject(L, idx);
        if (!IsFunction(f)) luaL_error(L, "expected a Function object");
        return f;
    }
    std::string path = lua_tostring(L, idx);
    UObject f = ue3::FindObject(path.rfind("Function ", 0) == 0 ? path : "Function " + path);
    if (!f) luaL_error(L, "function '%s' not found (use Package.Class.Function)", path.c_str());
    return f;
}

static UObject ReturnProp(UObject fn) {
    for (auto& p : ue3::FuncParams(fn))
        if (p.flags & ue3::CPF_ReturnParm) return p.prop;
    return 0;
}

// UnrealScript-style type name of a property, e.g. "int", "H7Creature", "array<H7BaseCreatureStack>".
static std::string TypeName(UObject prop) {
    switch (ue3::KindOf(prop)) {
    case PropKind::Byte: return ue3::PropInner(prop) ? ue3::ObjName(ue3::PropInner(prop)) : "byte";
    case PropKind::Int: return "int";
    case PropKind::Bool: return "bool";
    case PropKind::Float: return "float";
    case PropKind::Name: return "name";
    case PropKind::Str: return "string";
    case PropKind::QWord: return "qword";
    case PropKind::Object: case PropKind::Struct: return ue3::ObjName(ue3::PropInner(prop));
    case PropKind::Class: return "class";
    case PropKind::Array: return "array<" + TypeName(ue3::PropInner(prop)) + ">";
    default: {
        std::string cls = ue3::ObjName(ue3::ObjClass(prop));
        return cls.size() > 8 && cls.ends_with("Property") ? cls.substr(0, cls.size() - 8) : cls;
    }
    }
}

// h7.signature(func) -> "native event int Name(int A, out float B, optional bool C)"
static int H_signature(lua_State* L) {
    UObject fn = ResolveFunction(L, 1);
    uint32_t flags = ue3::FuncFlags(fn);
    std::string s;
    if (flags & ue3::FUNC_Static) s += "static ";
    if (flags & ue3::FUNC_Native) s += "native ";
    s += (flags & ue3::FUNC_Event) ? "event " : "function ";
    std::string args, ret = "void";
    for (auto& p : ue3::FuncParams(fn)) {
        if (p.flags & ue3::CPF_ReturnParm) {
            ret = TypeName(p.prop);
            continue;
        }
        if (!args.empty()) args += ", ";
        if (p.flags & ue3::CPF_OutParm) args += "out ";
        if (p.flags & ue3::CPF_OptionalParm) args += "optional ";
        args += TypeName(p.prop) + " " + p.name;
    }
    s += ret + " " + ue3::ObjPathName(fn) + "(" + args + ")";
    lua_pushstring(L, s.c_str());
    return 1;
}

static int WriteReturnThunk(lua_State* L) {
    auto addr = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(lua_touserdata(L, 2));
    auto prop = reinterpret_cast<UObject>(lua_touserdata(L, 3));
    WriteElem(L, 1, addr, prop, ue3::KindOf(prop));
    return 0;
}

static bool RunLuaHook(int ref, int mod, hooks::CallContext& ctx, bool post) {
    lua_State* L = State();
    if (!L) return false;
    ModScope scope(mod);
    int top = lua_gettop(L);
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Traceback);
    int eh = lua_gettop(L);
    lua_rawgeti(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, ref);
    PushObject(L, ctx.self);
    uint64_t frame = g_nextFrame++;
    g_liveFrames.insert(frame);
    if (ctx.locals) {
        PushStruct(L, reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(ctx.locals), ctx.function, frame);
        static_cast<StructRef*>(lua_touserdata(L, -1))->outParms = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(ctx.outParms);
    } else {
        lua_pushnil(L);
    }
    int nargs = 2;
    UObject ret = post && ctx.result ? ReturnProp(ctx.function) : 0;
    if (ret) {
        PushElem(L, reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(ctx.result), ret, ue3::KindOf(ret), frame);
        ++nargs;
    }
    bool block = false;
    if (lua_pcall(L, nargs, 1, eh) != LUA_OK) {
        Report("hook " + ue3::ObjPathName(ctx.function) + " (" + ModName(mod) + ")", lua_tostring(L, -1));
    } else if (!post) {
        block = lua_isboolean(L, -1) && lua_toboolean(L, -1);
    } else if (ret && !lua_isnil(L, -1)) {
        // Override the return value (protected, so type errors are reported instead of aborting).
        lua_pushcfunction(L, WriteReturnThunk);
        lua_insert(L, -2);
        lua_pushlightuserdata(L, ctx.result);
        lua_pushlightuserdata(L, reinterpret_cast<void*>(ret));
        if (lua_pcall(L, 3, 0, eh) != LUA_OK) Report("hook return " + ue3::ObjPathName(ctx.function), lua_tostring(L, -1));
    }
    g_liveFrames.erase(frame);
    lua_settop(L, top);
    return block;
}

// h7.hook(func, callback [, "pre"|"post"]) -> id
static int AddHook(lua_State* L, bool post) {
    UObject fn = ResolveFunction(L, 1);
    luaL_checktype(L, 2, LUA_TFUNCTION);
    lua_pushvalue(L, 2);
    int ref = luaL_ref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX);
    int mod = CurrentModId();
    hooks::PreFn pre;
    hooks::PostFn postFn;
    if (post) postFn = [ref, mod](hooks::CallContext& c) { RunLuaHook(ref, mod, c, true); };
    else pre = [ref, mod](hooks::CallContext& c) { return RunLuaHook(ref, mod, c, false); };
    int id = hooks::Add(fn, pre, postFn);
    if (!id) {
        luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, ref);
        luaL_error(L, "could not hook %s", ue3::ObjPathName(fn).c_str());
    }
    if (!post && (ue3::FuncFlags(fn) & ue3::FUNC_Native))
        Log("note: %s is native; its hook receives no params and cannot block", ue3::ObjPathName(fn).c_str());
    g_luaHooks[id] = {mod, ref, id};
    lua_pushinteger(L, id);
    return 1;
}
static int H_hook(lua_State* L) {
    const char* when = luaL_optstring(L, 3, "pre");
    return AddHook(L, strcmp(when, "post") == 0);
}
static int H_hook_post(lua_State* L) { return AddHook(L, true); }

static void RemoveHook(lua_State* L, int id) {
    auto it = g_luaHooks.find(id);
    if (it == g_luaHooks.end()) return;
    hooks::Remove(id);
    luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, it->second.ref);
    g_luaHooks.erase(it);
}

static int H_unhook(lua_State* L) {
    RemoveHook(L, (int)luaL_checkinteger(L, 1));
    return 0;
}

void RemoveModHooks(int modId) {
    std::vector<int> ids;
    for (auto& [id, h] : g_luaHooks)
        if (h.mod == modId) ids.push_back(id);
    for (int id : ids) RemoveHook(State(), id);
}

int Traceback(lua_State* L) {
    const char* msg = lua_tostring(L, 1);
    luaL_traceback(L, L, msg ? msg : luaL_tolstring(L, 1, nullptr), 1);
    return 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

void OpenBindings(lua_State* L) {
    luaL_newmetatable(L, kObj);
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Obj_index); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__index");
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Obj_newindex); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__newindex");
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Obj_eq); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__eq");
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Obj_tostring); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__tostring");
    lua_pop(L, 1);

    const luaL_Reg methods[] = {
        {"get_name", M_get_name}, {"get_full_name", M_get_full_name}, {"get_path_name", M_get_path_name},
        {"get_class", M_get_class}, {"get_outer", M_get_outer}, {"get_address", M_get_address},
        {"get_default", M_get_default}, {"get_super", M_get_super}, {"is_valid", M_is_valid},
        {"is_a", M_is_a}, {"call", M_call}, {nullptr, nullptr},
    };
    luaL_newlib(L, methods);
    lua_setfield(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, "H7ObjectMethods");

    luaL_newmetatable(L, kStruct);
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Struct_index); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__index");
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Struct_newindex); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__newindex");
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Struct_tostring); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__tostring");
    lua_pop(L, 1);

    luaL_newmetatable(L, kArray);
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Array_index); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__index");
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Array_newindex); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__newindex");
    lua_pushcfunction(L, Array_len); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__len");
    lua_pop(L, 1);

    const luaL_Reg h7lib[] = {
        {"log", H_log}, {"warn", H_warn}, {"error", H_error},
        {"find", H_find}, {"find_class", H_find_class}, {"find_all", H_find_all}, {"first", H_first},
        {"enum", H_enum}, {"properties", H_properties}, {"functions", H_functions}, {"to_table", H_to_table},
        {"exec", H_exec}, {"hook", H_hook}, {"hook_post", H_hook_post}, {"unhook", H_unhook},
        {"signature", H_signature}, {"new_object", H_new_object}, {"clone", H_clone}, {"package", H_package},
        {"name", H_name}, {"can_create", H_can_create}, {nullptr, nullptr},
    };
    lua_getglobal(L, "h7");
    if (lua_isnil(L, -1)) {
        lua_pop(L, 1);
        lua_newtable(L);
    }
    luaL_setfuncs(L, h7lib, 0);
    lua_newtable(L); // h7.flags: object flags for new_object / clone
    const std::pair<const char*, uint64_t> flagList[] = {
        {"Public", ue3::RF_Public}, {"Standalone", ue3::RF_Standalone}, {"Transient", ue3::RF_Transient},
        {"Archetype", ue3::RF_ArchetypeObject}, {"RootSet", ue3::RF_RootSet},
    };
    for (auto& [n, v] : flagList) { lua_pushinteger(L, (lua_Integer)v); lua_setfield(L, -2, n); }
    lua_setfield(L, -2, "flags");
    lua_setglobal(L, "h7");
}

} // namespace h7::lua

```

`src/lua/runtime.cpp`:

```cpp
// Lua runtime: state lifetime, mod discovery/loading/hot-reload, events, timers, dev exec folder.
// Everything here runs on the game thread (driven by the viewport Tick hook).
#include "runtime.h"
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include "../engine/paths.h"
#include "../ue3/core.h"
#include "../ue3/reflection.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <lua.h>
#include <lauxlib.h>
#include <lualib.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <filesystem>
#include <map>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace fs = std::filesystem;

namespace h7::lua {

static lua_State* g_L = nullptr;
lua_State* State() { return g_L; }

static const char* kVersion = "0.1.0";

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mods
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

// A setting the mod's manifest declares (`settings = { {key = ..., ...}, ... }`). Values are kept as text in
// H7ModEngine.ini, section [Mod.<name>], and converted to the setting's type when Lua reads them.
struct Setting {
    std::string key, label, description;
    std::string type;                // "bool" | "number" | "choice" | "text"
    double min = -HUGE_VAL, max = HUGE_VAL, step = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> options; // choice
    std::string def, value;          // normalized text
    bool reload = false;             // reload the mod when the value changes
    std::string control;             // how menus should show it ("slider", "input", "arrows"; "" = their choice)
};

struct Mod {
    int id;
    std::string name;
    std::string version;
    std::string author;
    std::string description;
    std::string category;       // manifest `category`: groups mods in the Mods menu
    std::vector<std::string> loadAfter;
    fs::path dir;
    bool defaultEnabled = true; // manifest `enabled`
    bool enabled = true;        // after the ini's [Mods] Enabled/Disabled lists
    bool hidden = false;        // manifest `hidden`: left out of mod lists shown to players
    std::vector<Setting> settings;
    bool pendingReload = false;  // a `reload` setting changed
    int envRef = LUA_NOREF;
    bool loaded = false;
    long long stamp = 0; // newest .lua write time, for hot reload
};

static std::vector<Mod> g_mods;
static int g_currentMod = 0;
static std::string g_noMod = "engine";

int CurrentModId() { return g_currentMod; }
int SetCurrentModId(int id) { return std::exchange(g_currentMod, id); }
const std::string& ModName(int id) {
    for (auto& m : g_mods)
        if (m.id == id) return m.name;
    return g_noMod;
}

void Report(const std::string& where, const std::string& error) { Log("ERROR in %s: %s", where.c_str(), error.c_str()); }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Events and timers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct Handler {
    int id;
    int mod;
    int ref;
};
static std::map<std::string, std::vector<Handler>> g_events;

struct Timer {
    int id;
    int mod;
    int ref;
    double due;
    double interval; // 0 = one-shot
};
static std::vector<Timer> g_timers;
static int g_nextId = 1;
static double g_clock = 0;

static int H_on(lua_State* L) {
    const char* ev = luaL_checkstring(L, 1);
    luaL_checktype(L, 2, LUA_TFUNCTION);
    lua_pushvalue(L, 2);
    int id = g_nextId++;
    g_events[ev].push_back({id, g_currentMod, luaL_ref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX)});
    lua_pushinteger(L, id);
    return 1;
}

static int H_off(lua_State* L) {
    int id = (int)luaL_checkinteger(L, 1);
    for (auto& [name, list] : g_events) {
        for (auto it = list.begin(); it != list.end(); ++it) {
            if (it->id != id) continue;
            luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, it->ref);
            list.erase(it);
            return 0;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

void EmitEvent(const char* name, int nargs) {
    lua_State* L = g_L;
    int base = lua_gettop(L) - nargs; // args occupy base+1..top
    auto it = g_events.find(name);
    if (it != g_events.end()) {
        std::vector<Handler> list = it->second; // handlers may unsubscribe while running
        for (auto& h : list) {
            ModScope scope(h.mod);
            lua_pushcfunction(L, Traceback);
            lua_rawgeti(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, h.ref);
            for (int i = 1; i <= nargs; ++i) lua_pushvalue(L, base + i);
            if (lua_pcall(L, nargs, 0, base + nargs + 1) != LUA_OK)
                Report(std::string("event '") + name + "' (" + ModName(h.mod) + ")", lua_tostring(L, -1));
            lua_settop(L, base + nargs);
        }
    }
    lua_settop(L, base);
}

static int AddTimer(lua_State* L, bool repeat) {
    double secs = luaL_checknumber(L, 1);
    luaL_checktype(L, 2, LUA_TFUNCTION);
    lua_pushvalue(L, 2);
    int id = g_nextId++;
    g_timers.push_back({id, g_currentMod, luaL_ref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX), g_clock + secs, repeat ? std::max(secs, 0.0) : 0});
    lua_pushinteger(L, id);
    return 1;
}
static int H_after(lua_State* L) { return AddTimer(L, false); }
static int H_every(lua_State* L) { return AddTimer(L, true); }

static int H_cancel(lua_State* L) {
    int id = (int)luaL_checkinteger(L, 1);
    for (auto it = g_timers.begin(); it != g_timers.end(); ++it) {
        if (it->id != id) continue;
        luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, it->ref);
        g_timers.erase(it);
        break;
    }
    return 0;
}

static void RunTimers() {
    lua_State* L = g_L;
    std::vector<Timer> due;
    for (auto it = g_timers.begin(); it != g_timers.end();) {
        if (it->due > g_clock) { ++it; continue; }
        due.push_back(*it);
        if (it->interval > 0) {
            it->due = g_clock + it->interval;
            ++it;
        } else {
            it = g_timers.erase(it);
        }
    }
    for (auto& t : due) {
        ModScope scope(t.mod);
        lua_pushcfunction(L, Traceback);
        lua_rawgeti(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, t.ref);
        if (lua_pcall(L, 0, 0, -2) != LUA_OK) Report("timer (" + ModName(t.mod) + ")", lua_tostring(L, -1));
        lua_settop(L, 0);
        if (t.interval == 0) luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, t.ref);
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mod loading
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static long long NewestLuaStamp(const fs::path& dir) {
    long long newest = 0;
    std::error_code ec;
    for (auto& e : fs::recursive_directory_iterator(dir, ec)) {
        if (e.path().extension() != ".lua") continue;
        newest = std::max<long long>(newest, e.last_write_time(ec).time_since_epoch().count());
    }
    return newest;
}

// [Mods] Enabled= / Disabled= in H7ModEngine.ini: comma-separated mod names that override the manifest's `enabled`.
static std::vector<std::string> ModList(const wchar_t* key) {
    wchar_t buf[2048] = {};
    GetPrivateProfileStringW(L"Mods", key, L"", buf, 2048, EnginePath(L"H7ModEngine.ini").c_str());
    std::vector<std::string> out;
    std::string s = Narrow(buf), cur;
    for (char c : s + ",") {
        if (c == ',') {
            while (!cur.empty() && cur.back() == ' ') cur.pop_back();
            if (!cur.empty()) out.push_back(cur);
            cur.clear();
        } else if (c != ' ' || !cur.empty()) {
            cur += c;
        }
    }
    return out;
}

static void WriteModList(const wchar_t* key, const std::vector<std::string>& names) {
    std::string s;
    for (auto& n : names) s += (s.empty() ? "" : ", ") + n;
    WritePrivateProfileStringW(L"Mods", key, Widen(s).c_str(), EnginePath(L"H7ModEngine.ini").c_str());
}

static bool Contains(const std::vector<std::string>& v, const std::string& s) {
    return std::find(v.begin(), v.end(), s) != v.end();
}

static bool EffectiveEnabled(const Mod& m, const std::vector<std::string>& enabled,
                             const std::vector<std::string>& disabled) {
    if (Contains(disabled, m.name)) return false;
    if (Contains(enabled, m.name)) return true;
    return m.defaultEnabled;
}

static std::string TableString(lua_State* L, int idx, const char* key, const std::string& fallback) {
    lua_getfield(L, idx, key);
    std::string v = lua_isstring(L, -1) ? lua_tostring(L, -1) : fallback;
    lua_pop(L, 1);
    return v;
}

// --- Settings ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static std::string FormatNumber(double v) {
    char buf[64];
    if (std::fabs(v) < 1e15 && v == std::floor(v)) snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%lld", (long long)v);
    else snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%.10g", v);
    return buf;
}

static std::string Lower(std::string s) {
    for (auto& c : s) c = (char)tolower((unsigned char)c);
    return s;
}

// The canonical text of `in` for setting `st`, or false if it isn't a valid value. Numbers are clamped to
// min/max and snapped to `step`.
static bool Normalize(const Setting& st, const std::string& in, std::string& out) {
    if (st.type == "bool") {
        std::string l = Lower(in);
        if (l == "true" || l == "1" || l == "yes" || l == "on") return out = "true", true;
        if (l == "false" || l == "0" || l == "no" || l == "off") return out = "false", true;
        return false;
    }
    if (st.type == "number") {
        char* end = nullptr;
        double v = strtod(in.c_str(), &end);
        if (end == in.c_str() || !std::isfinite(v)) return false;
        if (st.step > 0) {
            double base = std::isfinite(st.min) ? st.min : 0;
            v = base + std::round((v - base) / st.step) * st.step;
        }
        v = std::fmin(std::fmax(v, st.min), st.max);
        return out = FormatNumber(v), true;
    }
    if (st.type == "choice") {
        for (auto& o : st.options)
            if (_stricmp(o.c_str(), in.c_str()) == 0) return out = o, true;
        return false;
    }
    return out = in, true;
}

static std::wstring SettingsSection(const Mod& m) { return Widen("Mod." + m.name); }

static void ReadSettingValues(Mod& m) {
    for (auto& st : m.settings) {
        wchar_t buf[1024] = {};
        GetPrivateProfileStringW(SettingsSection(m).c_str(), Widen(st.key).c_str(), L"\x01", buf, 1024,
                                 EnginePath(L"H7ModEngine.ini").c_str());
        std::string raw = Narrow(buf), v;
        if (raw == "\x01") {
            st.value = st.def;
        } else if (Normalize(st, raw, v)) {
            st.value = v;
        } else {
            st.value = st.def;
            Log("Mod %s: setting %s has an invalid value '%s' in the ini; using %s", m.name.c_str(), st.key.c_str(),
                raw.c_str(), st.def.c_str());
        }
    }
}

// Lua value at idx -> text (booleans and numbers as Normalize expects them)
static std::string ValueText(lua_State* L, int idx) {
    switch (lua_type(L, idx)) {
    case LUA_TBOOLEAN: return lua_toboolean(L, idx) ? "true" : "false";
    case LUA_TNUMBER: return FormatNumber(lua_tonumber(L, idx));
    case LUA_TSTRING: return lua_tostring(L, idx);
    default: return "";
    }
}

static void PushSettingValue(lua_State* L, const Setting& st, const std::string& text) {
    if (st.type == "bool") {
        lua_pushboolean(L, text == "true");
    } else if (st.type == "number") {
        double v = strtod(text.c_str(), nullptr);
        if (v == std::floor(v) && std::fabs(v) < 1e15) lua_pushinteger(L, (lua_Integer)v);
        else lua_pushnumber(L, v);
    } else {
        lua_pushstring(L, text.c_str());
    }
}

// Parses one entry of the manifest's `settings` list (the table at the top of the stack).
static bool ParseSetting(lua_State* L, Mod& m, Setting& st) {
    int t = lua_gettop(L);
    st.key = TableString(L, t, "key", "");
    if (st.key.empty()) return Log("Mod %s: a setting without `key` is ignored", m.name.c_str()), false;
    st.label = TableString(L, t, "label", st.key);
    st.description = TableString(L, t, "description", "");
    lua_getfield(L, t, "options");
    if (lua_istable(L, -1)) {
        for (int i = 1; lua_rawgeti(L, -1, i) != LUA_TNIL; ++i) {
            st.options.push_back(ValueText(L, -1));
            lua_pop(L, 1);
        }
        lua_pop(L, 1); // the nil
    }
    lua_pop(L, 1);
    lua_getfield(L, t, "default");
    int defType = lua_type(L, -1);
    std::string def = ValueText(L, -1);
    lua_pop(L, 1);
    std::string inferred = defType == LUA_TBOOLEAN ? "bool" : defType == LUA_TNUMBER ? "number"
                           : !st.options.empty() ? "choice" : "text";
    st.type = TableString(L, t, "type", inferred);
    if (st.type != "bool" && st.type != "number" && st.type != "choice" && st.type != "text")
        return Log("Mod %s: setting %s has unknown type '%s'", m.name.c_str(), st.key.c_str(), st.type.c_str()), false;
    if (st.type == "choice" && st.options.empty())
        return Log("Mod %s: choice setting %s has no options", m.name.c_str(), st.key.c_str()), false;
    lua_getfield(L, t, "min"); if (lua_isnumber(L, -1)) st.min = lua_tonumber(L, -1); lua_pop(L, 1);
    lua_getfield(L, t, "max"); if (lua_isnumber(L, -1)) st.max = lua_tonumber(L, -1); lua_pop(L, 1);
    lua_getfield(L, t, "step"); if (lua_isnumber(L, -1)) st.step = lua_tonumber(L, -1); lua_pop(L, 1);
    lua_getfield(L, t, "reload"); st.reload = lua_toboolean(L, -1); lua_pop(L, 1);
    st.control = TableString(L, t, "control", "");
    std::string v;
    if (defType == LUA_TNIL || !Normalize(st, def, v)) {
        v = st.type == "bool" ? "false" : st.type == "number" ? FormatNumber(std::isfinite(st.min) ? st.min : 0)
            : st.type == "choice" ? st.options[0] : "";
        if (defType != LUA_TNIL)
            Log("Mod %s: setting %s has an invalid default; using %s", m.name.c_str(), st.key.c_str(), v.c_str());
    }
    st.def = st.value = v;
    return true;
}

// Reads mods/<dir>/mod.lua (optional manifest returning a table). False if the manifest fails to run.
static bool ReadManifest(Mod& m) {
    fs::path manifest = m.dir / "mod.lua";
    m.name = m.dir.filename().string();
    if (!fs::exists(manifest)) return true;
    lua_State* L = g_L;
    if (luaL_dofile(L, manifest.string().c_str()) != LUA_OK) {
        Report("manifest " + manifest.string(), lua_tostring(L, -1));
        lua_settop(L, 0);
        return false;
    }
    if (lua_istable(L, -1)) {
        m.name = TableString(L, -1, "name", m.name);
        m.version = TableString(L, -1, "version", "");
        m.author = TableString(L, -1, "author", "");
        m.description = TableString(L, -1, "description", "");
        m.category = TableString(L, -1, "category", "");
        lua_getfield(L, -1, "load_after");
        if (lua_istable(L, -1)) {
            for (int i = 1; lua_rawgeti(L, -1, i) == LUA_TSTRING; ++i) {
                m.loadAfter.push_back(lua_tostring(L, -1));
                lua_pop(L, 1);
            }
            lua_pop(L, 1);
        }
        lua_pop(L, 1);
        lua_getfield(L, -1, "enabled");
        m.defaultEnabled = lua_isnil(L, -1) || lua_toboolean(L, -1);
        lua_pop(L, 1);
        lua_getfield(L, -1, "hidden");
        m.hidden = lua_toboolean(L, -1);
        lua_pop(L, 1);
        lua_getfield(L, -1, "settings");
        if (lua_istable(L, -1)) {
            for (int i = 1; lua_rawgeti(L, -1, i) == LUA_TTABLE; ++i) {
                Setting st;
                if (ParseSetting(L, m, st)) m.settings.push_back(std::move(st));
                lua_pop(L, 1);
            }
        }
        lua_settop(L, 0);
        ReadSettingValues(m);
        return true;
    }
    lua_settop(L, 0);
    return true;
}

// mod.require("file") — loads <moddir>/file.lua in the mod's environment (cached per mod).
static int Mod_require(lua_State* L) {
    const char* name = luaL_checkstring(L, 1);
    int envIdx = lua_upvalueindex(1);
    lua_getfield(L, envIdx, "__modules");
    lua_getfield(L, -1, name);
    if (!lua_isnil(L, -1)) return 1;
    lua_pop(L, 1);
    lua_getfield(L, envIdx, "mod");
    lua_getfield(L, -1, "dir");
    std::string path = std::string(lua_tostring(L, -1)) + "\\" + name + ".lua";
    lua_pop(L, 2);
    if (luaL_loadfile(L, path.c_str()) != LUA_OK) return lua_error(L);
    lua_pushvalue(L, envIdx);
    lua_setupvalue(L, -2, 1); // _ENV
    lua_call(L, 0, 1);
    if (lua_isnil(L, -1)) {
        lua_pop(L, 1);
        lua_pushboolean(L, 1);
    }
    lua_pushvalue(L, -1);
    lua_setfield(L, -3, name);
    return 1;
}

// Persistent per-mod data: mod.save(tbl) / mod.load() -> tbl, stored as a Lua chunk in data\<mod>.lua.
static void Serialize(lua_State* L, int idx, std::string& out, int depth) {
    idx = lua_absindex(L, idx);
    switch (lua_type(L, idx)) {
    case LUA_TNUMBER:
        if (lua_isinteger(L, idx)) out += std::to_string(lua_tointeger(L, idx));
        else { char b[64]; snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "%.17g", lua_tonumber(L, idx)); out += b; }
        break;
    case LUA_TBOOLEAN: out += lua_toboolean(L, idx) ? "true" : "false"; break;
    case LUA_TSTRING: {
        size_t n;
        const char* s = lua_tolstring(L, idx, &n);
        out += '"';
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            unsigned char c = s[i];
            if (c == '"' || c == '\\') out += '\\', out += c;
            else if (c == '\n') out += "\\n";
            else if (c < 32) { char b[8]; snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\\%03d", c); out += b; }
            else out += c;
        }
        out += '"';
        break;
    }
    case LUA_TTABLE:
        if (depth > 32) luaL_error(L, "table too deep to save");
        out += "{";
        lua_pushnil(L);
        while (lua_next(L, idx)) {
            out += "[";
            Serialize(L, -2, out, depth + 1);
            out += "]=";
            Serialize(L, -1, out, depth + 1);
            out += ",";
            lua_pop(L, 1);
        }
        out += "}";
        break;
    default: out += "nil"; break;
    }
}

static fs::path DataFile(lua_State* L) {
    lua_getfield(L, lua_upvalueindex(1), "name");
    fs::path p = fs::path(EnginePath(L"data")) / (std::string(lua_tostring(L, -1)) + ".lua");
    lua_pop(L, 1);
    return p;
}

static int Mod_save(lua_State* L) {
    luaL_checktype(L, 1, LUA_TTABLE);
    std::string out = "return ";
    Serialize(L, 1, out, 0);
    fs::create_directories(EnginePath(L"data"));
    FILE* f = nullptr;
    if (_wfopen_s(&f, DataFile(L).wstring().c_str(), L"wb") || !f) return luaL_error(L, "could not write save data");
    fwrite(out.data(), 1, out.size(), f);
    fclose(f);
    return 0;
}

static int Mod_load(lua_State* L) {
    fs::path p = DataFile(L);
    if (!fs::exists(p)) return lua_newtable(L), 1;
    if (luaL_loadfile(L, p.string().c_str()) != LUA_OK) return lua_error(L);
    lua_newtable(L); // empty _ENV: data files can't call anything
    lua_setupvalue(L, -2, 1);
    lua_call(L, 0, 1);
    if (!lua_istable(L, -1)) lua_newtable(L);
    return 1;
}

static Mod* FindMod(const std::string& name) {
    for (auto& m : g_mods)
        if (m.name == name) return &m;
    return nullptr;
}
static Mod* FindModById(int id) {
    for (auto& m : g_mods)
        if (m.id == id) return &m;
    return nullptr;
}
static Setting* FindSetting(Mod& m, const std::string& key) {
    for (auto& st : m.settings)
        if (st.key == key) return &st;
    return nullptr;
}

// Sets a setting from the Lua value at idx: validated, saved in the ini, `setting_changed` fires, and a
// `reload` setting reloads the mod on the next frame. Pushes the stored value.
static int SetSetting(lua_State* L, Mod& m, const std::string& key, int idx) {
    Setting* st = FindSetting(m, key);
    if (!st) return luaL_error(L, "mod %s has no setting '%s'", m.name.c_str(), key.c_str());
    std::string v;
    if (!Normalize(*st, ValueText(L, idx), v))
        return luaL_error(L, "invalid value for %s.%s (%s)", m.name.c_str(), key.c_str(), st->type.c_str());
    bool changed = v != st->value;
    st->value = v;
    WritePrivateProfileStringW(SettingsSection(m).c_str(), Widen(key).c_str(), Widen(v).c_str(),
                               EnginePath(L"H7ModEngine.ini").c_str());
    if (changed) {
        if (st->reload && m.loaded) m.pendingReload = true;
        std::string name = m.name; // handlers may change g_mods
        lua_pushstring(L, name.c_str());
        lua_pushstring(L, key.c_str());
        PushSettingValue(L, *st, v);
        EmitEvent("setting_changed", 3);
    }
    Mod* again = FindMod(m.name);
    Setting* now = again ? FindSetting(*again, key) : nullptr;
    if (now) PushSettingValue(L, *now, now->value);
    else lua_pushnil(L);
    return 1;
}

// mod.settings: reads give the current values, writes change them (like h7.set_mod_setting), pairs() lists them.
static int Settings_index(lua_State* L) {
    Mod* m = FindModById((int)lua_tointeger(L, lua_upvalueindex(1)));
    Setting* st = m ? FindSetting(*m, luaL_checkstring(L, 2)) : nullptr;
    if (!st) return lua_pushnil(L), 1;
    PushSettingValue(L, *st, st->value);
    return 1;
}
static int Settings_newindex(lua_State* L) {
    Mod* m = FindModById((int)lua_tointeger(L, lua_upvalueindex(1)));
    if (!m) return luaL_error(L, "the mod is no longer loaded");
    return SetSetting(L, *m, luaL_checkstring(L, 2), 3);
}
static int Settings_pairs(lua_State* L) {
    Mod* m = FindModById((int)lua_tointeger(L, lua_upvalueindex(1)));
    lua_getglobal(L, "next");
    lua_newtable(L);
    if (m)
        for (auto& st : m->settings) {
            PushSettingValue(L, st, st.value);
            lua_setfield(L, -2, st.key.c_str());
        }
    lua_pushnil(L);
    return 3;
}
static void PushSettingsProxy(lua_State* L, int modId) {
    lua_newtable(L);
    lua_createtable(L, 0, 3);
    lua_pushinteger(L, modId); lua_pushcclosure(L, Settings_index, 1); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__index");
    lua_pushinteger(L, modId); lua_pushcclosure(L, Settings_newindex, 1); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__newindex");
    lua_pushinteger(L, modId); lua_pushcclosure(L, Settings_pairs, 1); lua_setfield(L, -2, "__pairs");
    lua_setmetatable(L, -2);
}

// h7.mod_settings(name) -> { {key, label, description, type, value, default, min, max, step, options, reload,
//                              control}, ... }
static int H_mod_settings(lua_State* L) {
    Mod* m = FindMod(luaL_checkstring(L, 1));
    if (!m) return luaL_error(L, "unknown mod '%s'", lua_tostring(L, 1));
    lua_createtable(L, (int)m->settings.size(), 0);
    int i = 0;
    for (auto& st : m->settings) {
        lua_createtable(L, 0, 11);
        lua_pushstring(L, st.key.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "key");
        lua_pushstring(L, st.label.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "label");
        lua_pushstring(L, st.description.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "description");
        lua_pushstring(L, st.type.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "type");
        PushSettingValue(L, st, st.value); lua_setfield(L, -2, "value");
        PushSettingValue(L, st, st.def); lua_setfield(L, -2, "default");
        if (st.type == "number") {
            if (std::isfinite(st.min)) { lua_pushnumber(L, st.min); lua_setfield(L, -2, "min"); }
            if (std::isfinite(st.max)) { lua_pushnumber(L, st.max); lua_setfield(L, -2, "max"); }
            if (st.step > 0) { lua_pushnumber(L, st.step); lua_setfield(L, -2, "step"); }
        }
        if (!st.options.empty()) {
            lua_createtable(L, (int)st.options.size(), 0);
            for (size_t k = 0; k < st.options.size(); ++k) {
                lua_pushstring(L, st.options[k].c_str());
                lua_rawseti(L, -2, (lua_Integer)k + 1);
            }
            lua_setfield(L, -2, "options");
        }
        lua_pushboolean(L, st.reload); lua_setfield(L, -2, "reload");
        if (!st.control.empty()) { lua_pushstring(L, st.control.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "control"); }
        lua_rawseti(L, -2, ++i);
    }
    return 1;
}

// h7.set_mod_setting(name, key, value) -> the stored value (clamped/snapped)
static int H_set_mod_setting(lua_State* L) {
    Mod* m = FindMod(luaL_checkstring(L, 1));
    if (!m) return luaL_error(L, "unknown mod '%s'", lua_tostring(L, 1));
    luaL_checkany(L, 3);
    return SetSetting(L, *m, luaL_checkstring(L, 2), 3);
}

static void UnloadMod(Mod& m) {
    if (!m.loaded) return;
    lua_State* L = g_L;
    {
        // Give the mod a chance to clean up: global function on_unload() in its environment.
        ModScope scope(m.id);
        lua_pushcfunction(L, Traceback);
        lua_rawgeti(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, m.envRef);
        lua_getfield(L, -1, "on_unload");
        if (lua_isfunction(L, -1)) {
            if (lua_pcall(L, 0, 0, 1) != LUA_OK) Report(m.name + ".on_unload", lua_tostring(L, -1));
        }
        lua_settop(L, 0);
    }
    RemoveModHooks(m.id);
    for (auto& [name, list] : g_events) {
        for (auto& h : list)
            if (h.mod == m.id) luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, h.ref);
        list.erase(std::remove_if(list.begin(), list.end(), [&](auto& h) { return h.mod == m.id; }), list.end());
    }
    for (auto& t : g_timers)
        if (t.mod == m.id) luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, t.ref);
    g_timers.erase(std::remove_if(g_timers.begin(), g_timers.end(), [&](auto& t) { return t.mod == m.id; }), g_timers.end());
    luaL_unref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX, m.envRef);
    m.envRef = LUA_NOREF;
    m.loaded = false;
    Log("Unloaded mod %s", m.name.c_str());
}

static void LoadMod(Mod& m) {
    lua_State* L = g_L;
    ModScope scope(m.id);
    fs::path entry = m.dir / "main.lua";
    if (!fs::exists(entry)) {
        Log("Mod %s has no main.lua; skipped", m.name.c_str());
        return;
    }
    // Environment: own globals, falling back to the shared _G.
    lua_newtable(L);
    int env = lua_gettop(L);
    lua_newtable(L);
    lua_getglobal(L, "_G");
    lua_setfield(L, -2, "__index");
    lua_setmetatable(L, env);
    lua_newtable(L);
    lua_setfield(L, env, "__modules");

    lua_createtable(L, 0, 8);
    lua_pushstring(L, m.name.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "name");
    lua_pushstring(L, m.version.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "version");
    lua_pushstring(L, m.author.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "author");
    lua_pushstring(L, m.dir.string().c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "dir");
    lua_pushvalue(L, env); lua_pushcclosure(L, Mod_require, 1); lua_setfield(L, -2, "require");
    lua_pushvalue(L, -1); lua_pushcclosure(L, Mod_save, 1); lua_setfield(L, -2, "save");
    lua_pushvalue(L, -1); lua_pushcclosure(L, Mod_load, 1); lua_setfield(L, -2, "load");
    PushSettingsProxy(L, m.id);
    lua_setfield(L, -2, "settings");
    lua_setfield(L, env, "mod");

    lua_pushvalue(L, env);
    m.envRef = luaL_ref(L, LUA_REGISTRYINDEX);
    m.loaded = true;
    m.stamp = NewestLuaStamp(m.dir);

    lua_pushcfunction(L, Traceback);
    int eh = lua_gettop(L);
    if (luaL_loadfile(L, entry.string().c_str()) != LUA_OK) {
        Report(m.name + "/main.lua", lua_tostring(L, -1));
    } else {
        lua_pushvalue(L, env);
        lua_setupvalue(L, -2, 1);
        if (lua_pcall(L, 0, 0, eh) != LUA_OK) Report(m.name + "/main.lua", lua_tostring(L, -1));
        else Log("Loaded mod %s %s%s", m.name.c_str(), m.version.c_str(), m.author.empty() ? "" : (" by " + m.author).c_str());
    }
    lua_settop(L, 0);
}

// Every mod folder is listed (h7.mods), but only enabled mods load.
static void DiscoverAndLoadMods() {
    g_mods.clear();
    std::vector<std::string> enabled = ModList(L"Enabled"), disabled = ModList(L"Disabled");
    std::error_code ec;
    int nextId = 1;
    std::vector<Mod> found;
    for (auto& e : fs::directory_iterator(EnginePath(L"mods"), ec)) {
        if (!e.is_directory()) continue;
        Mod m;
        m.id = nextId++;
        m.dir = e.path();
        if (!ReadManifest(m)) continue;
        m.enabled = EffectiveEnabled(m, enabled, disabled);
        if (!m.enabled) Log("Mod %s is disabled", m.name.c_str());
        found.push_back(std::move(m));
    }
    std::sort(found.begin(), found.end(), [](auto& a, auto& b) { return _stricmp(a.name.c_str(), b.name.c_str()) < 0; });
    // Order: alphabetical, then move each mod after the mods it lists in load_after.
    std::vector<Mod> ordered;
    while (!found.empty()) {
        bool progressed = false;
        for (auto it = found.begin(); it != found.end(); ++it) {
            bool ready = std::all_of(it->loadAfter.begin(), it->loadAfter.end(), [&](auto& dep) {
                return std::none_of(found.begin(), found.end(), [&](auto& f) { return f.name == dep; });
            });
            if (!ready) continue;
            ordered.push_back(std::move(*it));
            found.erase(it);
            progressed = true;
            break;
        }
        if (!progressed) { // dependency cycle: load the rest alphabetically
            Log("warning: load_after cycle among remaining mods");
            for (auto& m : found) ordered.push_back(std::move(m));
            break;
        }
    }
    g_mods = std::move(ordered);
    int loaded = 0;
    for (auto& m : g_mods)
        if (m.enabled) LoadMod(m), ++loaded;
    lua_pushinteger(g_L, loaded);
    EmitEvent("mods_loaded", 1);
}

// h7.set_mod_enabled(name, enabled): remembered in the ini; the mod loads or unloads on the next frame
// (never inside the caller, which may be the mod itself).
static bool g_toggled = false;
static int H_set_mod_enabled(lua_State* L) {
    const char* name = luaL_checkstring(L, 1);
    bool on = lua_toboolean(L, 2);
    auto it = std::find_if(g_mods.begin(), g_mods.end(), [&](auto& m) { return m.name == name; });
    if (it == g_mods.end()) return luaL_error(L, "unknown mod '%s'", name);
    std::vector<std::string> enabled = ModList(L"Enabled"), disabled = ModList(L"Disabled");
    std::erase(enabled, it->name);
    std::erase(disabled, it->name);
    if (on != it->defaultEnabled) (on ? enabled : disabled).push_back(it->name);
    WriteModList(L"Enabled", enabled);
    WriteModList(L"Disabled", disabled);
    it->enabled = on;
    g_toggled = true;
    return 0;
}

static void ApplyToggles() {
    for (auto& m : g_mods) {
        if (m.enabled == m.loaded) continue;
        if (m.enabled) {
            LoadMod(m);
        } else {
            UnloadMod(m);
        }
        lua_pushstring(g_L, m.name.c_str());
        lua_pushboolean(g_L, m.enabled);
        EmitEvent("mod_toggled", 2);
    }
}

static bool g_reloadAll = false;
static int H_reload(lua_State* L) {
    g_reloadAll = true;
    return 0;
}

static int H_mods(lua_State* L) {
    lua_newtable(L);
    int i = 0;
    for (auto& m : g_mods) {
        lua_createtable(L, 0, 7);
        lua_pushstring(L, m.name.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "name");
        lua_pushstring(L, m.version.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "version");
        lua_pushstring(L, m.author.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "author");
        lua_pushstring(L, m.description.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "description");
        lua_pushstring(L, m.category.c_str()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "category");
        lua_pushboolean(L, m.enabled); lua_setfield(L, -2, "enabled");
        lua_pushboolean(L, m.loaded); lua_setfield(L, -2, "loaded");
        lua_pushboolean(L, m.hidden); lua_setfield(L, -2, "hidden");
        lua_pushinteger(L, (lua_Integer)m.settings.size()); lua_setfield(L, -2, "settings");
        lua_rawseti(L, -2, ++i);
    }
    return 1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dev exec folder: drop a .lua file into H7ModEngine\exec\ and it runs on the next tick.
// Output (print/h7.log, return values and errors) goes to logs\engine.log; the file is renamed to .done.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static void RunExecFolder() {
    std::error_code ec;
    for (auto& e : fs::directory_iterator(EnginePath(L"exec"), ec)) {
        if (e.path().extension() != ".lua") continue;
        fs::path file = e.path();
        lua_State* L = g_L;
        Log("exec: running %s", file.filename().string().c_str());
        lua_pushcfunction(L, Traceback);
        if (luaL_loadfile(L, file.string().c_str()) != LUA_OK || lua_pcall(L, 0, LUA_MULTRET, 1) != LUA_OK) {
            Log("exec: ERROR %s", lua_tostring(L, -1));
        } else {
            for (int i = 2; i <= lua_gettop(L); ++i) Log("exec: => %s", luaL_tolstring(L, i, nullptr)), lua_pop(L, 1);
        }
        lua_settop(L, 0);
        fs::path done = file;
        done.replace_extension(".done");
        fs::remove(done, ec);
        fs::rename(file, done, ec);
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State lifetime and per-tick work
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

static int H_print(lua_State* L) {
    int n = lua_gettop(L);
    std::string s;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (i > 1) s += "\t";
        s += luaL_tolstring(L, i, nullptr);
        lua_pop(L, 1);
    }
    int id = CurrentModId();
    Log("%s%s", id > 0 ? ("[" + ModName(id) + "] ").c_str() : "[lua] ", s.c_str());
    return 0;
}

static void CreateState() {
    g_L = luaL_newstate();
    luaL_openlibs(g_L);
    lua_newtable(g_L);
    lua_pushstring(g_L, kVersion);
    lua_setfield(g_L, -2, "version");
    const luaL_Reg fns[] = {
        {"on", H_on}, {"off", H_off}, {"after", H_after}, {"every", H_every}, {"cancel", H_cancel},
        {"reload", H_reload}, {"mods", H_mods}, {"set_mod_enabled", H_set_mod_enabled},
        {"mod_settings", H_mod_settings}, {"set_mod_setting", H_set_mod_setting}, {nullptr, nullptr},
    };
    luaL_setfuncs(g_L, fns, 0);
    lua_setglobal(g_L, "h7");
    lua_register(g_L, "print", H_print);
    OpenBindings(g_L);
    // Shared Lua libraries for all mods: H7ModEngine\lib\?.lua
    std::string libPath = Narrow(EnginePath(L"lib")) + "\\?.lua";
    lua_getglobal(g_L, "package");
    lua_pushstring(g_L, libPath.c_str());
    lua_setfield(g_L, -2, "path");
    lua_pop(g_L, 1);
    Log("Lua %s.%s ready (H7ModEngine %s)", LUA_VERSION_MAJOR, LUA_VERSION_MINOR, kVersion);
}

// Reads an object-typed property (or element 0 of an array property) by name; 0 if missing.
static ue3::UObject ObjectProp(ue3::UObject obj, const char* name) {
    if (!obj || !ue3::IsValidObject(obj)) return 0;
    ue3::UObject prop = ue3::FindField(ue3::ObjClass(obj), name);
    if (!prop) return 0;
    uintptr_t at = obj + ue3::Describe(prop).offset;
    if (ue3::KindOf(prop) == ue3::PropKind::Array) {
        if (mem::ReadOr<int32_t>(at + 8, 0) <= 0) return 0;
        at = mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(at, 0);
    }
    return at ? mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(at, 0) : 0;
}

static std::string CurrentMapName(ue3::UObject viewport) {
    // The map is the outermost package of the local player's WorldInfo
    // (scanning for WorldInfo objects finds stale ones from loaded-but-inactive packages).
    // GamePlayers lives on the GameEngine, which is the viewport client's outer.
    ue3::UObject pc = ObjectProp(ObjectProp(ue3::ObjOuter(viewport), "GamePlayers"), "Actor");
    ue3::UObject top = ObjectProp(pc, "WorldInfo");
    while (ue3::ObjOuter(top)) top = ue3::ObjOuter(top);
    return top ? ue3::ObjName(top) : "";
}

void OnGameTick(float dt, uintptr_t viewport) {
    if (!g_L) {
        CreateState();
        DiscoverAndLoadMods();
    }
    g_clock += dt;

    static double nextSlowCheck = 0;
    if (g_clock >= nextSlowCheck) {
        nextSlowCheck = g_clock + 1.0;
        // Hot reload: reload a mod when any of its .lua files changed.
        for (auto& m : g_mods) {
            if (!m.loaded) continue;
            long long s = NewestLuaStamp(m.dir);
            if (s != m.stamp) {
                Log("Reloading mod %s (files changed)", m.name.c_str());
                UnloadMod(m);
                LoadMod(m);
            }
        }
        static std::string lastMap;
        std::string map = CurrentMapName(viewport);
        if (!map.empty() && map != lastMap) {
            lastMap = map;
            Log("Map: %s", map.c_str());
            lua_pushstring(g_L, map.c_str());
            EmitEvent("map_loaded", 1);
        }
        RunExecFolder();
    }

    if (g_toggled) {
        g_toggled = false;
        ApplyToggles();
    }

    for (auto& m : g_mods) {
        if (!m.pendingReload) continue;
        m.pendingReload = false;
        if (!m.loaded) continue;
        Log("Reloading mod %s (setting changed)", m.name.c_str());
        UnloadMod(m);
        LoadMod(m);
    }

    if (g_reloadAll) {
        g_reloadAll = false;
        Log("Reloading all mods");
        for (auto& m : g_mods) UnloadMod(m);
        DiscoverAndLoadMods();
    }

    RunTimers();
    lua_pushnumber(g_L, dt);
    EmitEvent("tick", 1);
}

} // namespace h7::lua

```

```h
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <cstdint>

struct lua_State;

namespace h7::lua {

// --- runtime.cpp: lifetime, mods, events (game thread only) -------------------------
void OnGameTick(float deltaSeconds, uintptr_t viewport); // driven by the viewport PostRender hook
void EmitEvent(const char* name, int nargs); // pops nargs values pushed on g_L
lua_State* State();

// Ownership tracking so a mod's hooks/handlers/timers are removed when it unloads.
int CurrentModId();
int SetCurrentModId(int id); // returns the previous id
const std::string& ModName(int id);

// Makes `id` the current mod while in scope: logs, hooks, handlers and timers created meanwhile belong to it.
struct ModScope {
    int saved;
    explicit ModScope(int id) : saved(SetCurrentModId(id)) {}
    ~ModScope() { SetCurrentModId(saved); }
};
void Report(const std::string& where, const std::string& error); // logs a Lua error with mod context

// --- bindings.cpp ------------------------------------------------------------------
void OpenBindings(lua_State* L);            // registers the global `h7` table and metatables
void RemoveModHooks(int modId);
void PushObject(lua_State* L, uintptr_t obj);
int Traceback(lua_State* L);                // message handler for lua_pcall

} // namespace h7::lua
```

`src/proxy/proxy.cpp`:

```cpp
// dinput8.dll proxy: the game imports DirectInput8Create, so Windows loads this DLL from the
// game folder. Every export forwards to the real system dinput8.dll.
#include <windows.h>

static HMODULE g_real = nullptr;

static FARPROC Real(const char* name) {
    if (!g_real) {
        wchar_t path[MAX_PATH];
        GetSystemDirectoryW(path, MAX_PATH);
        lstrcatW(path, L"\\dinput8.dll");
        g_real = LoadLibraryW(path);
    }
    return g_real ? GetProcAddress(g_real, name) : nullptr;
}

extern "C" {

HRESULT WINAPI Proxy_DirectInput8Create(HINSTANCE inst, DWORD ver, REFIID riid, LPVOID* out, void* outer) {
    using Fn = HRESULT(WINAPI*)(HINSTANCE, DWORD, REFIID, LPVOID*, void*);
    auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(Real("DirectInput8Create"));
    return fn ? fn(inst, ver, riid, out, outer) : E_FAIL;
}

HRESULT WINAPI Proxy_DllCanUnloadNow() {
    auto fn = reinterpret_cast<HRESULT(WINAPI*)()>(Real("DllCanUnloadNow"));
    return fn ? fn() : S_FALSE;
}

HRESULT WINAPI Proxy_DllGetClassObject(REFCLSID clsid, REFIID riid, LPVOID* out) {
    auto fn = reinterpret_cast<HRESULT(WINAPI*)(REFCLSID, REFIID, LPVOID*)>(Real("DllGetClassObject"));
    return fn ? fn(clsid, riid, out) : E_FAIL;
}

HRESULT WINAPI Proxy_DllRegisterServer() {
    auto fn = reinterpret_cast<HRESULT(WINAPI*)()>(Real("DllRegisterServer"));
    return fn ? fn() : E_FAIL;
}

HRESULT WINAPI Proxy_DllUnregisterServer() {
    auto fn = reinterpret_cast<HRESULT(WINAPI*)()>(Real("DllUnregisterServer"));
    return fn ? fn() : E_FAIL;
}

LPCVOID WINAPI Proxy_GetdfDIJoystick() {
    auto fn = reinterpret_cast<LPCVOID(WINAPI*)()>(Real("GetdfDIJoystick"));
    return fn ? fn() : nullptr;
}

} // extern "C"

```

`src/ue3/core.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "core.h"
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <cstring>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <mutex>

namespace h7::ue3 {

Layout L;

static uintptr_t g_names = 0;   // address of TArray<FNameEntry*>
static uintptr_t g_objects = 0; // address of TArray<UObject*>
static bool g_namesWideOnly = false;

uintptr_t GNamesAddress() { return g_names; }
uintptr_t GObjectsAddress() { return g_objects; }

static bool ReadArray(uintptr_t at, TArrayRaw& a) { return at && mem::Read(at, a); }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GNames discovery
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static int FindAnsi(const uint8_t* buf, size_t n, const char* s) {
    size_t len = strlen(s) + 1; // include terminator
    for (size_t i = 0; i + len <= n; ++i)
        if (memcmp(buf + i, s, len) == 0) return (int)i;
    return -1;
}

static int FindWide(const uint8_t* buf, size_t n, const char* s) {
    uint8_t w[128] = {};
    size_t len = strlen(s);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) w[i * 2] = (uint8_t)s[i];
    size_t wl = (len + 1) * 2;
    for (size_t i = 0; i + wl <= n; i += 2)
        if (memcmp(buf + i, w, wl) == 0) return (int)i;
    return -1;
}

static bool TryNamesCandidate(uintptr_t at, const TArrayRaw& a) {
    if (a.num < 1000 || a.num > 4'000'000 || a.max < a.num || a.max > 8'000'000) return false;
    if (a.data < 0x10000 || (a.data & 7) || mem::g_game.Contains(a.data)) return false;
    uintptr_t e[3];
    if (!mem::Read(reinterpret_cast<void*>(a.data), e, sizeof(e))) return false;
    uint8_t b0[0x48], b1[0x48], b2[0x48];
    if (!mem::Read((void*)e[0], b0, sizeof(b0)) || !mem::Read((void*)e[1], b1, sizeof(b1)) ||
        !mem::Read((void*)e[2], b2, sizeof(b2)))
        return false;
    int k = FindAnsi(b0, sizeof(b0), "None");
    bool wide = false;
    if (k < 0 || FindAnsi(b1, sizeof(b1), "ByteProperty") != k || FindAnsi(b2, sizeof(b2), "IntProperty") != k) {
        k = FindWide(b0, sizeof(b0), "None");
        if (k < 0 || FindWide(b1, sizeof(b1), "ByteProperty") != k) return false;
        wide = true;
    }
    g_names = at;
    L.nameEntryString = k;
    g_namesWideOnly = wide;

    // Find the Index field: (i << 1 | unicode) for the first entries.
    for (int off = 0; off + 4 <= k; off += 4) {
        bool ok = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < 32 && ok; ++i) {
            uintptr_t ent = 0;
            int32_t v = 0;
            ok = mem::Read(a.data + i * 8, ent) && ent && mem::Read(ent + off, v) && (v >> 1) == i;
        }
        if (ok) { L.nameEntryIndex = off; break; }
    }
    Log("GNames @ %p (data %p, num %d, max %d) nameOffset=0x%x indexField=0x%x wide=%d", (void*)at, (void*)a.data,
        a.num, a.max, k, L.nameEntryIndex, (int)wide);
    Log("FNameEntry[0..2]:\n%s%s%s", HexDump(b0, sizeof(b0), e[0]).c_str(), HexDump(b1, sizeof(b1), e[1]).c_str(),
        HexDump(b2, sizeof(b2), e[2]).c_str());
    return true;
}

static uintptr_t ScanModuleForArray(bool (*pred)(uintptr_t, const TArrayRaw&)) {
    uintptr_t found = 0;
    std::vector<uint8_t> buf;
    mem::ForEachRegion(mem::g_game.base, mem::g_game.end, true, [&](uintptr_t base, size_t size) {
        if (found) return;
        buf.resize(size);
        if (!mem::Read((void*)base, buf.data(), size)) return;
        for (size_t o = 0; o + sizeof(TArrayRaw) <= size; o += 8) {
            TArrayRaw a;
            memcpy(&a, buf.data() + o, sizeof(a));
            if (pred(base + o, a)) { found = base + o; return; }
        }
    });
    return found;
}

bool FindGNames() {
    if (g_names) return true;
    return ScanModuleForArray(TryNamesCandidate) != 0;
}

int32_t NameCount() {
    TArrayRaw a{};
    return ReadArray(g_names, a) ? a.num : 0;
}

std::string NameToString(int32_t index) {
    TArrayRaw a{};
    if (!ReadArray(g_names, a) || index < 0 || index >= a.num) return {};
    uintptr_t ent = 0;
    if (!mem::Read(a.data + (uintptr_t)index * 8, ent) || !ent) return {};
    bool wide = g_namesWideOnly;
    if (L.nameEntryIndex >= 0) {
        int32_t v = 0;
        if (mem::Read(ent + L.nameEntryIndex, v)) wide = (v & 1) != 0;
    }
    std::string out;
    uintptr_t p = ent + L.nameEntryString;
    if (wide) {
        wchar_t ch[256];
        if (!mem::Read((void*)p, ch, sizeof(ch))) return {};
        for (int i = 0; i < 256 && ch[i]; ++i) out.push_back(ch[i] < 128 ? (char)ch[i] : '?');
    } else {
        char ch[256];
        if (!mem::Read((void*)p, ch, sizeof(ch))) return {};
        for (int i = 0; i < 256 && ch[i]; ++i) out.push_back(ch[i]);
    }
    return out;
}

std::string NameToString(const FName& n) {
    std::string s = NameToString(n.index);
    if (n.number > 0) s += "_" + std::to_string(n.number - 1);
    return s;
}

static std::mutex g_cacheMutex;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, int32_t> g_nameCache;
static int32_t g_nameCacheCount = 0;
static std::unordered_map<std::string, UObject> g_objectCache;

void InvalidateLookupCache() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_cacheMutex);
    g_objectCache.clear();
}

int32_t FindNameIndex(const std::string& name) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_cacheMutex);
    int32_t count = NameCount();
    if (count != g_nameCacheCount) {
        for (int32_t i = g_nameCacheCount; i < count; ++i) {
            std::string s = NameToString(i);
            if (!s.empty()) g_nameCache.emplace(s, i);
        }
        g_nameCacheCount = count;
    }
    auto it = g_nameCache.find(name);
    if (it != g_nameCache.end()) return it->second;
    // Names are case-insensitive in UE3; fall back to a slow insensitive compare.
    for (auto& [k, v] : g_nameCache)
        if (_stricmp(k.c_str(), name.c_str()) == 0) return v;
    return -1;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GObjects discovery
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static bool TryObjectsCandidate(uintptr_t at, const TArrayRaw& a) {
    if (a.num < 1000 || a.num > 50'000'000 || a.max < a.num || a.max > 100'000'000) return false;
    if (a.data < 0x10000 || (a.data & 7) || mem::g_game.Contains(a.data)) return false;
    uintptr_t objs[256];
    if (!mem::Read((void*)a.data, objs, sizeof(objs))) return false;
    int32_t names = NameCount();
    int good = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 256; ++i) {
        if (!objs[i]) continue;
        uintptr_t vt = 0;
        int32_t idx = -1;
        FName n{};
        if (!mem::Read(objs[i] + L.objVTable, vt) || !mem::g_game.Contains(vt)) return false;
        if (!mem::Read(objs[i] + L.objInternalIndex, idx) || idx != i) return false;
        if (!mem::Read(objs[i] + L.objName, n) || n.index < 0 || n.index >= names) return false;
        ++good;
    }
    if (good < 128) return false;
    Log("GObjects @ %p (data %p, num %d, max %d)", (void*)at, (void*)a.data, a.num, a.max);
    return true;
}

bool FindGObjects() {
    if (g_objects) return true;
    g_objects = ScanModuleForArray(TryObjectsCandidate);
    return g_objects != 0;
}

int32_t ObjectCount() {
    TArrayRaw a{};
    return ReadArray(g_objects, a) ? a.num : 0;
}

UObject ObjectAt(int32_t index) {
    TArrayRaw a{};
    if (!ReadArray(g_objects, a) || index < 0 || index >= a.num) return 0;
    return mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(a.data + (uintptr_t)index * 8, 0);
}

bool IsValidObject(UObject o) {
    if (!o) return false;
    int32_t idx = -1;
    return mem::Read(o + L.objInternalIndex, idx) && ObjectAt(idx) == o;
}

UObject ObjClass(UObject o) { return o ? mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(o + L.objClass, 0) : 0; }
UObject ObjOuter(UObject o) { return o ? mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(o + L.objOuter, 0) : 0; }
FName ObjFName(UObject o) { return o ? mem::ReadOr<FName>(o + L.objName, FName{-1, 0}) : FName{-1, 0}; }
std::string ObjName(UObject o) { return o ? NameToString(ObjFName(o)) : "None"; }

std::string ObjPathName(UObject o) {
    if (!o) return "None";
    std::string s = ObjName(o);
    int guard = 0;
    for (UObject outer = ObjOuter(o); outer && guard < 64; outer = ObjOuter(outer), ++guard)
        s = ObjName(outer) + "." + s;
    return s;
}

std::string ObjFullName(UObject o) {
    if (!o) return "None";
    return ObjName(ObjClass(o)) + " " + ObjPathName(o);
}

bool IsA(UObject o, UObject cls) {
    if (!o || !cls || L.structSuper < 0) return false;
    int guard = 0;
    for (UObject c = ObjClass(o); c && guard < 128; c = mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(c + L.structSuper, 0), ++guard)
        if (c == cls) return true;
    return false;
}

static bool MatchesPath(UObject o, const std::string& path) {
    // Compare from the innermost name outwards to avoid building strings.
    size_t end = path.size();
    UObject cur = o;
    while (cur) {
        size_t dot = path.rfind('.', end - 1);
        size_t start = dot == std::string::npos ? 0 : dot + 1;
        std::string part = path.substr(start, end - start);
        if (_stricmp(ObjName(cur).c_str(), part.c_str()) != 0) return false;
        if (dot == std::string::npos) return ObjOuter(cur) == 0;
        end = dot;
        cur = ObjOuter(cur);
    }
    return false;
}

UObject FindObject(const std::string& query) {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_cacheMutex);
        auto it = g_objectCache.find(query);
        if (it != g_objectCache.end() && IsValidObject(it->second)) return it->second;
    }
    std::string className, path = query;
    size_t sp = query.find(' ');
    if (sp != std::string::npos) {
        className = query.substr(0, sp);
        path = query.substr(sp + 1);
    }
    size_t lastDot = path.rfind('.');
    std::string leaf = lastDot == std::string::npos ? path : path.substr(lastDot + 1);
    // Leaf may carry a _N number suffix; match on the base name index, then verify the full path.
    int32_t leafIdx = FindNameIndex(leaf);
    std::string leafBase = leaf;
    if (leafIdx < 0) {
        size_t us = leaf.rfind('_');
        if (us != std::string::npos) leafIdx = FindNameIndex(leafBase = leaf.substr(0, us));
        if (leafIdx < 0) return 0;
    }
    int32_t count = ObjectCount();
    for (int32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        UObject o = ObjectAt(i);
        if (!o || ObjFName(o).index != leafIdx) continue;
        if (!className.empty() && _stricmp(ObjName(ObjClass(o)).c_str(), className.c_str()) != 0) continue;
        if (!MatchesPath(o, path)) continue;
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(g_cacheMutex);
        g_objectCache[query] = o;
        return o;
    }
    return 0;
}

UObject FindClass(const std::string& path) {
    if (path.find('.') != std::string::npos) return FindObject("Class " + path);
    int32_t idx = FindNameIndex(path);
    if (idx < 0) return 0;
    int32_t count = ObjectCount();
    for (int32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        UObject o = ObjectAt(i);
        if (o && ObjFName(o).index == idx && ObjName(ObjClass(o)) == "Class") return o;
    }
    return 0;
}

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

`src/ue3/core.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "layout.h"

namespace h7::ue3 {

struct TArrayRaw {
    uintptr_t data;
    int32_t num;
    int32_t max;
};

struct FName {
    int32_t index;
    int32_t number;
};

using UObject = uintptr_t; // engine objects are handled as raw addresses + Layout offsets

// --- discovery --------------------------------------------------------------
bool FindGNames();   // locate the global FName table
bool FindGObjects(); // locate the global object array (needs names)
uintptr_t GNamesAddress();
uintptr_t GObjectsAddress();

// --- names ------------------------------------------------------------------
int32_t NameCount();
std::string NameToString(int32_t index);          // raw entry, no number suffix
std::string NameToString(const FName& name);      // with _N suffix like UE3
int32_t FindNameIndex(const std::string& name);   // -1 if absent (cached lookup)

// --- objects ----------------------------------------------------------------
int32_t ObjectCount();
UObject ObjectAt(int32_t index);
UObject ObjClass(UObject o);
UObject ObjOuter(UObject o);
FName ObjFName(UObject o);
std::string ObjName(UObject o);
std::string ObjPathName(UObject o);   // Outer.Outer.Name
std::string ObjFullName(UObject o);   // "Class Outer.Name"
bool IsA(UObject o, UObject cls);
bool IsValidObject(UObject o);

// Lookup by "Class Package.Group.Name" or by path "Package.Group.Name".
UObject FindObject(const std::string& fullOrPath);
UObject FindClass(const std::string& path); // "Core.Object" or "Object"

void InvalidateLookupCache();

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

`src/ue3/dumper.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "dumper.h"
#include "core.h"
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <cstdio>
#include <map>
#include <vector>

namespace h7::ue3 {

static FILE* OpenW(const std::wstring& path) {
    FILE* f = nullptr;
    _wfopen_s(&f, path.c_str(), L"wb");
    return f;
}

void DumpNamesAndObjects(const std::wstring& dir) {
    if (FILE* f = OpenW(dir + L"\\names.txt")) {
        int32_t n = NameCount();
        for (int32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) fprintf(f, "%d %s\n", i, NameToString(i).c_str());
        fclose(f);
        Log("Wrote %d names", n);
    }
    if (FILE* f = OpenW(dir + L"\\objects.txt")) {
        int32_t n = ObjectCount();
        for (int32_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            UObject o = ObjectAt(i);
            if (o) fprintf(f, "%d %016llx %s\n", i, (unsigned long long)o, ObjFullName(o).c_str());
        }
        fclose(f);
        Log("Wrote %d objects", n);
    }
}

void DumpProbe(const std::wstring& dir) {
    FILE* f = OpenW(dir + L"\\probe.txt");
    if (!f) return;
    const char* targets[] = {
        "Class Core.Object", "Class Core.Field", "Class Core.Struct", "Class Core.State", "Class Core.Class",
        "Class Core.Function", "Class Core.Property", "Class Core.BoolProperty",
        "ObjectProperty Core.Object.Outer", "NameProperty Core.Object.Name", "ClassProperty Core.Object.Class",
        "IntProperty Core.Object.NetIndex", "QWordProperty Core.Object.ObjectFlags",
        "BoolProperty Engine.Actor.bHidden", "BoolProperty Engine.Actor.bStatic", "StructProperty Engine.Actor.Location",
        "ArrayProperty Engine.Actor.Attached", "ByteProperty Engine.Actor.Physics", "ScriptStruct Core.Object.Vector",
        "Enum Engine.Actor.EPhysics", "Function Engine.Actor.Touch", "Function Engine.GameViewportClient.Tick",
        "Function Engine.PlayerController.ConsoleCommand", "Function Core.Object.Sin",
        "Function Engine.Actor.SetLocation", "Class MMH7Game.H7Creature", "Class MMH7Game.H7CombatController",
        "Object Core.Default__Object",
    };
    for (const char* t : targets) {
        UObject o = FindObject(t);
        fprintf(f, "=== %s -> %016llx\n", t, (unsigned long long)o);
        if (!o) continue;
        uint8_t buf[0x140];
        if (mem::Read((void*)o, buf, sizeof(buf))) fputs(HexDump(buf, sizeof(buf), o).c_str(), f);
        // Annotate pointer-sized fields that point at known objects.
        for (int off = 0; off < (int)sizeof(buf); off += 8) {
            uintptr_t v = *reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t*>(buf + off);
            if (IsValidObject(v)) fprintf(f, "    +%03x -> %s\n", off, ObjFullName(v).c_str());
            else if (mem::g_game.Contains(v)) fprintf(f, "    +%03x -> game+%llx\n", off, (unsigned long long)(v - mem::g_game.base));
        }
    }

    // UFunction::Func discovery: the most common code pointer across script functions is ProcessInternal.
    std::map<int, std::map<uintptr_t, int>> hist;
    int functions = 0;
    int32_t count = ObjectCount();
    for (int32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        UObject o = ObjectAt(i);
        if (!o || ObjName(ObjClass(o)) != "Function") continue;
        ++functions;
        uint8_t buf[0x140];
        if (!mem::Read((void*)o, buf, sizeof(buf))) continue;
        for (int off = L.objSize; off < (int)sizeof(buf); off += 8) {
            uintptr_t v = *reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t*>(buf + off);
            if (mem::g_game.Contains(v)) hist[off][v]++;
        }
    }
    fprintf(f, "\n=== code-pointer fields across %d UFunctions\n", functions);
    for (auto& [off, vals] : hist) {
        int total = 0, best = 0;
        uintptr_t bestV = 0;
        for (auto& [v, c] : vals) {
            total += c;
            if (c > best) best = c, bestV = v;
        }
        fprintf(f, "  +%03x: %d functions, %zu distinct, top game+%llx x%d (fn start game+%llx)\n", off, total,
                vals.size(), (unsigned long long)(bestV - mem::g_game.base), best,
                (unsigned long long)(mem::FunctionStart(bestV) ? mem::FunctionStart(bestV) - mem::g_game.base : 0));
    }

    // UObject vtable (from the Object CDO) with function starts, for ProcessEvent identification.
    UObject cdo = FindObject("Object Core.Default__Object");
    if (!cdo) cdo = ObjectAt(0);
    uintptr_t vt = mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(cdo + L.objVTable, 0);
    fprintf(f, "\n=== UObject vtable @ game+%llx\n", (unsigned long long)(vt - mem::g_game.base));
    for (int i = 0; i < 128; ++i) {
        uintptr_t fn = mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(vt + i * 8, 0);
        if (!mem::g_game.Contains(fn)) break;
        fprintf(f, "  [%3d] game+%llx\n", i, (unsigned long long)(fn - mem::g_game.base));
    }
    fclose(f);
    Log("Wrote probe.txt");
}

void DumpImage(const std::wstring& path) {
    size_t size = mem::g_game.end - mem::g_game.base;
    std::vector<uint8_t> img(size, 0);
    size_t unreadable = 0;
    for (size_t off = 0; off < size; off += 0x1000) {
        if (!mem::Read((void*)(mem::g_game.base + off), img.data() + off, 0x1000)) ++unreadable;
    }
    auto dos = reinterpret_cast<IMAGE_DOS_HEADER*>(img.data());
    auto nt = reinterpret_cast<IMAGE_NT_HEADERS64*>(img.data() + dos->e_lfanew);
    auto sec = IMAGE_FIRST_SECTION(nt);
    for (int i = 0; i < nt->FileHeader.NumberOfSections; ++i) {
        sec[i].PointerToRawData = sec[i].VirtualAddress;
        sec[i].SizeOfRawData = sec[i].Misc.VirtualSize;
        // The first section holds the unpacked code but is flagged as data; mark it executable.
        if (i == 0) sec[i].Characteristics |= IMAGE_SCN_CNT_CODE | IMAGE_SCN_MEM_EXECUTE;
    }
    nt->OptionalHeader.ImageBase = mem::g_game.base;
    if (FILE* f = OpenW(path)) {
        fwrite(img.data(), 1, img.size(), f);
        fclose(f);
        Log("Dumped image (%zu bytes, %zu unreadable pages)", size, unreadable);
    }
}

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

```h
#pragma once

namespace h7::ue3 {

// Writes names.txt and objects.txt (an "SDK index" modders can grep).
```
