`src/ue3/factory.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "factory.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <cstring>
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include "reflection.h"

namespace h7::ue3 {

namespace {

// RVAs in MMH7Game-Win64-Shipping.exe v2.2.1 (found by disassembling UObject::execNew, GNatives[0x11]).
constexpr uintptr_t kStaticConstructObject = 0x26e13b0;
constexpr uintptr_t kFNameCtor = 0x26cb5c0;
constexpr uintptr_t kGetTransientPackage = 0x26bea20;

// First bytes of each function (from the unpacked image) to make sure we call what we think we call.
const uint8_t kSigSCO[] = {0x48, 0x89, 0xe0, 0x4c, 0x89, 0x40, 0x18, 0x57, 0x41, 0x54, 0x41, 0x55, 0x48, 0x83, 0xec, 0x70};
const uint8_t kSigFName[] = {0x53, 0x48, 0x83, 0xec, 0x30, 0x45, 0x89, 0xc1, 0x45, 0x31, 0xc0, 0x48, 0x89, 0xcb};
const uint8_t kSigTransient[] = {0x48, 0x8b, 0x05};

// UObject* StaticConstructObject(UClass*, UObject* Outer, FName, EObjectFlags, UObject* Template,
//                                FOutputDevice* Error, UObject* SubobjectRoot, FObjectInstancingGraph*)
using SCOFn = UObject(__fastcall*)(UObject, UObject, FName, uint64_t, UObject, void*, UObject, void*);
// FName::FName(const TCHAR* Name, EFindName FindType, UBOOL)
using FNameFn = FName*(__fastcall*)(FName*, const wchar_t*, int, int);
using TransientFn = UObject(__fastcall*)();

bool g_ok = false;
SCOFn g_sco = nullptr;
FNameFn g_fname = nullptr;
TransientFn g_transient = nullptr;

bool Matches(uintptr_t at, const uint8_t* sig, size_t n) {
    uint8_t buf[32];
    return n <= sizeof(buf) && mem::Read(reinterpret_cast<const void*>(at), buf, n) && memcmp(buf, sig, n) == 0;
}

std::wstring Widen(const std::string& s) {
    if (s.empty()) return {};
    int n = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.data(), (int)s.size(), nullptr, 0);
    std::wstring w(n, L'\0');
    MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.data(), (int)s.size(), w.data(), n);
    return w;
}

// SEH wrappers: no C++ objects with destructors in these frames.
bool SafeName(const wchar_t* s, FName* out) {
    __try {
        g_fname(out, s, 1 /*FNAME_Add*/, 1);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

bool SafeConstruct(UObject cls, UObject outer, FName name, uint64_t flags, UObject tmpl, UObject* out) {
    __try {
        // Same as execNew: with a template the subobject root is INVALID_OBJECT (components keep pointing at the
        // template's), without one the engine instances them for the new object.
        UObject root = tmpl ? static_cast<UObject>(-1) : 0;
        *out = g_sco(cls, outer, name, flags, tmpl, nullptr, root, nullptr);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

} // namespace

bool InitFactory() {
    uintptr_t base = mem::g_game.base;
    bool sco = Matches(base + kStaticConstructObject, kSigSCO, sizeof(kSigSCO));
    bool fname = Matches(base + kFNameCtor, kSigFName, sizeof(kSigFName));
    bool trans = Matches(base + kGetTransientPackage, kSigTransient, sizeof(kSigTransient));
    g_ok = sco && fname && trans;
    if (!g_ok) {
        Log("Object factory disabled: unexpected code at StaticConstructObject=%d FName=%d GetTransientPackage=%d",
            sco, fname, trans);
        return false;
    }
    g_sco = reinterpret_cast<SCOFn>(base + kStaticConstructObject);
    g_fname = reinterpret_cast<FNameFn>(base + kFNameCtor);
    g_transient = reinterpret_cast<TransientFn>(base + kGetTransientPackage);
    Log("Object factory ready (StaticConstructObject at game+%llx)", (unsigned long long)kStaticConstructObject);
    return true;
}

bool FactoryAvailable() { return g_ok; }

FName MakeName(const std::string& name) {
    FName out{-1, 0};
    if (!g_ok) return out;
    std::wstring w = Widen(name);
    if (!SafeName(w.c_str(), &out)) out = {-1, 0};
    return out;
}

UObject TransientPackage() {
    if (!g_ok) return 0;
    __try {
        return g_transient();
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return 0;
    }
}

UObject ConstructObject(UObject cls, UObject outer, const std::string& name, uint64_t flags, UObject tmpl,
                        std::string& error) {
    if (!g_ok) return error = "object creation is not available (factory disabled, see engine.log)", 0;
    if (!cls || ObjName(ObjClass(cls)) != "Class") return error = "not a class", 0;
    if (tmpl && !IsA(tmpl, cls)) return error = "template " + ObjFullName(tmpl) + " is not a " + ObjName(cls), 0;
    if (!outer) outer = TransientPackage();
    if (!outer) return error = "no outer (transient package not found)", 0;
    FName fn{0, 0}; // NAME_None: the engine picks a unique name
    if (!name.empty()) {
        fn = MakeName(name);
        if (fn.index < 0) return error = "could not create name '" + name + "'", 0;
    }
    UObject result = 0;
    if (!SafeConstruct(cls, outer, fn, flags, tmpl, &result)) return error = "StaticConstructObject crashed", 0;
    if (!result) return error = "StaticConstructObject returned null", 0;
    if (flags & RF_RootSet) { // AddToRoot: the constructor may not keep it; garbage collection must never free it
        uint64_t f = mem::ReadOr<uint64_t>(result + L.objFlags, 0) | RF_RootSet;
        mem::Write(reinterpret_cast<void*>(result + L.objFlags), &f, sizeof(f));
    }
    InvalidateLookupCache();
    return result;
}

UObject ConstructTopLevel(UObject cls, const std::string& name, uint64_t flags, std::string& error) {
    if (!g_ok) return error = "object creation is not available (factory disabled, see engine.log)", 0;
    FName fn = MakeName(name);
    if (fn.index < 0) return error = "could not create name '" + name + "'", 0;
    UObject result = 0;
    if (!SafeConstruct(cls, 0, fn, flags, 0, &result)) return error = "StaticConstructObject crashed", 0;
    if (!result) return error = "StaticConstructObject returned null", 0;
    InvalidateLookupCache();
    return result;
}

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

`src/ue3/factory.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include "core.h"

// Creating engine objects at runtime: UObject::StaticConstructObject, FName(TCHAR*, FNAME_Add) and
// GetTransientPackage, called directly. The game is final (v2.2.1), so their addresses are fixed RVAs; each is
// checked against its first bytes before use and the feature stays off if anything differs.
namespace h7::ue3 {

// UE3 object flags used by the factory.
constexpr uint64_t RF_Public = 0x0000000400000000ull;
constexpr uint64_t RF_Standalone = 0x0008000000000000ull;
constexpr uint64_t RF_Transient = 0x0000400000000000ull;
constexpr uint64_t RF_ArchetypeObject = 0x400ull;
constexpr uint64_t RF_RootSet = 0x4000ull;

bool InitFactory();       // verifies the function signatures; logs and returns false if they don't match
bool FactoryAvailable();

// Finds or adds a name in the engine name table (FNAME_Add). {-1,0} if the factory is unavailable.
FName MakeName(const std::string& name);

UObject TransientPackage();

// StaticConstructObject(cls, outer or transient package, name ("" = auto), flags, template). Properties are
// initialised from the template (or the class defaults). Returns 0 and sets `error` on failure.
UObject ConstructObject(UObject cls, UObject outer, const std::string& name, uint64_t flags, UObject tmpl,
                        std::string& error);

// A top-level object (no outer), e.g. a UPackage.
UObject ConstructTopLevel(UObject cls, const std::string& name, uint64_t flags, std::string& error);

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

`src/ue3/hooks.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "hooks.h"
#include "reflection.h"
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <intrin.h>
#include <algorithm>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

namespace h7::hooks {

using namespace ue3;

struct Listener {
    int id;
    PreFn pre;
    PostFn post;
    bool removed = false; // set by Remove(); a dispatch already in flight skips it
};

struct HookEntry {
    UObject function;
    bool native;
    bool script;           // hooked through a bytecode trampoline (see "script hooks"), else through Func
    uintptr_t original;    // Func hooks: original UFunction::Func
    uint8_t* stub;         // Func hooks: per-function stub
    TArrayRaw origScript;  // script hooks: the function's own bytecode
    TArrayRaw trampScript; // script hooks: replacement bytecode
    uint8_t* shadow;       // script hooks: copy of the UFunction that keeps origScript
    int bodyStart = 0;     // script hooks: offset of the body in origScript (after the default-parm blocks)
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Listener>> listeners;
};

static std::recursive_mutex g_mutex;
static std::unordered_map<UObject, HookEntry*> g_hooks;
static std::unordered_map<int, UObject> g_listenerFn;
static int g_nextListener = 1;
static int g_frameNode = -1; // FFrame offset of Node (Object follows, then Code, then Locals)

// --- per-function stubs ----------------------------------------------------------
// mov r9, imm64(entry) ; mov rax, imm64(Handler) ; jmp rax
// Func is a UObject member function (this, FFrame&, Result) so r9 is free for the entry pointer.
static void __fastcall Handler(UObject self, uint8_t* stack, void* result, HookEntry* entry);

static uint8_t* AllocStub(HookEntry* entry) {
    static uint8_t* page = nullptr;
    static size_t used = 0;
    const size_t kStub = 32;
    if (!page || used + kStub > 0x10000) {
        page = static_cast<uint8_t*>(VirtualAlloc(nullptr, 0x10000, MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE));
        used = 0;
        if (!page) return nullptr;
    }
    uint8_t* s = page + used;
    used += kStub;
    uintptr_t e = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(entry), h = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(&Handler);
    s[0] = 0x49; s[1] = 0xB9; memcpy(s + 2, &e, 8);   // mov r9, entry
    s[10] = 0x48; s[11] = 0xB8; memcpy(s + 12, &h, 8); // mov rax, Handler
    s[20] = 0xFF; s[21] = 0xE0;                       // jmp rax
    FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), s, kStub);
    return s;
}

// --- ProcessEvent discovery --------------------------------------------------------
// A script event invoked from C++ runs as ProcessEvent -> (this->*Func)(...). The return address of
// our stub therefore lies inside ProcessEvent, which must also be a slot in the object's vtable.

static int g_peCandidate = -1;
static int g_peVotes = 0;

static void ObserveCaller(UObject self, uintptr_t retAddr) {
    if (L.ueProcessEventIndex >= 0) return;
    uintptr_t caller = mem::FunctionStart(retAddr);
    if (!caller) return;
    uintptr_t vt = mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(self, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 200; ++i) {
        uintptr_t f = mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(vt + i * 8, 0);
        if (!mem::g_game.Contains(f)) break;
        if (f != caller) continue;
        g_peVotes = g_peCandidate == i ? g_peVotes + 1 : 1;
        g_peCandidate = i;
        if (g_peVotes >= 3) {
            L.ueProcessEventIndex = i;
            Log("ProcessEvent = vtable[%d] (game+%llx)", i, (unsigned long long)(f - mem::g_game.base));
        }
        return;
    }
}

bool HasProcessEvent() { return L.ueProcessEventIndex >= 0; }

bool ProcessEvent(UObject obj, UObject fn, void* params) {
    if (L.ueProcessEventIndex < 0 || !obj || !fn) return false;
    using PE = void(__fastcall*)(UObject, UObject, void*, void*);
    uintptr_t vt = mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(obj, 0);
    auto pe = reinterpret_cast<PE>(mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>(vt + L.ueProcessEventIndex * 8, 0));
    if (!pe) return false;
    // ProcessEvent rejects functions that have a native opcode index; clear it for the call.
    auto* iNative = reinterpret_cast<uint16_t*>(fn + L.funcFlags + 4);
    uint16_t saved = *iNative;
    *iNative = 0;
    pe(obj, fn, params, nullptr);
    *iNative = saved;
    return true;
}

// --- handler -----------------------------------------------------------------------

// FFrame: FOutputDevice header, then Node, Object, Code, Locals (8 bytes each). Structs are packed to 4,
// so Node's offset is found by matching the function and object being executed.
template <class IsNode>
static bool DiscoverFrame(uint8_t* stack, UObject self, IsNode isNode) {
    if (g_frameNode >= 0) return true;
    auto at = [&](int off) { return mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>((uintptr_t)stack + off, 0); };
    for (int off = 8; off <= 0x40; off += 4) {
        if (isNode(at(off)) && at(off + 8) == self) {
            g_frameNode = off;
            Log("FFrame layout: Node=+%x Object=+%x Code=+%x Locals=+%x", off, off + 8, off + 16, off + 24);
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

static uint8_t* FrameLocals(uint8_t* stack, UObject self, UObject fn) {
    if (!DiscoverFrame(stack, self, [&](uintptr_t v) { return v == fn; })) return nullptr;
    if (mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>((uintptr_t)stack + g_frameNode, 0) != fn) return nullptr;
    return reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(mem::ReadOr<uintptr_t>((uintptr_t)stack + g_frameNode + 24, 0));
}

static void ClearReturnValue(UObject fn, void* result) {
    if (!result) return;
    for (auto& p : FuncParams(fn))
        if (p.flags & CPF_ReturnParm) memset(result, 0, p.elementSize);
}

static void __fastcall Handler(UObject self, uint8_t* stack, void* result, HookEntry* e) {
    using Orig = void(__fastcall*)(UObject, uint8_t*, void*);
    if (!e->native) ObserveCaller(self, reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(_ReturnAddress()));

    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Listener>> listeners;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::recursive_mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        listeners = e->listeners;
    }
    if (listeners.empty()) {
        reinterpret_cast<Orig>(e->original)(self, stack, result);
        return;
    }
    CallContext ctx{self, e->function, e->native ? nullptr : FrameLocals(stack, self, e->function), result, e->native};
    bool block = false;
    for (auto& l : listeners)
        if (l->pre && !l->removed && l->pre(ctx)) block = true;
    if (!block || e->native) reinterpret_cast<Orig>(e->original)(self, stack, result);
    for (auto& l : listeners)
        if (l->post && !l->removed) l->post(ctx);
}

// --- script hooks (bytecode trampoline) ------------------------------------------------
// Script-to-script calls run the callee through ProcessInternal directly, never through UFunction::Func, so a
// Func hook only sees calls made from C++ (ProcessEvent). A script hook instead replaces the function's bytecode
// with
//     EX_Return EX_FinalFunction(g_hookNative) EX_EndFunctionParms
// By the time that runs the engine has parsed the call's arguments into the frame, for script and C++ callers
// alike. g_hookNative is a fake native UFunction (never registered in the object table) whose Func is
// ScriptHookNative: it runs the listeners and executes the original bytecode on a copy of the frame whose Node
// is a shadow copy of the UFunction that still owns the original Script (jump opcodes index Node->Script).
// The return value is written straight into the caller's result buffer.
// (ProcessInternal steps statements until EX_Return, then evaluates one expression into the result, so the
// leading EX_Return makes our call that return expression.)
// A function with optional parameters starts with one entry per optional parameter: an EX_DefaultParmValue block
//     49 <u16 length> <default expression> 15
// or, for a parameter without a default, a lone EX_Nothing (0B). The engine steps one entry per optional
// parameter itself while binding the arguments, before the body. The trampoline keeps those blocks
// in front, and the original body is entered right after them.

constexpr uint8_t EX_Return = 0x04, EX_Nothing = 0x0B, EX_EndFunctionParms = 0x16, EX_FinalFunction = 0x1C,
                  EX_DefaultParmValue = 0x49, EX_EndOfScript = 0x53;
constexpr int kFrameCopy = 0x80;     // covers FFrame (Node at +0x14 .. OutParms) with room to spare
constexpr int kFrameOutParms = 0x28; // FFrame::OutParms relative to Node (EX_LocalOutVariable reads +0x3C)

uint8_t* OutParmAddr(const CallContext& ctx, UObject prop) {
    for (uint8_t* rec = ctx.outParms; rec; rec = *reinterpret_cast<uint8_t**>(rec + 16))
        if (*reinterpret_cast<UObject*>(rec) == prop) return *reinterpret_cast<uint8_t**>(rec + 8);
    return nullptr;
}

static uint8_t* g_hookNative = nullptr;

static void __fastcall ScriptHookNative(UObject self, uint8_t* stack, void* result) {
    if (!DiscoverFrame(stack, self, [](uintptr_t v) {
            std::lock_guard<std::recursive_mutex> lock(g_mutex);
            auto it = g_hooks.find(v);
            return it != g_hooks.end() && it->second->script;
        })) {
        Log("script hook: FFrame layout unknown; call skipped");
        return;
    }
    auto field = [&](int off) -> uintptr_t& { return *reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t*>(stack + g_frameNode + off); };
    UObject fn = field(0);
    uint8_t*& code = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*&>(field(16));
    if (*code == EX_EndFunctionParms) ++code; // P_FINISH: we take no parameters of our own

    HookEntry* e = nullptr;
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Listener>> listeners;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::recursive_mutex> lock(g_mutex);
        auto it = g_hooks.find(fn);
        if (it == g_hooks.end()) return;
        e = it->second;
        listeners = e->listeners;
    }
    CallContext ctx{self, fn, reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(field(24)), result, false,
                    reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(field(kFrameOutParms))};
    bool block = false;
    for (auto& l : listeners)
        if (l->pre && !l->removed && l->pre(ctx)) block = true;
    if (!block) {
        alignas(16) uint8_t frame[kFrameCopy];
        memcpy(frame, stack, kFrameCopy);
        *reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t*>(frame + g_frameNode) = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(e->shadow);
        *reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t*>(frame + g_frameNode + 16) = e->origScript.data + e->bodyStart;
        using Fn = void(__fastcall*)(UObject, uint8_t*, void*);
        reinterpret_cast<Fn>(ProcessInternal())(self, frame, result);
    } else {
        ClearReturnValue(fn, result);
    }
    for (auto& l : listeners)
        if (l->post && !l->removed) l->post(ctx);
}

static bool InitHookNative() {
    if (g_hookNative) return true;
    if (L.structScript < 0 || L.funcSize <= 0 || !ProcessInternal()) return false;
    UObject model = FindObject("Function Core.Object.Sin");
    if (!model) return false;
    auto* f = new uint8_t[L.funcSize];
    memcpy(f, reinterpret_cast<void*>(model), L.funcSize);
    *reinterpret_cast<uint32_t*>(f + L.funcFlags) = FUNC_Final | FUNC_Native;
    *reinterpret_cast<uint16_t*>(f + L.funcFlags + 4) = 0; // iNative
    *reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t*>(f + L.funcNative) = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(&ScriptHookNative);
    g_hookNative = f;
    return true;
}

// Length of the EX_DefaultParmValue blocks at the start of a function's script, or -1 if they don't parse.
static int DefaultParmBlocks(UObject fn, const TArrayRaw& script) {
    int optional = 0;
    for (const PropInfo& p : FuncParams(fn))
        if (p.flags & CPF_OptionalParm) ++optional;
    const uint8_t* s = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(script.data);
    // A parameter without a default (optional int X) has a lone EX_Nothing instead of a block.
    int pos = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < optional; ++i) {
        if (pos < script.num && s[pos] == EX_Nothing) {
            ++pos;
        } else if (pos + 3 <= script.num && s[pos] == EX_DefaultParmValue) {
            pos += 3 + *reinterpret_cast<const uint16_t*>(s + pos + 1);
        } else {
            return -1;
        }
    }
    return pos < script.num ? pos : -1;
}

static bool InitScriptHook(HookEntry* e) {
    if (!mem::Read(e->function + L.structScript, e->origScript) || e->origScript.num <= 0) return false;
    e->bodyStart = DefaultParmBlocks(e->function, e->origScript);
    if (e->bodyStart < 0) {
        Log("script hook: cannot parse the default parameter values of %s", ObjPathName(e->function).c_str());
        return false;
    }
    e->shadow = new uint8_t[L.funcSize];
    memcpy(e->shadow, reinterpret_cast<void*>(e->function), L.funcSize);
    uint8_t call[] = {EX_Return, EX_FinalFunction, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, EX_EndFunctionParms,
                      EX_Return, EX_Nothing, EX_EndOfScript};
    uintptr_t native = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(g_hookNative);
    memcpy(call + 2, &native, 8);
    int size = e->bodyStart + (int)sizeof(call);
    auto* data = static_cast<uint8_t*>(EngineRealloc(nullptr, size)); // the engine frees Script with its allocator
    if (!data) return false;
    memcpy(data, reinterpret_cast<void*>(e->origScript.data), e->bodyStart);
    memcpy(data + e->bodyStart, call, sizeof(call));
    e->trampScript = {reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(data), size, size};
    return true;
}

// Script hooks are only installed/removed from Lua, i.e. on the game thread between script calls.
static void SetScript(HookEntry* e, bool hooked) {
    const TArrayRaw& s = hooked ? e->trampScript : e->origScript;
    mem::Write(reinterpret_cast<void*>(e->function + L.structScript), &s, sizeof(s));
}

// --- listeners -----------------------------------------------------------------------

int Add(UObject fn, PreFn pre, PostFn post, bool viaFunc) {
    std::lock_guard<std::recursive_mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    uintptr_t* slot = FuncNativePtr(fn);
    if (!slot || !IsValidObject(fn)) return 0;
    HookEntry*& e = g_hooks[fn];
    if (!e) {
        uint32_t flags = FuncFlags(fn);
        bool native = (flags & FUNC_Native) != 0;
        e = new HookEntry{fn, native};
        e->script = !native && !viaFunc && !(flags & (FUNC_Latent | FUNC_Iterator)) && InitHookNative() &&
                    InitScriptHook(e);
        if (!e->script) {
            uintptr_t original = *slot;
            // Script functions are bound to ProcessInternal lazily; an unbound one can use it directly.
            if (!original && !native) original = ProcessInternal();
            e->original = original;
            e->stub = original ? AllocStub(e) : nullptr;
            if (!e->stub) {
                Log("cannot hook %s: function is not bound yet", ObjPathName(fn).c_str());
                delete e;
                g_hooks.erase(fn);
                return 0;
            }
            if (!native && !viaFunc)
                Log("warning: %s is hooked through Func; only calls from C++ will be seen", ObjPathName(fn).c_str());
        }
        // Probe events (Tick, Touch, ...) declared without a body are filtered out by ProcessEvent.
        if (!native && (flags & FUNC_Event) && !(flags & FUNC_Defined))
            Log("warning: %s has no script body; if it is a probe event the engine never calls it",
                ObjPathName(fn).c_str());
    }
    int id = g_nextListener++;
    e->listeners.push_back(std::make_shared<Listener>(Listener{id, std::move(pre), std::move(post)}));
    g_listenerFn[id] = fn;
    if (e->script)
        SetScript(e, true);
    else // Aligned 8-byte store: safe even if the game thread is calling this function right now.
        InterlockedExchange64(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG64*>(slot), reinterpret_cast<LONG64>(e->stub));
    return id;
}

void Maintain() {
    std::lock_guard<std::recursive_mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    for (auto& [fn, e] : g_hooks) {
        if (e->listeners.empty() || e->script) continue;
        uintptr_t* slot = FuncNativePtr(fn);
        uintptr_t cur = *slot;
        if (cur == reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(e->stub)) continue;
        // The engine (re)bound this function after we hooked it; adopt the new target and re-hook.
        if (cur) e->original = cur;
        InterlockedExchange64(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG64*>(slot), reinterpret_cast<LONG64>(e->stub));
        Log("re-applied hook on %s", ObjPathName(fn).c_str());
    }
}

void Remove(int id) {
    std::lock_guard<std::recursive_mutex> lock(g_mutex);
    auto it = g_listenerFn.find(id);
    if (it == g_listenerFn.end()) return;
    HookEntry* e = g_hooks[it->second];
    g_listenerFn.erase(it);
    auto& ls = e->listeners;
    ls.erase(std::remove_if(ls.begin(), ls.end(), [&](auto& l) {
                 if (l->id != id) return false;
                 l->removed = true;
                 return true;
             }),
             ls.end());
    if (ls.empty()) {
        // Restore the original; the entry, stub and shadow stay allocated in case a call is in flight.
        if (e->script)
            SetScript(e, false);
        else
            InterlockedExchange64(reinterpret_cast<volatile LONG64*>(FuncNativePtr(e->function)), (LONG64)e->original);
    }
}

} // namespace h7::hooks

```

`src/ue3/hooks.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include "core.h"
#include <functional>

namespace h7::hooks {

struct CallContext {
    ue3::UObject self;
    ue3::UObject function;
    uint8_t* locals;  // parameter/local storage for script functions; null for natives
    void* result;     // where the return value is written (may be null)
    bool native;      // function is implemented in C++ (params not readable)
    // Script hooks: the frame's FOutParmRec list {UProperty*, PropAddr, Next}. On script-to-script calls an out
    // parameter lives at the caller (PropAddr), not in locals.
    uint8_t* outParms = nullptr;
};

// Address of out parameter `prop` in the call, or null if it is not in the list.
uint8_t* OutParmAddr(const CallContext& ctx, ue3::UObject prop);

// pre returns true to skip the original (ignored for native functions, which must consume
// their parameters from the caller's bytecode).
using PreFn = std::function<bool(CallContext&)>;
using PostFn = std::function<void(CallContext&)>;

// Adds a listener to an engine function (no code patching; all listeners of a function share one hook).
// Script functions are hooked by replacing their bytecode with a trampoline, which sees every call; this must
// happen on the game thread. Native functions (and script functions with viaFunc) are hooked by swapping
// UFunction::Func to a per-function stub, which for script functions only sees calls made from C++.
// Returns a listener id (> 0), or 0 on failure.
int Add(ue3::UObject fn, PreFn pre, PostFn post, bool viaFunc = false);
void Remove(int listenerId);
// Re-applies hooks whose UFunction::Func was re-bound by the engine. Called every frame.
void Maintain();

// ProcessEvent: discovered automatically from the first hooked script event invoked by C++.
bool HasProcessEvent();
// Calls `fn` on `obj` with a params buffer laid out by the function's properties.
bool ProcessEvent(ue3::UObject obj, ue3::UObject fn, void* params);

} // namespace h7::hooks

```

`src/ue3/layout.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>

// Memory layout of the engine's UE3 core types (64-bit, package version 868).
// UObject offsets come from the game's own Object.uc (native var order, x64 alignment)
// and are verified at runtime. Offsets marked "calibrated" are discovered at startup.
namespace h7::ue3 {

struct Layout {
    // FNameEntry
    int nameEntryIndex = -1;     // calibrated: int holding (index << 1) | bUnicode
    int nameEntryString = -1;    // calibrated: start of the inline name characters

    // UObject
    int objVTable = 0x00;
    int objFlags = 0x10;
    int objInternalIndex = 0x38; // == index into GObjects
    int objOuter = 0x40;
    int objName = 0x48;          // FName { int Index; int Number; }
    int objClass = 0x50;
    int objArchetype = 0x58;
    int objSize = 0x60;

    // UField
    int fieldNext = 0x60;

    // UStruct / UFunction / UProperty: calibrated (see Calibrate*), -1 until known
    int structSuper = -1;
    int structChildren = -1;
    int structPropertySize = -1;
    int structScript = -1;       // TArray<BYTE> bytecode (4-byte packed, so not 8-aligned)

    int funcFlags = -1;
    int funcNative = -1;         // UFunction::Func (pointer-to-member, 8 bytes on x64 single inheritance)
    int funcSize = -1;           // sizeof(UFunction)
    int funcParmsSize = -1;
    int funcReturnOffset = -1;

    int propArrayDim = -1;
    int propElementSize = -1;
    int propFlags = -1;          // uint64
    int propOffset = -1;

    int boolBitMask = -1;        // UBoolProperty
    int objPropClass = -1;       // UObjectProperty::PropertyClass
    int structPropStruct = -1;   // UStructProperty::Struct
    int arrayPropInner = -1;     // UArrayProperty::Inner
    int bytePropEnum = -1;       // UByteProperty::Enum

    int ueProcessEventIndex = -1; // UObject vtable slot of ProcessEvent
};

extern Layout L;

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

`src/ue3/reflection.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "reflection.h"
#include "../core/log.h"
#include "../core/memory.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include <map>
#include <unordered_map>

namespace h7::ue3 {

static bool g_calibrated = false;
static int g_classDefaultObject = -1;
static int g_enumNames = -1;

bool IsCalibrated() { return g_calibrated; }

template <class T>
static T Rd(uintptr_t a, int off, T fallback = T{}) {
    return off < 0 ? fallback : mem::ReadOr<T>(a + off, fallback);
}

UObject StructSuper(UObject s) { return Rd<uintptr_t>(s, L.structSuper); }
UObject StructChildren(UObject s) { return Rd<uintptr_t>(s, L.structChildren); }
UObject FieldNext(UObject f) { return Rd<uintptr_t>(f, L.fieldNext); }
int32_t StructSize(UObject s) { return Rd<int32_t>(s, L.structPropertySize); }

uint32_t FuncFlags(UObject fn) { return Rd<uint32_t>(fn, L.funcFlags); }
uint16_t FuncNativeIndex(UObject fn) { return Rd<uint16_t>(fn, L.funcFlags >= 0 ? L.funcFlags + 4 : -1); }
uint16_t FuncParmsSize(UObject fn) { return Rd<uint16_t>(fn, L.funcParmsSize); }
uint16_t FuncReturnOffset(UObject fn) { return Rd<uint16_t>(fn, L.funcReturnOffset); }
uintptr_t* FuncNativePtr(UObject fn) { return L.funcNative < 0 ? nullptr : reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t*>(fn + L.funcNative); }

UObject ClassDefaultObject(UObject cls) { return Rd<uintptr_t>(cls, g_classDefaultObject); }

void* EngineRealloc(void* p, size_t n) {
    using Fn = void*(__cdecl*)(void*, size_t);
    static Fn fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleW(L"tbbmalloc.dll"), "scalable_realloc"));
    return fn ? fn(p, n) : nullptr;
}

static std::unordered_map<UObject, PropKind> g_kindCache;

PropKind KindOf(UObject prop) {
    UObject cls = ObjClass(prop);
    auto it = g_kindCache.find(cls);
    if (it != g_kindCache.end()) return it->second;
    static const std::map<std::string, PropKind> kinds = {
        {"ByteProperty", PropKind::Byte},         {"IntProperty", PropKind::Int},
        {"BoolProperty", PropKind::Bool},         {"FloatProperty", PropKind::Float},
        {"ObjectProperty", PropKind::Object},     {"ClassProperty", PropKind::Class},
        {"NameProperty", PropKind::Name},         {"StrProperty", PropKind::Str},
        {"StructProperty", PropKind::Struct},     {"ArrayProperty", PropKind::Array},
        {"DelegateProperty", PropKind::Delegate}, {"InterfaceProperty", PropKind::Interface},
        {"MapProperty", PropKind::Map},           {"QWordProperty", PropKind::QWord},
        {"ComponentProperty", PropKind::Component},
        // Heroes VII's lazily loaded object reference (Core.DynLoadObjectProperty, e.g. H7Faction.mStartTown): an
        // ObjectProperty subclass whose value is the object pointer (none until the game's getter loads it).
        {"DynLoadObjectProperty", PropKind::Object},
    };
    auto k = kinds.find(ObjName(cls));
    PropKind kind = k == kinds.end() ? PropKind::Unknown : k->second;
    g_kindCache[cls] = kind;
    return kind;
}

static int g_propClassSize = -1; // sizeof(UProperty): start of subclass fields

UObject PropInner(UObject prop) { return Rd<uintptr_t>(prop, g_propClassSize); }
uint32_t BoolMask(UObject prop) { return Rd<uint32_t>(prop, L.boolBitMask); }

PropInfo Describe(UObject prop) {
    PropInfo p;
    p.prop = prop;
    p.kind = KindOf(prop);
    p.name = ObjName(prop);
    p.offset = Rd<int32_t>(prop, L.propOffset);
    p.arrayDim = Rd<int32_t>(prop, L.propArrayDim, 1);
    p.elementSize = Rd<int32_t>(prop, L.propElementSize);
    p.flags = Rd<uint64_t>(prop, L.propFlags);
    return p;
}

std::vector<UObject> Fields(UObject s, bool includeSupers) {
    std::vector<UObject> out;
    int guard = 0;
    for (UObject cur = s; cur && guard < 256; cur = includeSupers ? StructSuper(cur) : 0, ++guard) {
        int n = 0;
        for (UObject f = StructChildren(cur); f && n < 20000; f = FieldNext(f), ++n) out.push_back(f);
    }
    return out;
}

UObject FindField(UObject s, const std::string& name) {
    int32_t idx = FindNameIndex(name);
    if (idx < 0) return 0;
    int guard = 0;
    for (UObject cur = s; cur && guard < 256; cur = StructSuper(cur), ++guard) {
        int n = 0;
        for (UObject f = StructChildren(cur); f && n < 20000; f = FieldNext(f), ++n)
            if (ObjFName(f).index == idx) return f;
    }
    return 0;
}

std::vector<PropInfo> FuncParams(UObject fn) {
    std::vector<PropInfo> out;
    for (UObject f : Fields(fn, false)) {
        if (KindOf(f) == PropKind::Unknown) continue;
        PropInfo p = Describe(f);
        if (p.flags & CPF_Parm) out.push_back(p);
    }
    return out;
}

std::vector<std::string> EnumNames(UObject e) {
    std::vector<std::string> out;
    TArrayRaw a{};
    if (g_enumNames < 0 || !mem::Read(e + g_enumNames, a) || a.num < 0 || a.num > 4096) return out;
    for (int i = 0; i < a.num; ++i) out.push_back(NameToString(mem::ReadOr<FName>(a.data + i * 8, FName{0, 0})));
    return out;
}

std::string ReadFString(uintptr_t at) {
    TArrayRaw a{};
    if (!mem::Read(at, a) || !a.data || a.num <= 0 || a.num > 1 << 20) return {};
    std::wstring w(a.num, L'\0');
    if (!mem::Read((void*)a.data, w.data(), a.num * sizeof(wchar_t))) return {};
    if (!w.empty() && w.back() == L'\0') w.pop_back();
    std::string s;
    // UTF-16 -> UTF-8
    for (size_t i = 0; i < w.size(); ++i) {
        uint32_t c = w[i];
        if (c >= 0xD800 && c < 0xDC00 && i + 1 < w.size()) c = 0x10000 + ((c - 0xD800) << 10) + (w[++i] - 0xDC00);
        if (c < 0x80) s += (char)c;
        else if (c < 0x800) s += (char)(0xC0 | c >> 6), s += (char)(0x80 | (c & 0x3F));
        else if (c < 0x10000) s += (char)(0xE0 | c >> 12), s += (char)(0x80 | (c >> 6 & 0x3F)), s += (char)(0x80 | (c & 0x3F));
        else s += (char)(0xF0 | c >> 18), s += (char)(0x80 | (c >> 12 & 0x3F)), s += (char)(0x80 | (c >> 6 & 0x3F)), s += (char)(0x80 | (c & 0x3F));
    }
    return s;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Calibration
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#define REQUIRE(cond, what)                                   \
    if (!(cond)) {                                            \
        Log("Calibration failed: %s", what);                  \
        return false;                                         \
    }

template <class T, class Pred>
static int FindOffset(int from, int to, int step, Pred pred) {
    for (int off = from; off + (int)sizeof(T) <= to; off += step)
        if (pred(off)) return off;
    return -1;
}

static UObject ChildNamed(UObject s, const char* name) {
    int32_t idx = FindNameIndex(name);
    for (UObject f = StructChildren(s); f; f = FieldNext(f))
        if (ObjFName(f).index == idx) return f;
    return 0;
}

bool Calibrate() {
    if (g_calibrated) return true;
    UObject cObject = FindObject("Class Core.Object");
    UObject cField = FindObject("Class Core.Field");
    UObject cStruct = FindObject("Class Core.Struct");
    UObject cProperty = FindObject("Class Core.Property");
    UObject cActor = FindObject("Class Engine.Actor");
    REQUIRE(cObject && cField && cStruct && cProperty && cActor, "core classes not found");

    // UStruct::SuperField — Field's super is Object.
    L.structSuper = FindOffset<uintptr_t>(L.objSize, 0xd0, 8, [&](int o) {
        return Rd<uintptr_t>(cField, o) == cObject && Rd<uintptr_t>(cActor, o) == cObject;
    });
    REQUIRE(L.structSuper >= 0, "UStruct::SuperField");

    // UStruct::PropertySize — sizeof(UObject) and sizeof(UField).
    L.structPropertySize = FindOffset<int32_t>(L.objSize, 0xd0, 4, [&](int o) {
        return Rd<int32_t>(cObject, o) == L.objSize && Rd<int32_t>(cField, o) == L.objSize + 8;
    });
    REQUIRE(L.structPropertySize >= 0, "UStruct::PropertySize");
    g_propClassSize = StructSize(cProperty);

    // UStruct::Children — first field whose Outer is the struct itself.
    L.structChildren = FindOffset<uintptr_t>(L.objSize, 0xd0, 8, [&](int o) {
        if (o == L.structSuper) return false;
        UObject a = Rd<uintptr_t>(cActor, o), b = Rd<uintptr_t>(cObject, o);
        return IsValidObject(a) && ObjOuter(a) == cActor && IsValidObject(b) && ObjOuter(b) == cObject;
    });
    REQUIRE(L.structChildren >= 0, "UStruct::Children");

    // UProperty: Object.Outer/Name/Class live at 0x40/0x48/0x50 and are 8 bytes each.
    UObject pOuter = ChildNamed(cObject, "Outer"), pName = ChildNamed(cObject, "Name"), pClass = ChildNamed(cObject, "Class");
    REQUIRE(pOuter && pName && pClass, "Object.Outer/Name/Class properties");
    int lo = L.fieldNext + 8, hi = g_propClassSize;
    L.propOffset = FindOffset<int32_t>(lo, hi, 4, [&](int o) {
        return Rd<int32_t>(pOuter, o) == L.objOuter && Rd<int32_t>(pName, o) == L.objName && Rd<int32_t>(pClass, o) == L.objClass;
    });
    L.propArrayDim = FindOffset<int32_t>(lo, hi, 4, [&](int o) {
        return Rd<int32_t>(pOuter, o) == 1 && Rd<int32_t>(pName, o) == 1 && Rd<int32_t>(pClass, o) == 1;
    });
    L.propElementSize = FindOffset<int32_t>(lo, hi, 4, [&](int o) {
        return o != L.propOffset && Rd<int32_t>(pOuter, o) == 8 && Rd<int32_t>(pName, o) == 8 && Rd<int32_t>(pClass, o) == 8;
    });
    REQUIRE(L.propOffset >= 0 && L.propArrayDim >= 0 && L.propElementSize >= 0, "UProperty Offset/ArrayDim/ElementSize");

    // Functions with known signatures from Actor.uc:
    //   event Touch(Actor Other, PrimitiveComponent OtherComp, vector HitLocation, vector HitNormal)  -> 4 parms, 0x28 bytes
    //   native(267) final function bool SetLocation(vector NewLocation)                                -> 2 parms, 0x10 bytes, ret @0xc
    UObject fTouch = ChildNamed(cActor, "Touch"), fSetLoc = ChildNamed(cActor, "SetLocation");
    REQUIRE(fTouch && fSetLoc, "Actor.Touch / Actor.SetLocation");

    // PropertyFlags: every Touch child is a parm (CPF_Parm), Object.Outer is not.
    L.propFlags = FindOffset<uint64_t>(lo, hi, 8, [&](int o) {
        if (Rd<uint64_t>(pOuter, o) & CPF_Parm) return false;
        int n = 0;
        for (UObject f = StructChildren(fTouch); f; f = FieldNext(f), ++n)
            if (!(Rd<uint64_t>(f, o) & CPF_Parm)) return false;
        return n == 4;
    });
    REQUIRE(L.propFlags >= 0, "UProperty::PropertyFlags");

    int fStart = StructSize(cStruct), fEnd = StructSize(FindObject("Class Core.Function"));
    L.funcFlags = FindOffset<uint32_t>(fStart, fEnd, 4, [&](int o) {
        uint32_t t = Rd<uint32_t>(fTouch, o), s = Rd<uint32_t>(fSetLoc, o);
        return (t & FUNC_Event) && !(t & FUNC_Native) && (s & (FUNC_Native | FUNC_Final)) == (FUNC_Native | FUNC_Final);
    });
    L.funcParmsSize = FindOffset<uint16_t>(fStart, fEnd, 2, [&](int o) {
        return Rd<uint16_t>(fTouch, o) == 0x28 && Rd<uint16_t>(fSetLoc, o) == 0x10;
    });
    L.funcReturnOffset = FindOffset<uint16_t>(fStart, fEnd, 2, [&](int o) {
        return o != L.funcParmsSize && Rd<uint16_t>(fSetLoc, o) == 0xc;
    });
    REQUIRE(L.funcFlags >= 0 && L.funcParmsSize >= 0 && L.funcReturnOffset >= 0, "UFunction flags/parms");
    if (Rd<uint16_t>(fSetLoc, L.funcFlags + 4) != 267) Log("warning: UFunction::iNative not at FunctionFlags+4");

    // UFunction::Func is the last member (8-byte pointer-to-member). It is bound lazily while packages
    // link, so its value is checked later in CalibrateFunctionPointers().
    L.funcNative = fEnd - 8;
    UObject fSin = FindObject("Function Core.Object.Sin");
    REQUIRE(fSin && mem::g_game.Contains(Rd<uintptr_t>(fSin, L.funcNative)), "UFunction::Func (Object.Sin not bound)");

    L.funcSize = fEnd;

    // UStruct::Script — bytecode TArray<BYTE>. Touch has an empty body: EX_Return EX_Nothing EX_EndOfScript.
    L.structScript = FindOffset<uintptr_t>(L.structPropertySize + 4, fStart, 4, [&](int o) {
        TArrayRaw a{};
        return mem::Read(fTouch + o, a) && a.num == 3 && mem::ReadOr<uint32_t>(a.data, 0) << 8 == 0x530b0400;
    });
    if (L.structScript < 0) Log("warning: UStruct::Script not found; script-to-script calls can't be hooked");

    // Subclass fields start right after UProperty.
    L.boolBitMask = L.objPropClass = L.structPropStruct = L.arrayPropInner = L.bytePropEnum = g_propClassSize;
    UObject bHidden = ChildNamed(cActor, "bHidden");
    uint32_t mask = bHidden ? BoolMask(bHidden) : 0;
    if (!mask || (mask & (mask - 1))) Log("warning: BoolProperty bitmask looks wrong (%08x)", mask);

    // UClass::ClassDefaultObject — pointer to Default__<ClassName>. Engine structs are packed to 4 bytes,
    // so pointers are not necessarily 8-aligned.
    UObject cdoActor = FindObject("Actor Engine.Default__Actor");
    g_classDefaultObject = FindOffset<uintptr_t>(fEnd, StructSize(FindObject("Class Core.Class")), 4,
                                                 [&](int o) { return cdoActor && Rd<uintptr_t>(cActor, o) == cdoActor; });
    if (g_classDefaultObject < 0) Log("warning: UClass::ClassDefaultObject not found");

    // UEnum::Names — TArray<FName> right after UField.
    if (UObject ePhys = FindObject("Enum Engine.Actor.EPhysics")) {
        for (int o = L.fieldNext + 8; o < 0x90 && g_enumNames < 0; o += 8) {
            TArrayRaw a{};
            if (mem::Read(ePhys + o, a) && a.num > 0 && a.num < 64 &&
                NameToString(mem::ReadOr<FName>(a.data, FName{0, 0})) == "PHYS_None")
                g_enumNames = o;
        }
    }

    Log("Calibrated: Struct{Super=%x Children=%x Size=%x} Prop{ArrayDim=%x ElemSize=%x Flags=%x Offset=%x sub=%x} "
        "Func{Flags=%x Parms=%x Ret=%x Func=%x Script=%x} Class{CDO=%x} Enum{Names=%x}",
        L.structSuper, L.structChildren, L.structPropertySize, L.propArrayDim, L.propElementSize, L.propFlags,
        L.propOffset, g_propClassSize, L.funcFlags, L.funcParmsSize, L.funcReturnOffset, L.funcNative,
        L.structScript, g_classDefaultObject, g_enumNames);
    g_calibrated = true;
    return true;
}

static uintptr_t g_processInternal = 0;
uintptr_t ProcessInternal() { return g_processInternal; }

bool CalibrateFunctionPointers() {
    // Script (non-native) functions all bind Func to UObject::ProcessInternal: take the most common value.
    std::map<uintptr_t, int> hist;
    int script = 0, unbound = 0;
    UObject cFunction = FindObject("Class Core.Function");
    for (int32_t i = 0, n = ObjectCount(); i < n; ++i) {
        UObject o = ObjectAt(i);
        if (!o || ObjClass(o) != cFunction || (FuncFlags(o) & FUNC_Native)) continue;
        ++script;
        uintptr_t v = Rd<uintptr_t>(o, L.funcNative);
        if (mem::g_game.Contains(v)) hist[v]++;
        else ++unbound;
    }
    int best = 0;
    for (auto& [v, c] : hist)
        if (c > best) best = c, g_processInternal = v;
    Log("Script functions: %d, bound to ProcessInternal(game+%llx): %d, unbound: %d, other: %d", script,
        (unsigned long long)(g_processInternal - mem::g_game.base), best, unbound, script - best - unbound);
    return g_processInternal != 0;
}

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

`src/ue3/reflection.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include "core.h"
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace h7::ue3 {

// Property flags (UE3)
constexpr uint64_t CPF_OptionalParm = 0x10;
constexpr uint64_t CPF_Parm = 0x80;
constexpr uint64_t CPF_OutParm = 0x100;
constexpr uint64_t CPF_ReturnParm = 0x400;

// Function flags (UE3)
constexpr uint32_t FUNC_Final = 0x1;
constexpr uint32_t FUNC_Defined = 0x2;
constexpr uint32_t FUNC_Iterator = 0x4;
constexpr uint32_t FUNC_Latent = 0x8;
constexpr uint32_t FUNC_Native = 0x400;
constexpr uint32_t FUNC_Event = 0x800;
constexpr uint32_t FUNC_Static = 0x2000;

enum class PropKind {
    Unknown, Byte, Int, Bool, Float, Object, Class, Name, Str, Struct, Array, Delegate, Interface, Map, QWord, Component
};

struct PropInfo {
    UObject prop = 0;
    PropKind kind = PropKind::Unknown;
    std::string name;
    int32_t offset = 0;
    int32_t arrayDim = 1;
    int32_t elementSize = 0;
    uint64_t flags = 0;
};

// Run after script packages are loaded; fills Layout L. Returns false if a required offset is missing.
bool Calibrate();
bool IsCalibrated();
// Run once the engine is fully initialised (functions bound): finds UObject::ProcessInternal.
bool CalibrateFunctionPointers();
uintptr_t ProcessInternal();

// UStruct / UField
UObject StructSuper(UObject s);
UObject StructChildren(UObject s);
UObject FieldNext(UObject f);
int32_t StructSize(UObject s);

// UProperty
PropKind KindOf(UObject prop);
PropInfo Describe(UObject prop);
UObject PropInner(UObject prop);        // Object/Class: PropertyClass, Struct: Struct, Array: Inner, Byte: Enum
uint32_t BoolMask(UObject prop);

// Finds a property/function by name in a struct and its supers (case-insensitive).
UObject FindField(UObject structObj, const std::string& name);
std::vector<UObject> Fields(UObject structObj, bool includeSupers);

// UFunction
uint32_t FuncFlags(UObject fn);
uint16_t FuncNativeIndex(UObject fn);
uint16_t FuncParmsSize(UObject fn);
uint16_t FuncReturnOffset(UObject fn);
uintptr_t* FuncNativePtr(UObject fn);   // address of UFunction::Func
std::vector<PropInfo> FuncParams(UObject fn); // CPF_Parm properties in order (incl. return)

// UClass
UObject ClassDefaultObject(UObject cls);

// UEnum
std::vector<std::string> EnumNames(UObject enumObj);

// FString helpers
std::string ReadFString(uintptr_t at);

// Engine allocator (GMalloc is backed by tbbmalloc in this build): use for any memory the engine may free.
void* EngineRealloc(void* p, size_t n);

} // namespace h7::ue3

```

`tests/click.ps1`:

```ps1
# Clicks the game window at a position given in window pixels (as in shot.ps1 screenshots), or drags from there:
#   powershell -File click.ps1 <x> <y> [<to x> <to y>]
# Brings the game to the front first. Used by in-game UI tests that need real mouse input.
param([int]$X, [int]$Y, [int]$ToX = -1, [int]$ToY = -1)
Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class Mouse {
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr hWnd, out RECT rect);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetProcessDPIAware();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void mouse_event(uint flags, uint dx, uint dy, uint data, UIntPtr extra);
}
"@
[Mouse]::SetProcessDPIAware() | Out-Null
$game = Get-Process MMH7Game-Win64-Shipping -ErrorAction Stop | Where-Object { $_.MainWindowHandle -ne 0 } | Select-Object -First 1
$r = New-Object Mouse+RECT
[Mouse]::GetWindowRect($game.MainWindowHandle, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
[Mouse]::SetForegroundWindow($game.MainWindowHandle) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300
# The game follows the mouse through raw input, which SetCursorPos doesn't produce: nudge it with relative moves.
[Mouse]::SetCursorPos($r.Left + $X, $r.Top + $Y) | Out-Null
[Mouse]::mouse_event(0x1, 1, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero)
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
[Mouse]::mouse_event(0x1, [uint32]4294967295, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero) # -1
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150
[Mouse]::mouse_event(0x2, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero) # left down
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 60
if ($ToX -ge 0) {
    # Absolute moves over the whole virtual desktop (MOVE | VIRTUALDESK | ABSOLUTE, normalized to 0..65535) reach
    # the game as mouse input; the game window may be on a monitor left of or above the primary one.
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
    $vs = [System.Windows.Forms.SystemInformation]::VirtualScreen
    for ($i = 1; $i -le 20; $i++) {
        $px = $r.Left + $X + ($ToX - $X) * $i / 20; $py = $r.Top + $Y + ($ToY - $Y) * $i / 20
        $nx = [uint32](($px - $vs.Left) * 65535 / ($vs.Width - 1)); $ny = [uint32](($py - $vs.Top) * 65535 / ($vs.Height - 1))
        [Mouse]::mouse_event(0xC001, $nx, $ny, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero)
        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 40
    }
}
[Mouse]::mouse_event(0x4, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero) # left up

```

`tests/daytrace.lua`:

```lua
-- Call tracer for the day-change and damage code paths: copy into <Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\ on an adventure map,
-- then end a day and fight a manual battle. Every 3s logs which traced functions ran since the last report.
local function T(fmt, ...) h7.log("[trace] " .. fmt:format(...)) end
local NATIVE, ITERATOR, LATENT, DEFINED, DELEGATE = 0x400, 0x4, 0x8, 0x2, 0x100000

local classes = { "MMH7Game.H7Calendar", "MMH7Game.H7GameProcessor", "MMH7Game.H7CombatResult" }
local extra = {
  "MMH7Game.H7Town.OnBeginDay", "MMH7Game.H7EventManager.OnOnBeginOfDay", "MMH7Game.H7EventManager.OnOnEndOfDay",
  "MMH7Game.H7EventManager.OnOnDoDamage", "MMH7Game.H7CreatureStack.ApplyDamage", "MMH7Game.H7Unit.ApplyDamage",
  "MMH7Game.H7Player.ApplyDamage", "MMH7Game.H7HallOfHeroesManager.UpdateEndDay",
  "MMH7Game.H7AdventureController.SetNextPlayer", -- calls mCalendar.NextDay() when the last player ends a turn
}

-- h7.first may pick an archetype from a Data package instead of the live controller.
for _, c in ipairs(h7.find_all("H7AdventureController")) do T("controller instance: %s", tostring(c)) end
for _, c in ipairs(h7.find_all("H7Calendar")) do T("calendar instance: %s", tostring(c)) end
T("h7.first controller: %s", tostring(h7.first("H7AdventureController")))
for _, c in ipairs(h7.find_all("H7AdventureController", true)) do T("with templates: %s", tostring(c)) end

local counts, reported, ids = {}, {}, {}
local function trace(path)
  local ok, id = pcall(h7.hook, path, function() counts[path] = (counts[path] or 0) + 1 end)
  if ok then ids[#ids + 1] = id else T("cannot hook %s: %s", path, tostring(id)) end
end

for _, cls in ipairs(classes) do
  local owner = cls:match("[^.]+$")
  for _, f in ipairs(h7.functions(cls)) do
    local fl = f.flags
    if f.owner == owner and fl & DEFINED ~= 0 and fl & (NATIVE | ITERATOR | LATENT | DELEGATE) == 0 then
      trace(cls .. "." .. f.name)
    end
  end
end
for _, p in ipairs(extra) do trace(p) end
T("tracing %d functions", #ids)

-- Details for the two silent mods: does the hook run, and what does it see?
local n1, n2 = 0, 0
ids[#ids + 1] = h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7Calendar.NextDay", function(calendar)
  n1 = n1 + 1
  if n1 > 3 then return end
  T("NextDay post on %s", tostring(calendar))
  local adv = h7.first("H7AdventureController")
  local ok, pl = pcall(function() return adv and adv:GetLocalPlayer(true) end)
  T("NextDay post: controller=%s localPlayer=%s (%s)", tostring(adv), tostring(pl), tostring(ok))
end)
ids[#ids + 1] = h7.hook_post("MMH7Game.H7GameProcessor.CalculateDamage", function(_, args)
  n2 = n2 + 1
  if n2 > 5 then return end
  T("CalculateDamage post: Result=%s idx=%s forecast=%s", tostring(args.Result), tostring(args.resultIdx),
    tostring(args.forecast))
end)

local function report()
  local lines = {}
  for path, c in pairs(counts) do
    if c ~= (reported[path] or 0) then
      lines[#lines + 1] = ("%s +%d"):format(path:gsub("^MMH7Game%.", ""), c - (reported[path] or 0))
      reported[path] = c
    end
  end
  table.sort(lines)
  for _, s in ipairs(lines) do T("%s", s) end
  h7.after(3, report)
end
h7.after(3, report)
return "tracing " .. #ids

```

`tests/hooktest.lua`:

```lua
-- Hook behaviour test: copy into <Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\ at the main menu; results go to logs\engine.log.
local function T(name, v) h7.log(("[hooktest] %-18s %s"):format(name, tostring(v))) end
local pc = h7.first("PlayerController")
-- most-derived GetHumanReadableName along pc's class chain
local fnPath
local cls = pc:get_class()
while cls and not fnPath do
  local p = cls:get_path_name() .. ".GetHumanReadableName"
  if h7.find("Function " .. p) then fnPath = p end
  cls = cls:get_super()
end
T("target", fnPath)
T("original", pc:GetHumanReadableName())

-- 1) pre-hook that blocks
local id = h7.hook(fnPath, function(self, args) return true end)
T("blocked", "'" .. tostring(pc:GetHumanReadableName()) .. "'")
h7.unhook(id)
-- 2) post-hook overriding the return value
id = h7.hook_post(fnPath, function(self, args, ret) return "Modded " .. tostring(ret) end)
T("overridden", pc:GetHumanReadableName())
h7.unhook(id)
T("restored", pc:GetHumanReadableName())

-- 3) reading params from a hooked event (FFrame locals)
local seen
local hid = h7.hook("Engine.GameViewportClient.PostRender", function(self, args)
  if not seen then seen = tostring(args and args.Canvas) end
end)
h7.after(0.5, function() T("PostRender.Canvas", seen); h7.unhook(hid) end)

-- 4) script-to-script calls: PostRender's bytecode calls DrawTransition(Canvas) every frame
local n, canvas = 0, nil
local did = h7.hook("Engine.GameViewportClient.DrawTransition", function(self, args)
  n = n + 1
  canvas = canvas or tostring(args.Canvas)
end)
h7.after(1, function() T("script->script", n .. " calls, Canvas=" .. tostring(canvas)); h7.unhook(did) end)
T("signature", h7.signature(fnPath))
return "ok3"

```

`tests/keys.ps1`:

```ps1
# Types keys into the game window: powershell -File keys.ps1 <key> [<key> ...]
# Keys: 0-9, BACK, RETURN, ESCAPE, PERIOD, MINUS (virtual-key names). Brings the game to the front first.
param([Parameter(ValueFromRemainingArguments = $true)][string[]]$Keys)
Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class Kbd {
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint MapVirtualKey(uint code, uint type);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void keybd_event(byte vk, byte scan, uint flags, UIntPtr extra);
}
"@
$vk = @{ BACK = 0x08; RETURN = 0x0D; ESCAPE = 0x1B; PERIOD = 0xBE; MINUS = 0xBD }
$game = Get-Process MMH7Game-Win64-Shipping -ErrorAction Stop | Where-Object { $_.MainWindowHandle -ne 0 } | Select-Object -First 1
[Kbd]::SetForegroundWindow($game.MainWindowHandle) | Out-Null
Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200
foreach ($k in $Keys) {
    $code = if ($vk.ContainsKey($k)) { $vk[$k] } elseif ($k.Length -eq 1) { [int][char]$k.ToUpper() } else { throw "unknown key $k" }
    $scan = [byte][Kbd]::MapVirtualKey([uint32]$code, 0)
    [Kbd]::keybd_event([byte]$code, $scan, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 60
    [Kbd]::keybd_event([byte]$code, $scan, 2, [UIntPtr]::Zero)
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 80
}
```

`tests/menulab.lua`:

```lua
-- Main menu UI experiment: adds a "Mods" button between Options and Quit and opens a popup when it is clicked.
-- Copy into <Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\ at the main menu; safe to re-run (it reuses what it created).
local function T(fmt, ...) h7.log("[menulab] " .. fmt:format(...)) end
local Clik = h7.find_class("GFxUI.GFxClikWidget")
local Obj = h7.find_class("GFxUI.GFxObject") -- omitted optional args are 0/none, so pass them explicitly

local cntl = h7.first("H7MainMenuCntl")
local mm = cntl.mMainMenu

local function geo(o)
  if not o then return "nil" end
  return ("x=%.0f y=%.0f w=%.0f h=%.0f sx=%.2f visible=%s"):format(o:GetFloat("x"), o:GetFloat("y"),
    o:GetFloat("width"), o:GetFloat("height"), o:GetFloat("scaleX"), tostring(o:GetBool("visible")))
end

-- Clicks on our buttons arrive at H7MainMenuCntl.Options with empty EventData, so find the button under the mouse.
local function under_mouse(o)
  if not o:GetBool("visible") then return false, "hidden" end
  local mx, my = o:GetFloat("mouseX"), o:GetFloat("mouseY")
  local w, h = o:GetFloat("width") / o:GetFloat("scaleX"), o:GetFloat("height") / o:GetFloat("scaleY")
  return mx >= 0 and my >= 0 and mx < w and my < h, ("mouse %.0f,%.0f in %.0fx%.0f"):format(mx, my, w, h)
end

-- 1) the button: Extras and Options move up one slot into the empty space, Mods takes Options' place, Quit stays
local btn = _G.menulab_btn
if not btn then
  local extras, options = mm:GetObject("mBtnExtras", Obj), mm:GetObject("mBtnOptions", Obj)
  local step = options:GetFloat("y") - extras:GetFloat("y")
  btn = mm:AttachMovie("H7TextButton", "mBtnMods", -1, Clik)
  _G.menulab_btn = btn
  btn:SetString("text", "Mods")
  btn:SetFloat("width", options:GetFloat("width"))
  btn:SetFloat("height", options:GetFloat("height"))
  btn:SetFloat("x", options:GetFloat("x"))
  btn:SetFloat("y", options:GetFloat("y"))
  extras:SetFloat("y", extras:GetFloat("y") - step)
  options:SetFloat("y", options:GetFloat("y") - step)
  btn:AddEventListener("CLIK_click", { object = cntl, func = "Options" })
end
for _, n in ipairs({ "mBtnTutorial", "mBtnExtras", "mBtnOptions", "mBtnMods", "mBtnQuit" }) do
  T("%-12s %s", n, geo(mm:GetObject(n, Obj)))
end

-- 2) the popup
local function open_popup()
  local root = cntl.mRootMC
  local popup = _G.menulab_popup
  if not popup then
    popup = root:AttachMovie("H7CustomDifficultyPopUp", "mModsPopup", -1, Obj)
    _G.menulab_popup = popup
    T("popup created: %s", tostring(popup))
  end
  popup:SetBool("visible", true)
  T("popup %s numChildren=%s", geo(popup), tostring(popup:GetInt("numChildren")))
  -- Timeline children of a freshly attached clip may only exist after the next frame.
  h7.after(0.5, function()
    T("popup after 0.5s %s numChildren=%s", geo(popup), tostring(popup:GetInt("numChildren")))
    for _, n in ipairs({ "mBG", "mTitle", "mCloseButton", "mStartResources", "mNeutralStrength", "mNeutralGrowth",
      "mAICheating", "mBtnOK", "mLblGeneralDifficulty", "mDDGeneralDifficulty" }) do
      T("  %-22s %s", n, geo(popup:GetObject(n, Obj)))
    end
    local title = popup:GetObject("mTitle", Obj)
    if title then title:SetString("text", "Mods") end
  end)
end

if _G.menulab_hook then h7.unhook(_G.menulab_hook) end
_G.menulab_hook = h7.hook("MMH7Game.H7MainMenuCntl.Options", function(self, args)
  local ours, why = under_mouse(btn)
  local _, why_opt = under_mouse(mm:GetObject("mBtnOptions", Obj))
  T("Options called: mods button %s (%s); options button %s", tostring(ours), why, why_opt)
  if not ours then return end
  open_popup()
  return true
end)
return "ok"

```

```lua
-- Mods list experiment: opens the map selection window (H7MapSelect movie) and fills its list with mods.

local function T(fmt, ...) h7.log("[modlab] " .. fmt:format(...)) end

local sel = h7.first("H7MapSelectCntl")

T("cntl %s list %s", tostring(sel), tostring(list))

T("added %d", n)

local function geo(o)
  if not o then return "nil" end
  return ("x=%.0f y=%.0f w=%.0f h=%.0f vis=%s"):format(o:GetFloat("x"), o:GetFloat("y"), o:GetFloat("width"),
    o:GetFloat("height"), tostring(o:GetBool("visible")))
end
for _, k in ipairs({ "mLblTitle", "mLblMapName", "mLblVictoryCondition", "mLblPlayers", "mLblMapSize", "mLblMapType",
  "mDropDifficulty", "mCustomDifficulty", "mMapThumbnail", "mLblNoThumbnail", "mMapList", "mMapDetails",
  "mBtnContinue", "mBtnBack", "mGenerateMap", "mLoadingAnimationMaps" }) do

  T("%-22s %s %s", k, geo(o), o and tostring(o:GetString("text")) or "")
end
local ml = list:GetObject("mMapList", Obj)
for _, k in ipairs({ "mDDCondition", "mDDPlayerAmount", "mDDMapSize", "mDDMapType", "mBtnClearFilters",
  "mBtnSortMapNameUp", "mBtnSortMapNameDown", "mLblNoMaps", "mScrollBar" }) do
  T("  mMapList.%-18s %s", k, geo(ml and ml:GetObject(k, Obj)))
end
return "ok"

```

`tests/modwindow_autotest.lua`:

```lua
-- Drives ModManager's Mods window without input: opens it, turns DailyGold on and off, goes back, then opens the
-- game's own map selection to check it was restored. Run at the main menu with tests/run_ingame.sh, which takes the
-- screenshots that "SHOT <name>" lines ask for.
local function T(fmt, ...) h7.log("[autotest] " .. fmt:format(...)) end
local Obj = h7.find_class("GFxUI.GFxObject")
local ui = _G.__modmanager
local sel = h7.first("H7MapSelectCntl")
local menu = h7.first("H7MainMenuCntl")
local list = sel.mMapSelection

local function child(parent, key) return parent and parent:GetObject(key, Obj) end
local function text(key) local o = child(list, key) return o and o:GetString("text") end
local function status_of(name)
    for _, m in ipairs(h7.mods()) do
        if m.name == name then return ("enabled=%s loaded=%s"):format(tostring(m.enabled), tostring(m.loaded)) end
    end
end
local function visible(path)
    local o = list
    for key in path:gmatch("[^.]+") do o = child(o, key) end
    return o and tostring(o:GetBool("visible")) or "nil"
end
-- DailyGold's row as the window holds it (the rows themselves can't be read).
local function dailygold_row()
    local rows = child(list, "mPolledMaps")
    for i = 0, (rows and rows:GetInt("length") or 0) - 1 do
        local r = rows:GetElementObject(i, Obj)
        if r:GetString("Name") == "DailyGold" then return r:GetString("Type") end
    end
    return "not listed"
end

-- The parts ModManager changes, as one line.
local function window_state()
    return ("title=%s col2=%s col2filter=%s continue=%s noThumb=%s thumb=%s generate=%s"):format(text("mLblTitle"),
        text("mLblVictoryCondition"), visible("mMapList.mDDCondition"), text("mBtnContinue"), visible("mLblNoThumbnail"),
        visible("mMapThumbnail"), visible("mGenerateMap"))
end

-- Continue on DailyGold's row. Only while the Mods window shows: for maps it would start a game.
local function toggle_dailygold()
    assert(ui.showing, "Mods window not showing")
    for i, name in pairs(ui.rows) do
        if name == "DailyGold" then return sel:BtnContinueClicked(i, false) end
    end
    error("DailyGold not listed")
end

-- Steps run one after another with a pause, so the window can draw and the watcher can take its screenshot.
local steps = {
    function()
        if ui.showing then sel:BtnBackClicked() end -- close a window left open
        T("open Mods window")
        ui.open()
    end,
    function()
        T("mods: %s", window_state())
        T("SHOT mods_open")
    end,
    function()
        T("DailyGold before: %s", status_of("DailyGold"))
        toggle_dailygold()
    end,
    function()
        T("DailyGold after enable: %s, row says %s; %s", status_of("DailyGold"), dailygold_row(), window_state())
        T("SHOT mods_enabled")
    end,
    function()
        toggle_dailygold()
    end,
    function()
        T("DailyGold after disable: %s, row says %s", status_of("DailyGold"), dailygold_row())
        sel:BtnBackClicked()
    end,
    function()
        T("back: menu visible=%s, window showing=%s", tostring(menu.mMainMenu:GetBool("visible")), tostring(ui.showing))
        menu:StartSPCustom(false) -- the game's own custom map selection
    end,
    function()
        T("maps: %s", window_state())
        T("SHOT maps_open")
    end,
    function()
        sel:BtnBackClicked()
        T("DONE")
    end,
}

local function run(i)
    if not steps[i] then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(steps[i])
    if not ok then T("step %d failed: %s", i, tostring(err)) end
    h7.after(2, function() run(i + 1) end)
end
run(1)
return "autotest started"

```

`tests/run_ingame.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Runs an in-game test script and takes the screenshots it asks for.
#   tests/run_ingame.sh <test.lua> <screenshot dir> [timeout seconds]
# The script is copied into <Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\; its log lines are printed. A line ending in
# "SHOT <name>" saves <screenshot dir>\<name>.png (tests\shot.ps1); a line ending in "DONE" ends the run.
set -u
# Game folder: set H7_GAME_DIR (Windows or Git Bash path) to use another one.
GAME="$(cygpath -u "${H7_GAME_DIR:-N:/Ubisoft/games/Might and Magic Heroes VII}")/H7ModEngine"
LOG="$GAME/logs/engine.log"
TEST="$1"; SHOTS="$2"; TIMEOUT="${3:-90}"
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
mkdir -p "$SHOTS"
from=$(($(wc -l < "$LOG") + 1))
cp "$TEST" "$GAME/exec/"
end=$((SECONDS + TIMEOUT))
while [ $SECONDS -lt $end ]; do
    while IFS= read -r line; do
        from=$((from + 1))
        line=${line%$'\r'}
        echo "$line"
        case "$line" in
            *" SHOT "*)
                name=${line##* SHOT }
                powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "$(cygpath -w "$HERE/shot.ps1")" \
                    "$(cygpath -w "$SHOTS/$name.png")" && echo "  -> $SHOTS/$name.png";;
            *" DONE") exit 0;;
            *"exec: ERROR"*) exit 1;;
        esac
    done < <(tail -n +"$from" "$LOG")
    sleep 0.5
done
echo "timed out after ${TIMEOUT}s"
exit 1

```

`tests/selftest.lua`:

```lua
-- Engine self-test: copy into <Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\ while the game runs; results go to logs\engine.log.
local function check(name, fn)
    local ok, v = pcall(fn)
    h7.log(("[selftest] %-22s %s  %s"):format(name, ok and "OK  " or "FAIL", tostring(v)))
    return ok and v
end

local Actor = check("find_class", function() return h7.find_class("Engine.Actor") end)
check("class default", function() return assert(Actor:get_default(), "no CDO") end)
check("super chain", function() return Actor:get_super() end)
check("enum", function() return h7.enum("EPhysics").PHYS_Walking end)
check("properties", function() return #h7.properties("Engine.Actor") .. " props" end)
check("functions", function() return #h7.functions("Engine.Actor") .. " functions" end)
local vp = check("viewport", function() return h7.first("GameViewportClient") end)
local pc = check("player controller", function() return h7.first("PlayerController") end)
check("read float", function() return pc.Location.X end)
check("read bool", function() return pc.bHidden end)
check("read struct table", function() local t = h7.to_table(pc.Location); return t.X .. "," .. t.Y .. "," .. t.Z end)
check("read object", function() return pc.WorldInfo end)
check("read string", function() return pc.PlayerReplicationInfo and pc.PlayerReplicationInfo.PlayerName end)
check("read array", function() return #pc.Attached end)
check("is_a", function() return pc:is_a("Actor") and not pc:is_a("Pawn") end)
check("write bool", function() local old = pc.bHidden; pc.bHidden = old; return pc.bHidden == old end)
check("find_all", function() return #h7.find_all("Engine.Actor") .. " actors" end)

-- Writing arrays from Lua tables (main menu only: uses the main menu's movie controller).
local cntl = h7.first("H7MainMenuCntl")
if cntl then
    check("write array", function()
        local old = h7.to_table(cntl.mLocaSection)
        cntl.mLocaSection = { "A", "B" }
        local got = #cntl.mLocaSection .. ":" .. cntl.mLocaSection[2]
        cntl.mLocaSection = old
        assert(got == "2:B", got)
        assert(#cntl.mLocaSection == #old, "not restored")
        return got
    end)
    check("array argument", function()
        local AS = h7.enum("ASType")
        local arr = cntl:CreateArray()
        arr:Invoke("push", { { Type = AS.AS_String, s = "first" }, { Type = AS.AS_Number, n = 2 } })
        local got = arr:GetElementString(0) .. "," .. arr:GetElementFloat(1)
        assert(got == "first,2.0", got)
        return got
    end)
end
check("mods", function() return #h7.mods() .. " mods" end)

-- Multiplexed hook on the function that drives the engine heartbeat.
local ticks = 0
local id = check("hook_post", function()
    return h7.hook_post("Engine.GameViewportClient.PostRender", function(self, args) ticks = ticks + 1 end)
end)
h7.after(2, function()
    h7.log(("[selftest] hook fired %d times in 2s"):format(ticks))
    h7.unhook(id)
end)

-- Calling a UnrealScript function through ProcessEvent.
check("call function", function() return pc:GetHumanReadableName() end)
return "selftest dispatched"

```

`tests/settingstest.lua`:

```lua
-- Mod settings self-test: copy into <Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\ while the game runs; results go to logs\engine.log.
-- Uses the DamageScale and HeroMovement example settings and puts every value back afterwards.
local function check(name, fn)
    local ok, v = pcall(fn)
    h7.log(("[settingstest] %-24s %s  %s"):format(name, ok and "OK  " or "FAIL", tostring(v)))
    return ok and v
end
local function find(mod, key)
    for _, s in ipairs(h7.mod_settings(mod)) do if s.key == key then return s end end
end

local old = find("DamageScale", "multiplier").value
local oldLog = find("DamageScale", "log").value
local oldWho = find("HeroMovement", "who").value

check("listed in h7.mods", function()
    for _, m in ipairs(h7.mods()) do if m.name == "DamageScale" then assert(m.settings == 2, m.settings) return m.settings end end
    error("DamageScale not found")
end)
check("descriptor", function()
    local s = find("DamageScale", "multiplier")
    assert(s.type == "number" and s.min == 0.25 and s.max == 4 and s.step == 0.25 and s.default == 1.25, "fields")
    return ("%s %s [%s..%s] step %s"):format(s.label, tostring(s.value), s.min, s.max, s.step)
end)
check("set number", function() return assert(h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "multiplier", 2) == 2) end)
check("snap to step", function() local v = h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "multiplier", 1.3); assert(v == 1.25, v) return v end)
check("clamp to max", function() local v = h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "multiplier", 99); assert(v == 4, v) return v end)
check("number from text", function() local v = h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "multiplier", "1.5"); assert(v == 1.5, v) return v end)
check("bad number rejected", function()
    local ok = pcall(h7.set_mod_setting, "DamageScale", "multiplier", "abc")
    assert(not ok, "accepted") return "error raised"
end)
check("bool", function() local v = h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "log", false); assert(v == false) return v end)
check("choice", function() local v = h7.set_mod_setting("HeroMovement", "who", "everyone"); assert(v == "Everyone", v) return v end)
check("bad choice rejected", function()
    assert(not pcall(h7.set_mod_setting, "HeroMovement", "who", "Nobody"), "accepted") return "error raised"
end)
check("unknown key rejected", function()
    assert(not pcall(h7.set_mod_setting, "HeroMovement", "nope", 1), "accepted") return "error raised"
end)
local seen = {}
local id = h7.on("setting_changed", function(mod, key, value) seen[#seen + 1] = mod .. "." .. key .. "=" .. tostring(value) end)
check("setting_changed event", function()
    h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "multiplier", 3)
    h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "multiplier", 3) -- unchanged: no event
    assert(#seen == 1, #seen) return seen[1]
end)
h7.off(id)

h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "multiplier", old)
h7.set_mod_setting("DamageScale", "log", oldLog)
h7.set_mod_setting("HeroMovement", "who", oldWho)
check("restored", function() return find("DamageScale", "multiplier").value == old end)
h7.log("[settingstest] DONE")

```

`tests/shot.ps1`:

```ps1
# Saves a screenshot of the game window (any display): powershell -File shot.ps1 <out.png>
# Used by the in-game autotests. PrintWindow renders the window itself, so it may be covered or on another display.
param([string]$Out)
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
Add-Type @"
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class Win {
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr hWnd, out RECT rect);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetProcessDPIAware();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool PrintWindow(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hdc, uint flags);
}
"@
[Win]::SetProcessDPIAware() | Out-Null
$game = Get-Process MMH7Game-Win64-Shipping -ErrorAction Stop | Where-Object { $_.MainWindowHandle -ne 0 } | Select-Object -First 1
$r = New-Object Win+RECT
[Win]::GetWindowRect($game.MainWindowHandle, [ref]$r) | Out-Null
$w = $r.Right - $r.Left; $h = $r.Bottom - $r.Top
$bmp = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap $w, $h
$g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bmp)
$hdc = $g.GetHdc()
[Win]::PrintWindow($game.MainWindowHandle, $hdc, 2) | Out-Null # PW_RENDERFULLCONTENT
$g.ReleaseHdc($hdc)
$bmp.Save($Out, [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
$g.Dispose()
$bmp.Dispose()

```

`tests/swfdump.lua`:

```lua
-- Copies every loaded Flash movie (SwfMovie.RawData) to <Game>\H7ModEngine\dumps\swf\ and logs the open movie
-- players. Copy into <Game>\H7ModEngine\exec\ at the main menu.
local function T(fmt, ...) h7.log("[swfdump] " .. fmt:format(...)) end

local exec_dir = debug.getinfo(1, "S").source:match("^@(.*)[/\\][^/\\]+$")
local out_dir = exec_dir .. "\\..\\dumps\\swf\\"
os.execute('mkdir "' .. out_dir .. '" 2>nul')

local MAX = 16 * 1024 * 1024
for _, movie in ipairs(h7.find_all("SwfMovie")) do
  local data = movie.RawData
  local n = #data
  local file = out_dir .. movie:get_path_name():gsub("[^%w%._-]", "_") .. ".swf"
  if n > 0 and n <= MAX then
    local f = assert(io.open(file, "wb"))
    local chunk = {}
    for i = 1, n do
      chunk[#chunk + 1] = data[i]
      if #chunk == 4096 or i == n then
        f:write(string.char(table.unpack(chunk)))
        chunk = {}
      end
    end
    f:close()
  end
  T("%-60s %9d bytes%s", movie:get_path_name(), n, n > MAX and " (skipped)" or "")
end

for _, p in ipairs(h7.find_all("GFxMoviePlayer")) do
  T("player %s movie=%s", tostring(p), tostring(p.MovieInfo))
end

local cntl = h7.first("H7MainMenuCntl")
T("main menu controller: %s", tostring(cntl))
if cntl then
  T("mMainMenu=%s mBtnOptions=%s mBtnQuit=%s", tostring(cntl.mMainMenu), tostring(cntl.mBtnOptions),
    tostring(cntl.mBtnQuit))
end
return "done"

```

`tools/docs/hooks.py`:

```py
"""MkDocs hooks for the H7ModEngine documentation site (see mkdocs.yml).

- Stamps the release the site was built from (H7_DOCS_REF, e.g. "v0.2.0") in the site name and footer.
- Rewrites relative links that leave docs/ (e.g. ``../src/lua/bindings.cpp``) into GitHub links at that ref,
  so the guides can point at source files and the links work both on GitHub and on the site.
"""

import os
import posixpath
import re

REPO = "https://github.com/Whaileee/H7ModEngine"
REF = os.environ.get("H7_DOCS_REF", "").strip()

_LINK = re.compile(r"(\]\()([^)\s#]+)(#[^)\s]*)?(\))")


def on_config(config):
    label = REF or "development build"
    config["site_name"] = f"H7ModEngine {REF}" if REF else "H7ModEngine (dev)"
    config["copyright"] = (
        f"Documentation for H7ModEngine {label}. "
        "Unofficial fan project, not affiliated with or endorsed by Ubisoft."
    )
    return config


def on_page_markdown(markdown, page, config, files):
    src_dir = posixpath.dirname(page.file.src_uri)
    ref = REF or "main"

    def fix(m):
        target = m.group(2)
        if re.match(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:", target, re.I) or target.startswith("/"):
            return m.group(0)
        resolved = posixpath.normpath(posixpath.join(src_dir, target))
        if not resolved.startswith("../"):
            return m.group(0)  # inside docs/: MkDocs resolves it
        repo_path = resolved[3:]
        kind = "tree" if repo_path.endswith("/") or "." not in posixpath.basename(repo_path) else "blob"
        return f"{m.group(1)}{REPO}/{kind}/{ref}/{repo_path}{m.group(3) or ''}{m.group(4)}"

    return _LINK.sub(fix, markdown)

```

`tools/docs/requirements.txt`:
